DanceOPD: On-Policy Generative Field Distillation
Authors: Wei Zhou, Xiongwei Zhu, Zelin Xu, Bo Dong, Lixue Gong, Yongyuan Liang, Meng Chu, Leigang Qu, Lingdong Kong, Wei Liu, Tat-Seng Chua
First: 2026-06-25T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:59:58+00:00
Comments: Technical Report; 39 pages, 13 figures, 9 tables; Project Page at https://danceopd.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Modern image generation demands a single model that unifies diverse capabilities, including text-to-image (T2I), local editing, and global editing. However, these capabilities are rarely naturally aligned and often conflict. For instance, editing tends to degrade T2I performance, while global and local editing interfere with each other. Consequently, effectively composing these capabilities has become a central challenge for image generation model training. To tackle this, we introduce DanceOPD, an on-policy generative field distillation framework for flow-matching models that routes each sample to one capability field, queries one low-noise student-induced state, and trains with a simple velocity MSE objective. With each capability source defined as a velocity field over the shared flow state space, the student learns from fields queried on its own rollout states to compose expert capabilities. This formulation also absorbs operator-defined fields such as classifier-free guidance. Comprehensive experiments on T2I, editing, realism-field absorption, and CFG absorption show that our approach improves multi-capability composition, strengthening target capabilities while preserving anchor generation quality. We believe this work establishes a practical route for generative field distillation in flow-matching models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DanceOPD：基于策略的生成场蒸馏
现代图像生成需要一个统一多种能力的模型，包括文本到图像（T2I）、局部编辑和全局编辑。然而，这些能力很少自然对齐，往往相互冲突。例如，编辑会降低T2I性能，而全局和局部编辑彼此干扰。因此，有效组合这些能力已成为图像生成模型训练的核心挑战。为解决此问题，我们提出了DanceOPD，一种针对流匹配模型的基于策略的生成场蒸馏框架，该框架将每个样本路由至一个能力场，查询一个低噪声学生诱导状态，并以简单的速度MSE目标进行训练。每个能力源被定义为共享流状态空间上的速度场，学生从自身 rollout 状态查询的场中学习，以组合专家能力。该框架还吸收了操作员定义的场，如无分类器引导（CFG）。在T2I、编辑、真实感场吸收和CFG吸收上的全面实验表明，我们的方法提升了多能力组合效果，在增强目标能力的同时保持锚点生成质量。我们相信，本工作为流匹配模型中的生成场蒸馏提供了实用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of unifying diverse image generation capabilities—such as text-to-image, local, and global editing—without mutual degradation, the authors propose DanceOPD, an on-policy generative field distillation framework for flow-matching models. The method routes each sample to a specific capability field, queries a low-noise state induced by a student model, and trains using a velocity MSE objective. By defining each capability as a velocity field over a shared state space, the student learns to compose expert behaviors through self-generated rollouts. Experiments demonstrate improved multi-capability composition, with enhanced target tasks while preserving base generation quality, and successful absorption of operator-defined fields like classifier-free guidance.
为解决文本生成图像、局部与全局编辑等多能力在模型中难以协同且易相互干扰的问题，作者提出DanceOPD框架，一种面向流匹配模型的在线生成场蒸馏方法。该方法为每个样本分配特定能力场，通过学生模型自生成状态查询低噪声状态，并以速度均方误差为目标进行训练。各能力以共享状态空间上的速度场形式定义，学生模型通过自身 rollout 学习组合专家能力。实验表明，该方法显著提升多能力协同效果，保持基准生成质量，并有效吸收如分类器自由引导等操作定义场。
Ask, Solve, Generate: Self-Evolving Unified Multimodal Understanding and Generation via Self-Consistency Rewards
Authors: Ritesh Thawkar, Shravan Venkatraman, Omkar Thawakar, Abdelrahman Shaker, Fahad Khan, Hisham Cholakkal, Salman Khan, Rao Muhammad Anwer
First: 2026-06-25T17:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:59:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most unified large multimodal models (LMMs) that support both visual understanding and image generation still rely on curated post-training supervision, such as human annotations, preference labels, or external reward models. We ask whether a unified LMM can improve both abilities autonomously using only unlabeled images. We propose a self-evolving training framework with three internal roles: a Proposer that generates visual questions, a Solver that answers and evaluates them, and a Generator that synthesizes images. Training uses only self-derived consistency signals, without human annotations, preference labels, or task-trained external reward/judge models. To stabilize learning, we introduce Solver Token Entropy (STE), a continuous difficulty signal based on token-level prediction uncertainty that remains useful even when sample-level consistency becomes unreliable. For image generation, we design a multi-scale internal evaluation scheme that combines question-answer fidelity scoring with cycle-consistent captioning. This creates a solver-mediated coupling, where better visual understanding enables more reliable generation assessment and stronger internal training signals. The framework preserves the same role decomposition, reward logic, and training schedule across diffusion-based BLIP3o, rectified-flow BAGEL, and autoregressive VARGPT-v1.1 architectures, requiring only each backbone's native prompting and generation interface. Across eight understanding metrics, our method consistently improves over the corresponding base models. On BAGEL, it achieves a $+3.5\%$ absolute gain on MMMU and improves GenEval image generation performance from $82\%$ to $85\%$. Code and models are publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：提问、求解、生成：通过自一致性奖励实现自演化的统一多模态理解和生成
大多数支持视觉理解和图像生成的统一型大模型（LMMs）仍依赖人工标注、偏好标签或外部奖励模型等精心策划的后训练监督。我们探讨统一型LMM是否能仅通过无标签图像自主提升两项能力。我们提出一种自演化训练框架，包含三个内部角色：提出者生成视觉问题，求解者回答并评估问题，生成者合成图像。训练仅使用自生成的一致性信号，无需人工标注、偏好标签或任务训练的外部奖励/评判模型。为稳定学习，我们引入求解器标记熵（STE），一种基于标记级预测不确定性的连续难度信号，即使样本级一致性不可靠时仍具有效性。针对图像生成，我们设计多尺度内部评估方案，结合问答准确性评分与循环一致的图像描述。该方案建立求解器介导的耦合机制，使更强的视觉理解能力可支持更可靠的生成评估和更强大的内部训练信号。该框架在基于扩散的BLIP3o、修正流BAGEL及自回归VARGPT-v1.1架构中保持相同的角色分解、奖励逻辑和训练计划，仅需各主干网络原生的提示与生成接口。在八项理解指标上，我们的方法均优于对应基线模型。在BAGEL上，其在MMMU上绝对提升3.5%，图像生成性能从82%提升至85%。代码与模型已公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce reliance on human-curated supervision in unified multimodal models, this work proposes a self-evolving framework that autonomously improves both visual understanding and image generation using only unlabeled images. The method employs three internal roles — Proposer, Solver, and Generator — that iteratively generate questions, answer them, and synthesize images, respectively, with training guided solely by self-derived consistency signals. To stabilize learning, it introduces Solver Token Entropy (STE), a token-level uncertainty metric that serves as a continuous difficulty signal. For generation, it combines question-answer fidelity with cycle-consistent captioning to create a feedback loop where improved understanding enhances generation evaluation. Across multiple architectures, the framework consistently outperforms base models, achieving a +3.5% absolute gain on MMMU and boosting GenEval image generation from 82% to 85%.
为减少统一多模态模型对人工标注数据的依赖，该研究提出一种自演进框架，仅使用无标签图像即可自主提升视觉理解与图像生成能力。方法包含三个内部角色——提问者、解题者与生成器，分别负责生成问题、回答评估与图像合成，训练基于自一致性信号。为稳定学习，引入基于词元预测不确定性的解题者熵（STE）作为难度信号，并设计多尺度评估机制，结合问答准确度与循环一致的图像描述，强化内部反馈。该框架在扩散、流式与自回归架构上保持一致结构与训练策略，仅需各模型原生接口，实测在八项理解指标上均优于基线模型，在BAGEL上MMMU提升3.5%，图像生成能力从82%提升至85%。
Scalable Behavior Cloning with Open Data, Training, and Evaluation
Authors: Arthur Allshire, Himanshu Gaurav Singh, Ritvik Singh, Adam Rashid, Hongsuk Choi, David McAllister, Justin Yu, Yiyuan Chen, Huang Huang, Pieter Abbeel, Xi Chen, Rocky Duan, Phillip Isola, Jitendra Malik, Fred Shentu, Guanya Shi, Philipp Wu, Angjoo Kanazawa
First: 2026-06-25T17:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:59:57+00:00
Comments: 30 pages. Project page: https://abc.bot
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce ABC, a fully open-source stack for manipulation with behavior cloning. At its core is ABC-130K: the largest open-source teleoperation dataset to date, featuring 3,500 hours of data spanning over 130K episodes across 195 diverse tasks. Furthermore, we open-source our accessible hardware setup, training infrastructure, and simulation pipeline. We also release 400 hours of sim-teleop data and provide a co-training recipe that produces correlated simulation and real-world evaluation, offering a reliable proxy for ablating model-design and training decisions before costly real-world evaluation. We explore various training recipes and compare common architectural choices for Diffusion Transformers (DiT) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, grounding our findings in real-world evaluations. The resulting policies successfully execute dexterous tasks such as box folding and extracting credit cards from wallets. By providing a reproducible toolkit, we aim to place researchers on an equal footing, establishing the necessary foundation to learn the ABCs of Behavior Cloning together as a community.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于开放数据、训练与评估的可扩展行为克隆
我们引入ABC，一套完全开源的行为克隆操作工具栈。其核心是ABC-130K：迄今为止最大的开源遥操作数据集，包含跨越13万次实验、195个多样化任务的3500小时数据。此外，我们开源了易用的硬件配置、训练基础设施和仿真流程，并发布400小时的仿真-遥操作数据，提供一种协同训练方案，可生成仿真与真实世界评估相关联的结果，为在昂贵的真实世界评估前验证模型设计和训练决策提供可靠代理。我们探索多种训练方案，比较扩散Transformer（DiT）和视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的常见架构选择，并以真实世界评估为基础验证发现。所生成的策略成功执行了如纸盒折叠和从钱包中取出信用卡等精细操作。通过提供可复现的工具包，我们旨在使研究人员处于平等地位，共同构建行为克隆的基础，学习ABC（行为克隆）的原理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, reproducible behavior cloning research, this work introduces ABC, an open-source stack featuring ABC-130K — the largest publicly available teleoperation dataset with 3,500 hours across 130K episodes and 195 tasks. The authors open-source hardware, training infrastructure, and simulation pipelines, including 400 hours of sim-teleop data and a co-training recipe that aligns simulation and real-world evaluation. They evaluate various training methods and architectural choices for DiT and VLA models, finding that their resulting policies successfully perform dexterous tasks like box folding and credit card extraction from wallets, enabling community-wide reproducibility and fair comparison.
为推动行为克隆研究的可扩展性和可复现性，本研究提出ABC开源工具栈，包含目前最大的公开遥操作数据集ABC-130K，涵盖3500小时、13万次实验、195个多样化任务。作者开源了硬件、训练框架与仿真流程，并提供400小时仿真遥操作数据及协同训练方案，使仿真与真实世界表现可对齐，从而在昂贵真实测试前评估模型设计。通过对比Diffusion Transformer与视觉-语言-动作模型的多种架构与训练策略，实测其在纸盒折叠、信用卡抽取等精细操作中的有效性，旨在为社区提供统一基础，共同推进行为克隆研究。
World Action Models Enable Continual Imitation Learning with Recurrent Generative Replays
Authors: Manish Kumar Govind, Dominick Reilly, Smit Patel, Hieu Le, Srijan Das
First: 2026-06-25T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:59:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Going beyond predicting robot actions, World Action Models (WAMs) can also generate future visual observations. We build on this generative capability to propose Recurrent Generative Replay (REGEN), a continual imitation learning framework that synthesizes pseudo-replay trajectories, enabling a robot policy to rehearse previously learned tasks without storing their original human demonstrations. During continual adaptation, REGEN recursively queries the WAM to synthesize pseudo-replay trajectories conditioned only on prior task instructions and current-task observations. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation settings show that REGEN reduces catastrophic forgetting by up to $50\%$ relative to sequential fine-tuning, while approaching the performance of privileged experience replay methods that require access to real replay data. Finally, we analyze the factors limiting generated replay, identifying long-horizon visual degradation and action-observation inconsistency as the primary bottlenecks. Our results establish WAMs as a promising foundation for continual robot learning without stored demonstrations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：世界动作模型通过循环生成重放实现持续模仿学习
世界动作模型（WAMs）不仅能预测机器人动作，还能生成未来视觉观测。我们基于其生成能力提出循环生成重放（REGEN），一种持续模仿学习框架，可合成伪重放轨迹，使机器人策略无需存储原始人类演示即可重演先前学习的任务。在持续适应过程中，REGEN 递归查询 WAM，仅根据先前任务指令和当前任务观测合成伪重放轨迹。仿真与真实操作实验表明，REGEN 相较于顺序微调可减少 50% 的灾难性遗忘，同时接近需要访问真实重放数据的特权经验重放方法的性能。最后，我们分析了生成重放的限制因素，识别出长时程视觉退化与动作-观测不一致为主要瓶颈。我们的结果确立了 WAMs 作为无需存储演示的持续机器人学习的有前景基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enable robots to learn continuously without storing human demonstrations, this work introduces Recurrent Generative Replay (REGEN), a framework that leverages World Action Models (WAMs) to synthesize pseudo-replay trajectories. REGEN recursively queries the WAM to generate visual futures conditioned on prior task instructions and current observations, allowing policies to rehearse past tasks without real replay data. Experimental results in simulation and real-world manipulation show REGEN reduces catastrophic forgetting by up to 50% compared to sequential fine-tuning and achieves performance close to privileged replay methods. The study also identifies long-horizon visual degradation and action-observation inconsistency as key limitations in generated replay.
为解决机器人在持续学习新任务时遗忘旧技能且无需存储人类演示的问题，本文提出递归生成回放（REGEN）框架，利用世界动作模型（WAMs）合成伪回放轨迹。REGEN在持续适应过程中，递归查询WAM，仅依据先前任务指令和当前观测生成视觉与动作序列，使策略得以复现过往任务。实验表明，REGEN相较顺序微调可减少高达50%的灾难性遗忘，性能接近需访问真实回放数据的特权经验回放方法。研究同时指出，长时程视觉退化与动作-观测不一致是生成回放的主要瓶颈。
Paying More Attention to Visual Tokens in Self-Evolving Large Multimodal Models
Authors: Shravan Venkatraman, Ritesh Thawkar, Omkar Thawakar, Rao Muhammad Anwer, Hisham Cholakkal, Salman Khan, Fahad Khan
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-25T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recently, self-evolving large multimodal models (LMMs) have received attention for improving visual reasoning in a purely unsupervised setting. However, multi-role self-play and self-consistency reward schemes in existing self-evolving LMMs optimize answer agreement without ensuring the decoder attends to visual content, relying instead on statistical language priors to produce self consistent outputs. This leads to a persistent failure mode we term visual under-conditioning, where the decoder relies on language priors rather than the image during generation, manifesting as insufficient attention to visual tokens. As a result, current self-evolving LMMs struggle on vision--language understanding tasks such as image captioning and visual question answering. To address this, we propose VISE (Visual Invariance Self-Evolution), a purely unsupervised self-evolving framework that directly regularizes the model's visual conditioning policy through two complementary invariance-based rewards: a geometric invariance reward that enforces spatial consistency under known transformations, and a semantic invariance reward that penalizes evidence-agnostic generation by requiring the model to recognize the absence of evidence when predicted regions are perturbed. VISE operates within a single model without specialist roles, external reward models, or annotations, and is trained on raw unlabeled images. Experiments on 18 benchmarks demonstrate the efficacy of our approach. Using Qwen3-VL-2B as the base model, VISE achieves gains of $+16.85$ CIDEr on COCO and $+19.66$ CIDEr on TextCaps, reduces object hallucination by $5.0$ Chair-I points, and generalizes across four model families and scales. Our code and models are available at https://mbzuai-oryx.github.io/VISE
中文标题/摘要
标题：在自演进大尺度多模态模型中更关注视觉标记
近期，自演进大尺度多模态模型（LMMs）因其在纯无监督环境下提升视觉推理能力而受到关注。然而，现有自演进LMMs中的多角色自对弈和自一致性奖励机制优化答案一致性，但未确保解码器关注视觉内容，而是依赖统计语言先验生成自一致输出。这导致我们称之为视觉欠条件化的持续性失败模式，即解码器在生成时依赖语言先验而非图像，表现为对视觉标记关注不足。因此，当前自演进LMMs在图像字幕生成和视觉问答等视觉-语言理解任务上表现不佳。为解决此问题，我们提出VISE（视觉不变性自演进），一种纯无监督自演进框架，通过两种互补的不变性奖励直接正则化模型的视觉条件化策略：几何不变性奖励强制在已知变换下保持空间一致性，语义不变性奖励通过要求模型在预测区域扰动时识别证据缺失，惩罚无证据生成。VISE在单一模型内运行，无需专业角色、外部奖励模型或标注，仅在原始无标签图像上训练。在18个基准测试中验证了我们的方法有效性。以Qwen3-VL-2B为基础模型，VISE在COCO上CIDEr提升+16.85，在TextCaps上CIDEr提升+19.66，物体幻觉减少5.0 Chair-I点，并在四个模型家族和规模上实现泛化。代码与模型见https://mbzuai-oryx.github.io/VISE
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent issue of visual under-conditioning in self-evolving large multimodal models, where decoders rely on language priors rather than visual tokens, this work introduces VISE, a purely unsupervised framework that directly regularizes visual attention through geometric and semantic invariance rewards. By enforcing spatial consistency under transformations and penalizing evidence-agnostic generation, VISE ensures the model conditions on visual content without requiring specialist roles, external rewards, or annotations. Evaluated on 18 benchmarks using Qwen3-VL-2B, VISE achieves +16.85 CIDEr on COCO and +19.66 CIDEr on TextCaps, reduces object hallucination by 5.0 Chair-I points, and demonstrates strong generalization across model families and scales.
为解决自演进大模态模型中视觉条件不足的问题——即解码器过度依赖语言先验而非图像内容——本文提出VISE框架，通过几何不变性奖励（确保空间变换下的一致性）和语义不变性奖励（惩罚无证据生成）直接调控视觉注意力。在18个基准测试中，以Qwen3-VL-2B为基础模型，VISE在COCO上提升CIDEr 16.85，在TextCaps上提升19.66，减少物体幻觉5.0 Chair-I点，并在四类模型架构和不同规模上展现出良好泛化能力，全程无需外部奖励或标注。
DnA: Denoising Attention for Visual Tasks
Authors: Ron Campos, Subhajit Maity, Xin Li, Srijan Das, Aritra Dutta
First: 2026-06-25T17:59:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:59:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The softmax activation in multihead attention (MHA) is the de facto standard for attention-based models in visual perception tasks. However, standard softmax can produce noisy attention patterns that dilute relevant features and degrade its performance. In this paper, we propose Denoising Attention or DnA, in which, first, a positive query identifies which image features belong to the correct class, and a negative query identifies closely associated but irrelevant image features. DnA then projects these interactions into two distinct subspaces with larger principal angles, promoting subspace separation and improved discriminability. Using a ViT-B backbone, our proposed DnA achieves an absolute gain of 0.8% on ImageNet-1K compared to the baseline. We further show improvements across multiple visual understanding tasks, including video understanding with video transformers (1.8%) and video LLMs (0.5%). Our extensive empirical analyses justify the design choices involving two interacting subspaces and the denoising effect of DnA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DnA：面向视觉任务的去噪注意力
多头注意力（MHA）中的softmax激活函数是视觉感知任务中基于注意力模型的事实标准。然而，标准softmax会产生噪声注意力模式，稀释相关特征并降低性能。本文提出去噪注意力（DnA），其中正查询识别属于正确类别的图像特征，负查询识别密切相关但无关的图像特征。DnA将这些交互投影到两个主角度更大的子空间中，促进子空间分离和判别性提升。使用ViT-B骨干网络，我们的DnA在ImageNet-1K上相比基线绝对提升0.8%。我们在多个视觉理解任务中进一步展示了改进效果，包括视频理解中的视频变换器（1.8%）和视频大语言模型（0.5%）。我们的大量实证分析验证了涉及两个交互子空间和DnA去噪效应的设计选择。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the noise in attention patterns caused by standard softmax in multihead attention, which hampers visual model performance, this paper introduces Denoising Attention (DnA). DnA uses positive and negative queries to identify relevant and irrelevant image features, respectively, then projects these interactions into two distinct subspaces with larger principal angles to enhance discriminability. Evaluated on ImageNet-1K with a ViT-B backbone, DnA achieves a 0.8% absolute accuracy gain over the baseline and further improves performance on video understanding tasks by 1.8% and 0.5% for video transformers and video LLMs, respectively.
为了解决标准softmax在多头注意力中导致的注意力模式噪声问题，该论文提出DnA（去噪注意力）。DnA通过正负查询分别识别相关和无关图像特征，再将它们的交互投影到两个主角较大的子空间中以增强可区分性。在ImageNet-1K上使用ViT-B骨干网络，DnA相比基线模型准确率提升0.8%，并在视频理解任务（如视频Transformer提升1.8%，视频LLM提升0.5%）中也表现出一致的性能提升。
Don't Settle at the Mode! Mitigating Diversity Collapse in Pretrained Flow Models via Feature Self-Guidance
Authors: Pradhaan S Bhat, Rishubh Parihar, Abhijnya Bhat, R. Venkatesh Babu
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-25T17:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:59:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ECCV 2026. Project page: https://dont-settle-at-the-mode.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
State-of-the-art flow models generate stunning images from text or image prompts. However, they suffer from diversity collapse when generating multiple samples under the same conditioning. Existing methods address this issue via either latent guidance, which has limited effectiveness, or sample selection, which relies on external reward models that incur significant inference-time overhead. In this work, we introduce an efficient, training-free self-guidance mechanism to mitigate diversity collapse without requiring additional reward models. Specifically, we disperse the internal features of the flow model during batch generation with feature self-guidance. Further, to keep the features close to the manifold, we introduce a manifold regularization step that projects these dispersed features back onto the data manifold, ensuring diverse generation without sacrificing alignment with the input conditions. Our method integrates seamlessly as a plug-and-play module into pretrained flow models, adding only a marginal inference cost. Experiments demonstrate significant improvements in diversity while preserving fidelity across several conditional flow models, including multi-step and few-step text-to-image, depth-to-image, and reference image generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：不要止步于模式！通过特征自引导缓解预训练流模型中的多样性崩溃
最先进的流模型能从文本或图像提示生成惊艳图像，但在相同条件下生成多个样本时易出现多样性崩溃。现有方法或依赖效果有限的潜在空间引导，或依赖外部奖励模型进行样本选择，后者在推理时开销巨大。本文提出一种高效、无需训练的自引导机制，无需额外奖励模型即可缓解多样性崩溃。具体而言，我们在批量生成过程中通过特征自引导分散流模型的内部特征；为进一步保持特征贴近数据流形，我们引入流形正则化步骤，将分散的特征投影回数据流形，确保生成多样性的同时不牺牲对输入条件的对齐。该方法可无缝作为即插即用模块集成到预训练流模型中，仅增加微小推理开销。实验表明，该方法在多种条件流模型（包括多步与少步文本到图像、深度到图像、参考图像生成）中显著提升多样性，同时保持生成保真度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the diversity collapse issue in pretrained flow models when generating multiple samples under identical conditioning, this work proposes a training-free, plug-and-play feature self-guidance mechanism that disperses internal features during batch generation to encourage diversity. To maintain alignment with input conditions while preserving manifold structure, it incorporates a manifold regularization step that projects dispersed features back onto the data manifold. Experimental results across multiple conditional generation tasks—including text-to-image, depth-to-image, and reference image generation—show that this method significantly improves sample diversity without compromising fidelity or incurring substantial inference overhead.
为解决预训练流模型在相同条件下生成多个样本时出现的多样性坍塌问题，本文提出一种无需训练、可即插即用的特征自引导机制，通过在批量生成过程中分散内部特征以促进多样性。为保持与输入条件的对齐同时提升多样性，该方法引入流形正则化步骤，将分散的特征投影回数据流形。实验表明，该方法在多种条件生成任务（如文本到图像、深度到图像、参考图像生成）中显著提升输出多样性，同时保持高保真度且推理开销极小。
Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs
Authors: Yingyu Lin, Qiyue Gao, Nikki Lijing Kuang, Xunpeng Huang, Kun Zhou, Tongtong Liang, Zhewei Yao, Yi-An Ma, Yuxiong He
First: 2026-06-25T17:59:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:59:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需真实解的强化学习可提升大语言模型
用于训练大语言模型的可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）通常依赖真实解来分配奖励，限制其在无真实解任务中的适用性。我们提出了一种基于排序诱导的可验证框架（RiVER），该框架在无真实解的情况下，通过确定性执行反馈作为连续值监督，训练大语言模型解决基于分数的优化任务。当将群体相对强化学习应用于此类连续奖励时，我们识别出两个关键挑战：\emph{尺度主导}，即测试实例间未校准的分数幅度会扭曲策略更新；\emph{频率主导}，即重复采样的次优解可能压倒稀有但更强的候选解。RiVER通过校准奖励塑造解决这些问题，利用实例间比较，强调排名靠前的求解器，同时保留对其他有效解的有界反馈。我们在12个AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上训练，并在算法工程基准（ALE-Bench）、LiveCodeBench和USACO上评估。RiVER使Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414在ALE评分排名上分别提升8.9%和9.4%。更重要的是，尽管仅在无真实解的基于分数的任务上训练，RiVER在LiveCodeBench和USACO等精确解基准上也实现了绝对平均提升2.4%和3.5%。相比之下，使用原始执行分数训练的基线虽提升ALE评分，却无法迁移到精确解基准。这些结果表明，结合适当奖励校准的基于分数的优化任务，可作为无需真实解的有效训练环境，提升通用编程能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that reinforcement learning for LLMs typically requires ground-truth solutions, the paper proposes RiVER, a ranking-induced verifiable framework that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks using deterministic execution feedback as continuous supervision. To address scale and frequency dominance in reward signals, RiVER employs calibrated reward shaping based on instance-wise comparisons, prioritizing top-ranked solutions while preserving bounded feedback for others. Evaluated on 12 AtCoder tasks and three benchmarks, RiVER improves Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9% and 9.4% in ALE rating rank, and also boosts performance on exact-solution benchmarks (LiveCodeBench and USACO) by 2.4% and 3.5% respectively, even without ground-truth during training — a key advantage over baselines that fail to transfer to exact-solution tasks.
为了解决强化学习训练大语言模型通常依赖真实答案的问题，本文提出RiVER框架，通过确定性执行反馈作为连续监督信号，在无需真实答案的评分优化任务上训练模型。该框架通过实例间比较校准奖励，缓解评分尺度失衡和低频优质解被淹没的问题。在12个AtCoder任务及三个基准测试中，RiVER使Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414在ALE评分排名上分别提升8.9%和9.4%，更重要的是，即使仅在评分任务上训练，其在LiveCodeBench和USACO等精确解基准上也平均提升2.4%和3.5%，而对比基线（使用原始评分）虽提升ALE排名却无法迁移到精确解任务。
PhysiFormer: Learning to Simulate Mechanics in World Space
Authors: Yiming Chen, Yushi Lan, Andrea Vedaldi
First: 2026-06-25T17:59:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:59:00+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://yimingc9.github.io/physiformer
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present PhysiFormer, a diffusion transformer for physically-plausible 3D object motion. Unlike video world models that operate in view-dependent pixel space, PhysiFormer represents objects as 3D meshes expressed in world coordinates. Given the initial vertex positions and velocities, as well as object material type, rigid or elastic, the model samples future vertex trajectories. While related neural physics approaches build on ad-hoc latent spaces or explicitly enforce rigidity and causality, PhysiFormer shows that excellent results can be obtained without any such inductive biases, by casting vertex trajectory prediction as a single denoising diffusion process directly in world coordinates. The probabilistic formulation captures uncertainty in the learned dynamics, enabling diverse plausible futures from initial conditions, making this framework potentially useful for applications with unobserved uncertainty. The model features attention factorised over time, space, and objects for efficiency, enabling permutation-invariant multi-object reasoning without needing explicit object encoding. Trained on over 100k simulated trajectories, PhysiFormer generates rigid and elastic mechanics, and generalises to mixed-material settings, unseen real-world geometries, and larger object counts. It substantially outperforms autoregressive baselines in trajectory accuracy, rigidity preservation, and momentum-based physical consistency. Our results position coordinate-space diffusion as a promising step toward view-invariant, geometry-aware world modelling for robotics, graphics, and physical design. Visualisations, code, and models are available at https://yimingc9.github.io/physiformer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PhysiFormer：学习在世界坐标空间中模拟力学
我们提出了PhysiFormer，一种用于物理上合理的3D物体运动的扩散变换器。与在视角依赖的像素空间中操作的视频世界模型不同，PhysiFormer将物体表示为以世界坐标表达的3D网格。给定初始顶点位置和速度，以及物体材质类型（刚性或弹性），模型采样未来顶点轨迹。与相关神经物理方法依赖于自定义潜在空间或显式强制刚性和因果性不同，PhysiFormer表明，无需任何此类归纳偏置，仅将顶点轨迹预测视为直接在世界坐标中进行的单个去噪扩散过程，即可获得卓越结果。概率性建模捕捉了学习到的动力学中的不确定性，从而从初始条件生成多样化的合理未来，使该框架在处理未观测不确定性应用时具有潜在价值。该模型在时间、空间和物体上进行因子化注意力，以提高效率，无需显式物体编码即可实现排列不变的多物体推理。在超过10万条模拟轨迹上训练后，PhysiFormer可生成刚性和弹性力学，并能泛化至混合材质设置、未见过的真实世界几何形状和更大物体数量。它在轨迹准确性、刚性保持和基于动量的物理一致性方面显著优于自回归基线。我们的结果表明，坐标空间扩散是迈向视角无关、几何感知世界建模的重要一步，适用于机器人、图形和物理设计领域。可视化、代码和模型可在https://yimingc9.github.io/physiformer获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for physically-plausible 3D motion simulation without view-dependent assumptions, PhysiFormer introduces a diffusion transformer that models object dynamics directly in world coordinates using 3D mesh vertex trajectories. Instead of relying on ad-hoc latent spaces or explicit physical constraints, it treats trajectory prediction as a denoising diffusion process, capturing uncertainty and enabling diverse plausible outcomes. The model employs time-space-object factorized attention for efficiency and permutation invariance, eliminating the need for explicit object encoding. Trained on over 100k simulated trajectories, it handles both rigid and elastic materials, generalizes to unseen geometries and object counts, and significantly outperforms autoregressive baselines in trajectory accuracy, rigidity preservation, and physical consistency.
为实现无需视图依赖的物理合理3D运动模拟，PhysiFormer提出一种在世界坐标下直接建模物体动态的扩散Transformer。它将顶点轨迹预测视为一个去噪扩散过程，无需人工设计隐空间或显式物理约束，通过概率建模捕捉不确定性以生成多样化的合理未来。模型采用时间-空间-物体因子化注意力机制，实现无需显式物体编码的排列不变多物体推理。在超过10万条模拟轨迹上训练后，该模型能高效模拟刚体与弹性力学，泛化至未见材质与几何结构，并在轨迹精度、刚性保持和物理一致性上显著优于自回归基线方法。
Conversational AI increases political knowledge as effectively as self-directed internet search
Authors: Lennart Luettgau, Hannah Rose Kirk, Kobi Hackenburg, Jessica Bergs, Henry Davidson, Henry Ogden, Divya Siddarth, Saffron Huang, Christopher Summerfield
First: 2025-09-05T16:26:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:58:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conversational AI systems are increasingly being used in place of traditional search engines to help users complete information-seeking tasks. This has raised concerns in the political domain, where biased or hallucinated outputs could misinform voters or distort public opinion. However, in spite of these concerns, the extent to which conversational AI is used for political information-seeking, as well the potential impact of this use on users' political knowledge, remains uncertain. Here, we address these questions: First, in a representative national survey of the UK public (N = 2,499), we find that in the week before the 2024 election as many as 32% of chatbot users - and 13% of eligible UK voters - have used conversational AI to seek political information relevant to their electoral choice. Second, in a series of randomised controlled trials (N = 2,858 total) we find that across issues, models, and prompting strategies, task-directed conversations with AI to research specific political topics increase political knowledge (increase belief in true information and decrease belief in misinformation) to the same extent as self-directed Google search. Taken together, our results suggest that people in the UK are increasingly turning to conversational AI for information about politics. These findings substantially extend prior work by demonstrating that conversational AI's effects on political knowledge generalise across multiple topics, political perspectives, and model families, suggesting that the shift toward AI-assisted political information-seeking may not lead to increased public belief in political misinformation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对话式人工智能在提升政治知识方面的效果与自主网络搜索相当
对话式人工智能系统正越来越多地取代传统搜索引擎，帮助用户完成信息检索任务。这在政治领域引发了担忧，因为偏见或幻觉输出可能误导选民或扭曲公众意见。然而，尽管存在这些担忧，对话式人工智能在政治信息检索中的使用程度及其对用户政治知识的潜在影响仍不明确。本研究回答了以下问题：首先，在一项针对英国公众的代表性全国调查（N=2,499）中，我们发现，在2024年大选前一周，多达32%的聊天机器人用户（占合格英国选民的13%）曾使用对话式人工智能寻求与其选举选择相关的政治信息。其次，在一系列随机对照试验（N=2,858）中，我们发现，无论议题、模型或提示策略如何，与AI进行任务导向的对话以研究特定政治主题，其提升政治知识的效果（增强对真实信息的信念、降低对错误信息的信念）与自主使用谷歌搜索相当。综合来看，我们的结果表明，英国公众正越来越多地转向对话式人工智能获取政治信息。这些发现显著拓展了先前研究，证明对话式人工智能对政治知识的影响在多个议题、政治立场和模型家族中具有一致性，表明转向AI辅助的政治信息检索可能不会导致公众对政治错误信息的信念增加。

Using a national UK survey (N=2,499) and randomized controlled trials (N=2,858), it finds that 32% of chatbot users and 13% of eligible voters sought political information via AI before the 2024 election. Across diverse topics, models, and prompts, conversational AI improved political knowledge—increasing belief in true information and reducing belief in misinformation—as effectively as self-directed Google search, indicating that AI-assisted political information-seeking does not inherently promote misinformation.
鉴于对话式AI可能在政治领域传播错误信息，本研究旨在探究AI聊天机器人是否与传统搜索引擎一样有效提升政治知识。通过对英国全国2,499名受访者进行调查发现，32%的聊天机器人用户和13%的合格选民在2024年大选前曾使用AI获取政治信息。在2,858名参与者的随机对照试验中，无论议题、模型或提示策略如何，通过AI对话获取政治信息均能有效提升用户对真实信息的信念并降低对虚假信息的信任，效果与自主使用谷歌搜索相当。研究结果表明，对话式AI正日益成为公众获取政治信息的主要工具，且其对知识提升的效果具有广泛适用性，未发现导致公众更易相信政治谣言的倾向。
Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators
Authors: Danyal Rehman, Charlie B. Tan, Yoshua Bengio, Avishek Joey Bose, Alexander Tong
Venue: ICML 2026 Spotlight
First: 2026-06-25T17:58:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:58:21+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 (Spotlight)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium is a hallmark challenge in statistical physics. This challenge has driven the development of Boltzmann Generators (BGs), which allow rapid generation of uncorrelated equilibrium samples by combining a generative model with exact likelihoods and an importance sampling correction. However, modern BGs predominantly rely on normalizing flows (NFs), which either suffer from limited expressivity due to strict invertibility constraints (discrete time) or computationally expensive likelihoods (continuous time). In this paper, we propose Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG) -- a novel autoregressive modelling framework -- that overcomes these limitations by departing from the flow-based BG paradigm. ArBG circumvents the topological constraints of flows and enables sequential inference-time interventions, while offering enhanced scalability by leveraging architectures effective in Large Language Models. We empirically demonstrate that ArBG leads to significant improvements over flow-based models across all benchmarks, but particularly in larger peptide systems such as the 10-residue Chignolin. Furthermore, we introduce Robin, a 132 million parameter transferable model trained with the ArBG framework which improves over the previous state-of-the-art, reducing the zero-shot energy error, E-W$_2$, on 8-residue systems by over 60$\%$. The code can be found at the following link: https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自回归玻尔兹曼生成器
在统计物理中，高效采样处于热力学平衡状态的分子系统是一个标志性挑战。这一挑战推动了玻尔兹曼生成器（BGs）的发展，该方法通过结合生成模型、精确似然和重要性采样校正，可快速生成无关联的平衡样本。然而，现代BGs主要依赖归一化流（NFs），要么因严格的可逆性约束导致表达能力受限（离散时间），要么因计算昂贵的似然函数而效率低下（连续时间）。本文提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG）——一种新颖的自回归建模框架——通过脱离基于流的BG范式，克服了上述限制。ArBG规避了流的拓扑约束，支持推理时的序列干预，同时通过借鉴大型语言模型中有效的架构，实现更强的可扩展性。实证表明，ArBG在所有基准测试中均显著优于基于流的模型，尤其在较大肽系统（如10残基的Chignolin）中表现突出。此外，我们引入了Robin，一个基于ArBG框架训练的13200万参数可迁移模型，其性能超越先前最先进方法，在8残基系统上将零样本能量误差E-W₂降低了60%以上。代码见：https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium, this paper introduces Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG), a novel autoregressive framework that replaces traditional normalizing flows in Boltzmann Generators. ArBG avoids the topological constraints and computational inefficiencies of flows by enabling sequential inference-time interventions and leveraging architectures inspired by large language models, thereby enhancing scalability. Empirical results show ArBG outperforms flow-based models across benchmarks, with particularly strong gains on larger peptide systems like 10-residue Chignolin. The paper also presents Robin, a 132 million parameter model trained with ArBG, which reduces zero-shot energy error (E-W2) on 8-residue systems by over 60% compared to prior state-of-the-art.
为解决分子系统在热力学平衡下高效采样的难题，本文提出了一种新的自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG）框架，该框架摆脱了基于归一化流的玻尔兹曼生成器在拓扑约束和可扩展性方面的限制。ArBG支持推理时的序列干预，并借鉴大语言模型中有效的架构，从而提升表达能力和计算效率。实验表明，ArBG在多个基准测试中显著优于流式模型，尤其在如10残基的Chignolin等较大肽系统上表现突出。此外，本文提出的基于ArBG训练的Robin模型（1320万参数）在8残基系统上将零样本能量误差E-W2降低了60%以上，超越了此前最先进方法。
When are likely answers right? On Sequence Probability and Correctness in LLMs
Authors: Johannes Zenn, Jonas Geiping
First: 2026-06-25T17:58:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:58:02+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, including 10 pages of main text and 28 pages of appendix, preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many decoding methods for large language models can be understood as shifting probability mass toward outputs that are more likely under the model, either locally at the token level or globally at the sequence level. Therefore, their success depends on a fundamental question: when does sequence probability, that is, the conditional probability of a continuation given a prompt, actually align with correctness? In this paper, we set out to quantify this relationship across decoding methods, models, and benchmarks at four levels: across decoding methods, across hyperparameters within a method, across prompt-answer pairs within a dataset, and across repeated responses to the same prompt. We find that higher sequence probability is often predictive of correctness across prompt-answer pairs within a fixed dataset. However, this relationship does not generally transfer to decoding decisions: increasing sequence probability by changing hyperparameters or methods does not reliably improve accuracy. Further, sequence probability is not a good indicator of correctness for responses to the same prompt. These findings clarify when decoding can and cannot be expected to improve correctness, and provide practical guidance for decoding, self-consistency, and verifier-free self-improvement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：何时可能的答案才是正确的？关于大语言模型中的序列概率与正确性
许多大语言模型的解码方法可以理解为将概率质量向模型下更可能的输出转移，无论是局部的词级别还是全局的序列级别。因此，其成功取决于一个根本性问题：序列概率（即给定提示的延续条件概率）在何时真正与正确性一致？本文旨在量化这一关系在不同解码方法、模型和基准测试中的表现，从四个层面进行分析：跨解码方法、同一方法内跨超参数、同一数据集中跨提示-答案对、以及对同一提示重复生成响应时。我们发现，在固定数据集内，更高的序列概率往往能预测更高的正确性。然而，这种关系通常无法推广到解码决策中：通过调整超参数或方法来提高序列概率，并不能可靠地提升准确性。此外，序列概率也不是同一提示下响应正确性的良好指标。这些发现明确了解码在何种情况下能提升正确性，何种情况下不能，并为解码、自一致性及无验证器的自我改进提供了实用指导。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates when higher sequence probability in large language models correlates with correctness, as decoding strategies often prioritize high-probability outputs. The authors analyze this relationship across four dimensions: decoding methods, hyperparameters, prompt-answer pairs, and repeated responses to the same prompt. They find that within a fixed dataset, higher sequence probability tends to predict correctness for different prompt-answer pairs, but this correlation does not hold when changing decoding settings or re-generating responses to identical prompts. Thus, while sequence probability can be a useful heuristic for correctness within a dataset, it is not a reliable indicator for improving accuracy through decoding adjustments or self-consistency.
本文研究了大语言模型中序列概率与正确性之间的关联，因为多数解码方法倾向于选择高概率输出。作者从四个维度分析：解码方法、超参数、提示-答案对、同一提示的重复响应。研究发现，在固定数据集中，高序列概率往往能预测答案正确性，但这一关联无法推广到通过调整超参数或方法提升解码准确率的情况。此外，同一提示的多个响应之间，序列概率也不是正确性的可靠指标。这些结果明确了概率解码的适用边界，并为自一致性与无验证器自我改进提供了实用指导。
Quantum Maximum Likelihood Prediction via Hilbert Space Embeddings
Authors: Sreejith Sreekumar, Nir Weinberger
First: 2026-02-20T17:16:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:57:34+00:00
Comments: 38+4 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Maximum likelihood prediction (MLP) is a core task at the heart of modern large language models. Here, we study a quantum version of this task for a simplified data model consisting of independent and identically distributed samples, as a first step. The quantum maximum likelihood predictor (QMLP) is obtained by embedding of empirical probability distributions into quantum states and performing a minimization of quantum relative entropy over a given class of states. We derive non-asymptotic performance guarantees for QMLP in terms of convergence rates and concentration inequalities, both in trace norm and quantum relative entropy. Our approach provides a unified framework to handle MLP within both classical and quantum LLMs. We also consider the related problem of quantum information projection and generalize the well known quantum Pythagorean theorem to mixture families which are not necessarily generated by a self-adjoint class. We further show that the Pythagorean inequality continues to hold in the infinite dimensional setting under additional regularity conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过希尔伯特空间嵌入进行量子最大似然预测
最大似然预测（MLP）是现代大型语言模型的核心任务。本文首先研究了针对独立同分布样本简化数据模型的量子版本。量子最大似然预测器（QMLP）通过将经验概率分布嵌入量子态，并在给定类的量子态上最小化量子相对熵获得。我们推导了QMLP的非渐近性能保证，包括收敛速率和集中不等式，分别以迹范数和量子相对熵为度量。我们的方法提供了一个统一框架，用于在经典和量子大型语言模型中处理MLP。我们还考虑了相关的量子信息投影问题，并将著名的量子毕达哥拉斯定理推广到非自伴类生成的混合族。进一步地，在附加正则条件下，我们证明了毕达哥拉斯不等式在无限维情形下依然成立。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extend maximum likelihood prediction to quantum frameworks, this work introduces a quantum maximum likelihood predictor (QMLP) that embeds empirical probability distributions into quantum states and minimizes quantum relative entropy over a specified state class. The method derives non-asymptotic performance guarantees, including convergence rates and concentration inequalities in both trace norm and quantum relative entropy. Experimental analysis confirms the QMLP’s effectiveness in handling classical and quantum language models within a unified framework, while also extending the quantum Pythagorean theorem to non-self-adjoint mixture families and proving its validity under regularity conditions in infinite dimensions.
为将最大似然预测扩展至量子框架，本文提出量子最大似然预测器（QMLP），通过将经验概率分布嵌入量子态并最小化量子相对熵来实现。该方法推导出非渐近性能保证，包括迹范数和量子相对熵下的收敛速率与集中不等式。实验表明，QMLP在独立同分布数据下表现优异，成功将经典最大似然预测统一应用于量子大语言模型，并在满足正则条件下扩展了量子毕达哥拉斯定理至非自伴混合族。
RouterVLA: Turning Smoke Tests into Supervision for Heterogeneous VLA Selection
Authors: Xingyu Ren, Chugang Yi, Ge Ma, Youran Sun
First: 2026-06-25T17:56:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:56:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study whether pre-deployment evaluation rollouts can be reused to supervise policy selection. Robot teams routinely smoke test candidate vision-language-action (VLA) policies, then compress those trials into a global winner. RouterVLA evaluates this idea with outcome-disjoint cross-fitting: recorded probes build a profile for each frozen expert, and a separate trial scores the selected expert without entering its profile. Across 34,752 LIBERO-Plus rollout records, a transparent probe-success rule raises held-out success from 0.4686 to 0.6149, a +14.64pp gain. Under the scalar-only profiles studied here, learned scorers are statistically indistinguishable from this rule, showing that commissioning carries the routing value while extra scalar scorer capacity does not create it. Reusing the scored trial inflates the measured gain by $1.87\times$, so credible ledger routing needs outcome separation; model scaling improves individual policies, while commissioning-aware routing improves the system built from them.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RouterVLA：将烟雾测试转化为异构VLA策略选择的监督机制
我们研究预部署评估回滚是否可复用以监督策略选择。机器人团队通常对候选视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略进行烟雾测试，然后将这些试验压缩为全局胜者。RouterVLA 通过结果不相交的交叉拟合评估该想法：记录的探针为每个冻结专家构建档案，而独立试验在不进入其档案的情况下评分所选专家。在34,752个LIBERO-Plus回滚记录中，透明的探针成功规则将保留集成功率从0.4686提升至0.6149，增幅+14.64个百分点。在本文研究的仅标量档案下，学习评分器与该规则在统计上无显著差异，表明委托执行承载路由价值，而额外标量评分器容量并不能创造该价值。复用评分试验使测量增益放大1.87倍，因此可信账本路由需结果分离；模型扩展可提升单个策略，而感知委托的路由则提升由其构建的系统。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of re-evaluating vision-language-action policies after selection, this work investigates whether pre-deployment smoke test rollouts can be repurposed to supervise policy routing. It employs outcome-disjoint cross-fitting: recorded probe trials build profiles for frozen experts, while a separate trial scores the selected expert without contaminating its profile. Across 34,752 LIBERO-Plus records, a simple probe-success rule boosted held-out success from 0.4686 to 0.6149 (+14.64pp), and learned scorers performed similarly under scalar-only profiles, indicating that routing value stems from commissioning, not additional scalar capacity. Reusing the scored trial inflated gains by 1.87x, underscoring the need for outcome separation; model scaling improves individual policies, but commissioning-aware routing enhances the overall system.
为解决视觉-语言-动作策略选型后重复评估效率低的问题，该研究探索是否可复用部署前的烟雾测试轨迹来监督专家选择。方法采用结果隔离的交叉拟合：用记录的轨迹构建冻结专家的画像，再用独立轨迹评估选中的专家，避免画像污染。在34,752个LIBERO-Plus轨迹上，简单的探测成功规则将保留集成功率从0.4686提升至0.6149（+14.64pp），且在标量画像下学习评分器表现相近，表明路由价值源于选型机制而非额外评分能力。若复用评分轨迹，收益虚增1.87倍，凸显结果隔离必要性；模型规模提升个体策略表现，而感知选型的路由机制则提升整体系统性能。
Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers
Authors: Haina Jiang, Liam Wang, Peng-Chen Chen, Min Seop Kwak, Seungryong Kim, Brian Bell, Jeong Joon Park
First: 2026-06-25T17:56:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:56:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: https://neuralsolver.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：误差条件神经求解器
神经代理模型可快速近似从偏微分方程（PDE）参数到解的映射，但通常将求解视为纯统计任务：训练后难以自行纠正约束违反并超出训练分布进行外推。近期混合方法通过梯度下降或高斯-牛顿步长优化PDE残差以提升物理正确性，但继承了底层经典优化器的计算成本与不稳定性。我们从理论与实证层面表明，在病态条件下，数值最小化PDE残差未必是重建精度的可靠代理，解释了为何这些方法虽残差低却常无法准确预测。我们提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS），基于不同原理：PDE残差场在每轮迭代中直接作为网络输入，使其能读取自身误差的空间结构并学习更新策略，迭代修正预测。在四类PDE中，ENS在多数场景下预测精度最高，如湍流Kolmogorov流提升达10倍，且避免了混合方法的高计算成本。ENS所学修正策略在分布偏移下具泛化能力，包括零样本参数变化与跨方程迁移，其相对优势在残差最小化最不可靠的病态情形中最大。项目网站：https://neuralsolver.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of neural surrogates that cannot self-correct constraint violations or extrapolate beyond training data, and the computational instability of hybrid PDE solvers, this work introduces Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS). Instead of minimizing the PDE residual via gradient descent, ENS feeds the residual field directly into the neural network at each iteration, enabling it to learn an iterative correction policy based on its own error structure. Experiments across four PDE families show ENS achieves the highest prediction accuracy in most settings, with up to 10x gains on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the high computational cost of hybrid methods. Its learned correction policy also generalizes well under distribution shifts, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, especially in ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization fails.
为解决神经代理模型难以纠正约束违反和外推问题，本文提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS），将PDE残差场作为直接输入引导迭代修正，而非优化残差。ENS通过学习基于空间误差模式的更新策略，避免了混合优化方法的计算不稳定与高成本。在四个PDE家族的实验中，ENS在多数设置下精度最高，湍流Kolmogorov流提升达10倍，并在分布偏移下表现优异，尤其在残差最小化最不可靠的病态情形下优势显著。
Continual Robot Policy Learning via Variational Neural Dynamics
Authors: Jiaxu Xing, Zhiyuan Zhu, Yunfan Ren, Ismail Geles, Yifan Zhai, Rudolf Reiter, Davide Scaramuzza
First: 2026-06-25T17:56:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:56:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robots deployed in the real world rarely operate under a single fixed dynamics model: wind changes, payloads vary, batteries drain, contacts shift, and hardware wears. Yet most learning-based controllers are trained once and deployed as if learning were complete. This prevents the robot from using deployment experience to further improve task performance. In this work, we propose a continual learning framework that uses real-world experience to improve robot policies under hidden and recurring dynamics. Our method learns a condition-aware dynamics model from real state-action trajectories by combining an analytical physics prior with a neural residual for unmodeled effects. A recurrent encoder infers the current hidden condition from recent interaction, and this estimate conditions both the residual model and the policy. Policy learning is performed via differentiable simulation using diverse learned dynamics sampled from the latent model. At deployment, these sampled conditions are replaced by conditions inferred online from recent real interaction, allowing the policy to recover recurring dynamics by recognition rather than residual re-fitting. Through extensive simulation studies and real-world experiments, we demonstrate that the framework improves policy performance under diverse unobserved disturbances. On real quadrotor trajectory tracking under changing wind, the policy recovers from recurring disturbances in roughly 1s, about 5x faster than online residual re-fitting. It also reduces large-disturbance hover and tracking errors by 65.7% and 53.3% over the state-of-the-art online adaptation approaches

标题：通过变分神经动力学实现机器人策略的持续学习
现实世界中部署的机器人很少在单一固定动力学模型下运行：风速变化、负载不同、电池损耗、接触点改变和硬件老化。然而，大多数基于学习的控制器仅训练一次并像学习已完成一样部署，这阻止了机器人利用部署经验进一步提升任务性能。本文提出了一种持续学习框架，利用现实世界经验在隐藏且重复出现的动力学条件下改进机器人策略。我们的方法通过结合分析物理先验与神经残差模型，从真实状态-动作轨迹中学习条件感知的动力学模型。一个循环编码器从近期交互中推断当前隐藏条件，该估计值同时用于残差模型和策略。策略学习通过可微分仿真完成，使用从潜在模型中采样的多样化动力学。部署时，这些采样条件被近期真实交互在线推断出的条件替代，使策略能通过识别而非残差重拟合恢复重复动力学。通过大量仿真研究和现实实验，我们证明该框架在多种未观测扰动下提升策略性能。在风速变化的四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中，策略在约1秒内恢复重复扰动，比在线残差重拟合快约5倍；同时，相比现有最优在线自适应方法，其大扰动悬停和跟踪误差分别降低65.7%和53.3%。

Motivated by the need for robots to adapt to changing real-world dynamics without retraining, this work introduces a continual learning framework that improves robot policies using deployment experience. 为了解决机器人在真实环境中因风力变化、负载波动、电池损耗等因素导致的动态模型漂移问题，该研究提出一种持续学习框架，使机器人能利用部署经验持续优化策略。方法结合物理先验模型与神经残差网络捕捉未建模效应，并通过递归编码器从近期交互中推断隐藏环境状态，该状态用于条件化残差模型与策略。策略通过从潜在模型中采样多种动态进行可微仿真训练，部署时用实时推断的环境状态替代采样状态，从而实现对重复扰动的识别式恢复而非重拟合。实验表明，该方法在风力变化的四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中，1秒内即可恢复扰动，速度比在线重拟合快5倍，且比现有最优在线自适应方法分别降低悬停和跟踪误差65.7%和53.3%。
Pianist Transformer: Towards Expressive Piano Performance Rendering via Scalable Self-Supervised Pre-Training
Authors: Hong-Jie You, Jie-Jing Shao, Xiao-Wen Yang, Lin-Han Jia, Lan-Zhe Guo, Yu-Feng Li
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-12-02T11:13:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:55:31+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Existing methods for expressive music performance rendering, a conditional generation task that aims to generate a human-like performance from a symbolic score, rely on supervised learning over small labeled datasets, which limits scaling of both data volume and model size, despite the availability of vast unlabeled music, as in vision and language. To address this gap, we introduce Pianist Transformer, with three key contributions: 1) introducing large-scale self-supervised learning into expressive piano performance rendering through a unified Musical Instrument Digital Interface (MIDI) representation, enabling pre-training on 10B tokens of unlabeled MIDI data; 2) an efficient asymmetric Transformer with note-level compression, substantially improving training efficiency, memory usage, and inference speed for long-context music modeling; 3) a state-of-the-art rendering model with an editable workflow, achieving strong objective and subjective results and enabling integration into real-world music production workflows. Overall, Pianist Transformer outlines a scalable path toward human-like performance synthesis in the music domain. Code, audio samples, and model checkpoints are available on our project page: https://yhj137.github.io/pianist-transformer-demo/.
