为突破现有监督学习方法受限于小规模标注数据的瓶颈，该研究提出Pianist Transformer，通过统一MIDI表示在100亿个无标注MIDI数据上进行自监督预训练，实现钢琴表演生成的规模化。其采用不对称Transformer架构并引入音符级压缩，显著提升长时序音乐建模的训练效率与推理速度。实验表明，该模型在客观与主观评估中均达到当前最优水平，并可无缝嵌入实际音乐制作流程，为音乐领域生成类人钢琴表演提供了可扩展的路径。
Bridging Performance and Generalization in Reinforcement Learning for Agile Flight
Authors: Jonathan Green, Jiaxu Xing, Nico Messikommer, Angel Romero, Davide Scaramuzza
First: 2026-06-25T17:54:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:54:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous drone racing is a fundamentally challenging regime for autonomous aerial robots, requiring time-optimal control while operating under persistent actuation saturation. While reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved human-level performance in this domain, current methods fail to generalize; policies trained on specific environments often crash immediately in unseen configurations. This failure reflects the intrinsic difficulty of zero-shot generalization in agile flight, arising from high-dimensional task variation and the tight coupling between safety and performance at high speeds. Existing approaches that improve generalization impose a substantial cost on flight speed: control policies must significantly degrade performance to achieve even modest levels of generalization. In this work, we propose a framework for zero-shot generalization in agile flight for RL-based drone racing. By combining task-aware switching based on learning progress with a physically informed procedural track generator, the framework produces a fast and robust generalist policy without test-time adaptation. Our method achieves strong zero-shot performance across a wide range of unseen racetracks in the real world, demonstrating a 7.4x improvement in generalization over the state-of-the-art approaches, while maintaining competitive racing speeds. We validate our method's results in both simulation and real-world settings, including a challenging vision-based, end-to-end control setting that operates without explicit state estimation, where all prior approaches fail to generalize.
中文标题/摘要
标题：强化学习在敏捷飞行中的性能与泛化能力协同提升
自主无人机竞速是自主空中机器人面临的基本挑战，要求在持续执行饱和条件下实现时间最优控制。尽管强化学习（RL）已在该领域达到人类水平表现，但现有方法缺乏泛化能力：在特定环境中训练的策略在未见过的配置中常立即崩溃。这一失败反映了敏捷飞行中零样本泛化的固有难度，源于高维任务变化及高速下安全与性能的紧密耦合。现有提升泛化能力的方法往往显著牺牲飞行速度：为实现适度泛化，控制策略必须大幅降低性能。本文提出一种面向RL无人机竞速的零样本泛化框架，通过结合基于学习进度的任务感知切换与物理驱动的程序化赛道生成器，无需测试时适应即可生成快速且鲁棒的通用策略。该方法在真实世界多种未见赛道上实现强零样本表现，相较现有最先进方法泛化能力提升7.4倍，同时保持竞争力的竞速速度。我们在仿真与真实世界场景中验证了该方法，包括无需显式状态估计的视觉端到端控制挑战场景——此前所有方法均无法在此场景中泛化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for RL-based drone racing policies that generalize across unseen tracks without performance degradation, this work introduces a framework combining task-aware switching with a physically informed track generator to enable zero-shot generalization. The method dynamically switches control policies based on learning progress and generates diverse, realistic tracks to train a robust generalist policy. Experimental results show the approach achieves 7.4x better generalization than prior methods while maintaining competitive flight speeds, validated in both simulation and real-world vision-based end-to-end control settings where previous methods fail.
为解决强化学习在无人机竞速中难以跨未见赛道泛化且性能严重下降的问题，该研究提出一种结合任务感知切换与物理驱动赛道生成器的框架，实现零样本泛化。方法根据学习进度动态切换控制策略，并生成多样化的现实赛道以训练鲁棒的通用策略。实验表明，该方法在模拟与真实世界（含视觉端到端控制）中均优于现有方法7.4倍，同时保持了竞争力的飞行速度，验证了其在无显式状态估计场景下的泛化能力。
Weak-to-Strong Elicitation via Mismatched Wrong Drafts
Authors: Wei Deng
First: 2026-05-17T08:12:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:53:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider whether off-policy experience from a smaller, weaker model can elicit capability in a stronger learner that on-policy RL fine-tuning (e.g., GRPO) does not reach. We find that injecting mathematically wrong drafts from a smaller but more domain-trained model -- mismatched to the current problem -- into a stronger learner's GRPO context consistently outperforms standard on-policy GRPO on held-out MATH-500 and out-of-distribution AIME 2025/2026. Concretely, we use Mathstral-7B as the learner, Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B as the draft model, 8.8K Level 3--5 MATH problems (with MATH-500 held out), and train with Dr. GRPO. Mismatch is an active ingredient: shuffling drafts to mismatched problems while holding everything else constant yields $+1.62$pp on MATH-500 (greedy pass@1) over the matched-wrong variant ($n=10$ seeds, $p=0.0015$, Welch's $t$). In fact, the mismatched-wrong variant leads all other variants we tested on MATH-500 across both greedy pass@1 and sampling pass@$k$. On out-of-distribution AIME 2025 and 2026, the mismatched-wrong variant uniquely lifts pass@$k$ above both Mathstral-7B (in its native [INST] format) and the Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B draft model at every sample budget from $k=1$ to $k=1024$ across 2 seeds ($+14.2$pp on 2025 and $+9.0$pp on 2026 at pass@1024 over Mathstral-7B), and at pass@1024 also leads no-draft, matched-wrong, and mismatched-correct variants on both years. All variants use the same prompt with no draft injection at test time. The recipe -- trained on a single GPU with no SFT, no reward models, no synthesized data, and no produce-critique-revise inner loop -- reaches 71.98% MATH-500 on Mathstral-7B-v0.1, the highest published result on this model to our knowledge, surpassing the heavier WizardMath pipeline at 70.9% on full MATH (SFT + PPO with process/instruction reward models).
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过不匹配的错误草稿实现弱到强的引导
我们探讨较小、较弱模型的离策略经验是否能激发较强学习者的能力，而后者在策略梯度微调（如GRPO）中无法达到。我们发现，将来自较小但领域训练更充分的模型的数学上错误草稿（与当前问题不匹配）注入较强学习者的GRPO上下文，能持续优于标准的策略梯度微调方法，在MATH-500和分布外AIME 2025/2026数据集上表现更优。具体而言，我们使用Mathstral-7B作为学习者，Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B作为草稿模型，8.8K个Level 3–5的MATH问题（MATH-500保留为验证集），并使用Dr. GRPO训练。不匹配是关键因素：在保持其他条件不变的情况下，将草稿随机分配到不匹配的问题上，相比匹配错误草稿的变体，在MATH-500上（greedy pass@1）提升1.62个百分点（n=10种子，p=0.0015，Welch t检验）。事实上，该不匹配错误草稿变体在MATH-500上所有测试变体中表现最佳，无论greedy pass@1还是sampling pass@$k$。在分布外的AIME 2025和2026数据集上，该变体在所有采样预算（k=1至1024）下，均显著提升pass@$k$表现，超越Mathstral-7B（原生[INST]格式）和Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B草稿模型（2个种子，2025年+14.2pp，2026年+9.0pp，pass@1024相比Mathstral-7B），并在pass@1024时也优于无草稿、匹配错误、不匹配正确等变体。所有变体在测试时均使用相同提示，无草稿注入。该方法仅需单GPU训练，无需SFT、奖励模型、合成数据或produce-critique-revise内循环，已在Mathstral-7B-v0.1上达到71.98%的MATH-500准确率，据我们所知是该模型最高发表结果，超越了更重的WizardMath流水线（70.9%全MATH，SFT+PPO+过程/指令奖励模型）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of off-policy learning to unlock capabilities beyond on-policy fine-tuning, this study investigates whether injecting mathematically incorrect drafts from a smaller, domain-specialized model into a stronger learner’s GRPO training can enhance performance. Using Mathstral-7B as the learner and Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B as the draft source, the method trains with Dr. GRPO on 8.8K MATH problems, excluding MATH-500 for evaluation. Key findings show that mismatching drafts to unrelated problems significantly boosts performance: +1.62pp on MATH-500 (greedy pass@1) over matched-wrong variants, and on out-of-distribution AIME 2025/2026, the mismatched-wrong variant outperforms all others at every sample budget up to 1024, achieving +14.2pp and +9.0pp gains over the base learner at pass@1024. The approach requires no SFT, reward models, or synthesized data, reaching 71.98% on MATH-500 — the highest reported result for Mathstral-7B-v0.1 — surpassing WizardMath’s 70.9% on full MATH.
该研究旨在探索弱模型的离策略经验是否能激发强学习者超越其策略梯度训练（如GRPO）的能力。方法上，使用Mathstral-7B作为学习者，Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B作为生成错误草稿的模型，通过Dr. GRPO在8.8K MATH问题上训练（MATH-500保留用于评估）。实验发现，将与当前问题不匹配的错误草稿注入训练过程，显著优于匹配错误或正确草稿的变体，在MATH-500和AIME 2025/2026测试集上均表现最佳，MATH-500提升1.62个百分点，AIME 2025在pass@1024时提升14.2个百分点。该方法无需监督微调、奖励模型或合成数据，最终在MATH-500上达到71.98%的准确率，超越WizardMath在完整MATH数据集上的70.9%表现。
Mapping Political-Elite Networks in Europe with a Multilingual Joint Entity-Relation Extraction Pipeline
Authors: Kirill Solovev, Jana Lasser
First: 2026-06-25T17:51:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:51:59+00:00
Comments: 34 pages, 17 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Whether political elites organise into rent-seeking coalitions that capture public resources or civic networks that sustain governance is a central question in comparative politics. Yet observing these complex, informal, and adversarial ties at scale has historically required intensive manual coding, while automated text-as-data methods have largely been limited to simple co-occurrence. Recent large language model (LLM) approaches offer a path forward but often rely on proprietary APIs, lack cross-lingual capability, and struggle with scalable entity resolution. We present a modular, fully open-weight pipeline for multilingual joint entity-relation extraction that builds signed, temporal knowledge graphs from massive unstructured news corpora. It combines span-based named-entity recognition (NER) with a three-stage linking cascade mapping mentions to language-independent Wikidata identifiers; a high-throughput, ontology-constrained mixture-of-experts model then uses guided decoding to extract directed, signed relationships grounded in a domain ontology. A full-coverage spot-check against a 3491-relation gold standard shows high textual correctness (68.2% strict to 93.7% lenient). Two large-scale case studies validate the pipeline against the public record. In Austria, it reconstructs a political party's complete lifecycle, dating internal fractures and tracking personnel into successor factions and court convictions. In a Polish corpus, it uncovers the overlapping economic and governance networks of state-enterprise patronage, alongside the structurally balanced, signed conflict network of the polarized Civic Platform (Platforma Obywatelska, PO)--Law and Justice (Prawo i Sprawiedliwość, PiS) duopoly. By bridging raw multilingual text and structured relational data, our framework provides a robust, replicable foundation for cross-national empirical computational social science.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用多语言联合实体-关系抽取管道绘制欧洲政治精英网络
政治精英是组织为追求租金的联盟以攫取公共资源，还是构建支持治理的公民网络，这是比较政治学的核心问题。然而，大规模观察这些复杂、非正式且对抗性的联系，历史上需要大量人工编码，而自动化文本数据方法大多仅限于简单的共现分析。近期的大语言模型（LLM）方法提供了新的路径，但往往依赖专有API、缺乏跨语言能力，且在可扩展的实体解析方面存在困难。我们提出了一种模块化、完全开源权重的多语言联合实体-关系抽取管道，该管道从海量非结构化新闻语料库构建带符号、时间维度的知识图谱。它结合基于跨度的命名实体识别（NER）与三阶段链接级联，将提及映射到语言无关的Wikidata标识符；随后，一个高吞吐量、受本体约束的专家混合模型通过引导解码提取基于领域本体的有向、带符号关系。对3491个关系的黄金标准进行全覆盖抽查显示，文本正确率高（严格标准68.2%至宽松标准93.7%）。两个大规模案例研究验证了该管道与公开记录的一致性：在奥地利，它重建了某政党的完整生命周期，追溯内部裂痕并追踪人员至继任派系和法院判决；在波兰语料库中，它揭示了国家企业庇护网络的重叠经济与治理关系，以及极化公民平台（Platforma Obywatelska, PO）与法律与公正党（Prawo i Sprawiedliwość, PiS）二元垄断结构中平衡的、带符号的冲突网络。通过连接原始多语言文本与结构化关系数据，我们的框架为跨国实证计算社会科学提供了稳健、可复制的基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand whether political elites form rent-seeking coalitions or civic networks, this study develops a multilingual joint entity-relation extraction pipeline to automate the mapping of complex political ties from unstructured news text. The method combines span-based NER with a three-stage linking cascade to map mentions to Wikidata identifiers, followed by a mixture-of-experts model using guided decoding to extract directed, signed relationships grounded in a domain ontology. Experimental results show high textual correctness (68.2% strict to 93.7% lenient) against a gold standard, and two case studies—Austria and Poland—demonstrate the pipeline’s ability to reconstruct political party lifecycles and uncover overlapping patronage and conflict networks among major parties.
为理解政治精英是形成寻租联盟还是公民网络，本研究构建了一个多语言联合实体-关系抽取管道，自动从非结构化新闻文本中映射复杂政治关系。该方法结合基于跨度的命名实体识别与三阶段链接级联，将提及映射至Wikidata标识符，再通过基于领域本体的混合专家模型抽取有向、带符号的关系。实验结果显示文本正确率高（严格68.2%至宽松93.7%），在奥地利和波兰的案例研究中验证了其重建政党生命周期、揭示利益输送与政治冲突网络的能力。
RayPE: Ray-Space Positional Encoding for 3D-Aware Video Generation
Authors: Minghao Yin, Jiahao Lu, Wenbo Hu, Wang Zhao, Shan Ying, Kai Han
First: 2026-06-25T17:51:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:51:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern video diffusion transformers position their tokens through RoPE on the (u,v,t) axes -- a description of the camera's sampling grid that says nothing about the 3D structure of the scene. We observe that the geometric relation between two camera rays is captured by the Plucker reciprocal product, which is bilinear in the two rays -- the same algebraic form as the dot product in Transformer attention. Building on this analogy, we propose RayPE, a positional-encoding extension that injects per-token 6D Plucker coordinates additively into the queries and keys of self-attention, with a query/key flip arrangement under which the symmetric identity configuration coincides exactly with the reciprocal product. The injection is additive, the resulting attention score decomposes into a content term, a geometry term, and two content and geometry cross-terms -- all of which our experiments find individually necessary. To make the encoding stable across video data with heterogeneous camera-translation scales (SfM, deep SLAM, metric), we further decouple ray direction from moment magnitude, gate the encoding by a learned function of the log-magnitude, and apply RMSNorm to align it with the QKNorm-normalized content branch. The full module adds less than 0.1% parameters to a pretrained video DiT, is zero-initialized to start from the pretrained weights, and improves camera controllability, cross-frame 3D consistency, and overall video quality on a four-dataset training mixture.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RayPE：用于3D感知视频生成的射线空间位置编码
现代视频扩散Transformer在(u,v,t)轴上通过RoPE对token进行定位——这是一种描述相机采样网格的方法，但未涉及场景的3D结构。我们观察到，两条相机射线之间的几何关系可通过Plucker互乘积捕捉，该乘积在两条射线上是双线性的——与Transformer注意力中的点积具有相同的代数形式。基于这一类比，我们提出RayPE，一种位置编码扩展方法，将每个token的6D Plucker坐标加性注入自注意力的查询和键中，并采用查询/键翻转配置，使得对称恒等配置恰好与互乘积一致。该注入为加性，生成的注意力分数分解为内容项、几何项以及两个内容-几何交叉项——实验表明，这些项均单独必要。为使编码在具有异质相机平移尺度的视频数据（SfM、深度SLAM、度量）上保持稳定，我们进一步将射线方向与矩量幅度解耦，通过学习的对数幅度函数门控编码，并应用RMSNorm使其与QKNorm归一化的内容分支对齐。完整模块为预训练视频DiT增加的参数少于0.1%，初始时零初始化以从预训练权重开始，且在四个数据集混合训练中提升了相机可控性、跨帧3D一致性及整体视频质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing video diffusion models that encode token positions using RoPE without capturing 3D scene geometry, the authors propose RayPE, a positional encoding method that injects per-token 6D Plucker coordinates into transformer queries and keys, leveraging the bilinear structure of the Plucker reciprocal product to encode geometric relations between camera rays. The method uses a query/key flip to align the attention score decomposition with the reciprocal product, enabling separate content and geometry terms that are shown to be individually necessary for performance. To handle varying camera scales across datasets, the encoding decouples ray direction from magnitude, gates it via a learned log-magnitude function, and normalizes it with RMSNorm. Experiments demonstrate that RayPE, added with minimal parameters to a pretrained DiT, enhances camera controllability, cross-frame 3D consistency, and overall video quality across four datasets.
为解决现有视频扩散模型仅通过RoPE编码2D相机采样网格而忽略3D场景几何结构的问题，作者提出RayPE方法，将每个token的6D Plucker坐标加性注入Transformer的查询与键中，利用Plucker互反积的双线性特性与注意力点积结构相似，并通过查询/键翻转确保对称性匹配。实验表明，该注入分解为内容、几何及交叉项，各项均对性能至关重要。为适应不同相机尺度（SfM、深度SLAM、度量尺度）的数据，方法通过幅度门控与RMSNorm稳定编码，并在预训练视频DiT上仅增加不到0.1%参数，显著提升相机可控性、跨帧3D一致性及整体视频质量。
VibeAct: Vibration to Actions for Contact-Rich Reactive Robot Dexterity
Authors: Yuemin Mao, Uksang Yoo, Jean Oh, Jonathan Francis, Jeffrey Ichnowski
First: 2026-06-25T17:50:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:50:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Dexterous manipulation depends on contact events that are fast, local, and often visually occluded. Piezoelectric microphones offer a compact and high-bandwidth way to sense these interactions, but the resulting vibro-acoustic signals are difficult to simulate faithfully enough for end-to-end sim-to-real policy learning on dexterous robot hands. We propose VibeAct, a framework that bridges real vibrotactile sensing and simulation-based reinforcement learning through a shared physical representation of contact and slip. In the real world, we embed piezoelectric microphones into a dexterous robot hand and collect vibro-acoustic data through teleoperation, then replay the recordings in a calibrated digital clone to automatically label per-finger contact and slip. A tactile estimator learns to predict contact and slip from real microphone waveforms, while manipulation policies are trained in simulation on the same representation computed directly from simulated contacts. This decoupling lets policies exploit rapid tactile feedback without simulating raw audio. Across five contact-rich tasks spanning regrasping, in-hand reorientation, and insertion, VibeAct consistently outperforms a proprioception-and-point-cloud baseline in simulation, with the largest gains on tasks requiring sustained reactive control, where the continuous slip-magnitude channel proves the most informative observation. The learned policies transfer to a physical dexterous hand-arm platform, improving success rates on deployed tasks. Project videos and additional details are at https://vibeact.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VibeAct：基于振动感知的接触密集型反应式机器人灵巧操作
灵巧操作依赖于快速、局部且常被视觉遮挡的接触事件。压电麦克风提供了一种紧凑且高带宽的感知方式，但由此产生的声振信号难以足够真实地模拟，从而阻碍了灵巧机器人手的端到端仿真到现实策略学习。我们提出VibeAct框架，通过共享接触与滑移的物理表示，连接真实振动触觉感知与基于仿真的强化学习。在真实世界中，我们将压电麦克风嵌入灵巧机器人手中，通过远程操作收集声振数据，再在标定的数字克隆体中回放录音，自动标注每根手指的接触与滑移。触觉估计器从真实麦克风波形中学习预测接触与滑移，而操作策略则在仿真环境中基于直接从模拟接触计算出的相同表示进行训练。这种解耦使策略能利用快速触觉反馈，而无需模拟原始音频。在涵盖重新抓取、手内重定向和插入等五类接触密集型任务中，VibeAct在仿真中持续优于基于本体感觉与点云的基线方法，尤其在需要持续反应控制的任务中表现最佳，其中连续滑移幅度通道是最具信息量的观测值。所学习的策略可迁移到物理灵巧手臂平台，提升部署任务的成功率。项目视频与更多详情见https://vibeact.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of simulating fast, occluded contact events for dexterous robot manipulation, the authors introduce VibeAct, a framework that aligns real vibro-acoustic sensing with simulation-based reinforcement learning via a shared physical model of contact and slip. Piezoelectric microphones embedded in a robot hand capture real-world vibro-acoustic signals, which are replayed in a calibrated digital twin to automatically label contact and slip per finger. A tactile estimator learns to predict these states from microphone data, while manipulation policies are trained in simulation using the same contact-based representation. Experimental results across five contact-rich tasks show VibeAct outperforms a proprioception-and-point-cloud baseline, especially in tasks demanding sustained reactive control, where the slip-magnitude channel significantly boosts performance. The learned policies successfully transfer to a physical robot platform, improving task success rates.
为解决机器人灵巧操作中接触事件难以感知的问题，作者提出VibeAct框架，将压电麦克风传感与基于模拟的强化学习相结合。通过在机器人手中嵌入麦克风并记录遥操作过程中的振动声信号，构建数字孪生体自动标注每根手指的接触与滑移事件。一个触觉估计器从真实麦克风信号中学习预测接触与滑移，而策略则在模拟环境中使用由模拟接触直接生成的相同表示进行训练。这种解耦方式使策略能利用快速触觉反馈而不需模拟原始音频。在五个接触密集型任务中，VibeAct显著优于基于 proprioception 和点云的基线，尤其在需要持续反应控制的任务中表现突出，其中滑移幅度通道最为关键。所学习的策略成功迁移到物理机器人平台，提升了部署任务的成功率。
Understanding Domain-Aware Distribution Alignment in Budgeted Entity Matching
Authors: Nicholas Pulsone, Gregory Goren, Roee Shraga
First: 2026-06-25T17:49:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:49:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Entity Matching (EM) is a core operation in the data integration pipeline, where records from different sources are compared to determine whether they refer to the same real-world entity. Recent work has incorporated domain information and low-resource learning techniques to better adapt EM systems to realistic settings. While these approaches have demonstrated strong performance, it remains unclear how they behave under varying data constraints and levels of supervision in practice. In this paper, we investigate a state-of-the-art method for low-resource, domain-aware EM--BEACON--and study how its performance is affected by different algorithmic choices and data availability conditions. We conduct a series of targeted experiments to evaluate these variations, providing deeper insight into the role of distribution alignment and the behavior of the BEACON framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：预算实体匹配中领域感知分布对齐的理解
实体匹配（EM）是数据集成流程中的核心操作，用于比较不同来源的记录以判断它们是否指向同一现实世界实体。近期研究已结合领域信息和低资源学习技术，以更好地使EM系统适应现实场景。尽管这些方法表现优异，但其在不同数据约束和监督水平下的实际表现仍不明确。本文研究当前最先进的低资源、领域感知EM方法——BEACON，并探讨其性能如何受不同算法选择和数据可用性条件的影响。我们通过一系列针对性实验评估这些变化，深入揭示分布对齐的作用及BEACON框架的行为。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how domain-aware distribution alignment affects performance in low-resource entity matching, motivated by the need to understand how state-of-the-art methods like BEACON behave under varying data constraints and supervision levels. The authors systematically evaluate BEACON across different algorithmic configurations and data availability scenarios through targeted experiments, revealing that its effectiveness is highly sensitive to distribution alignment strategies and the amount of labeled data available, with performance degrading significantly under severe budget constraints.
该研究旨在探究领域感知分布对齐在低资源实体匹配中的作用，动机源于理解实际场景下不同数据约束对系统性能的影响。论文通过系统实验评估了当前最先进的BEACON框架，在不同算法选择和数据可用性条件下测试其表现。结果表明，BEACON的性能对分布对齐策略和数据预算高度敏感，特定对齐方法在资源受限时仍能稳定提升效果。
SAM2Matting: Generalized Image and Video Matting
Authors: Ruiqi Shen, Guangquan Jie, Chang Liu, Henghui Ding
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-25T17:48:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:48:27+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026. Extended version. Project Page: https://henghuiding.com/SAM2Matting/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite impressive advances in image matting, video matting remains challenging due to the inherent gap between high-level tracking, which requires frame-wise understanding, and low-level matting, which focuses on extremely fine-grained details. Existing methods attempt this with expensive and narrowly-scoped video matting datasets, which may limit out-of-domain generalization and compromise tracking robustness. We rethink the paradigm with SAM2Matting, a tracker-to-matting framework that advances VOS trackers to high-fidelity video matting. Specifically, it decouples the task by enhancing a foundational tracker (e.g., SAM2, SAM3) with a region-proposal bridge and dedicated matting heads, enabling the uncompromised tracker to handle temporal consistency while the matting components resolve fine-grained details. Notably, despite being trained only on images, SAM2Matting establishes new state-of-the-art performance on video matting, supports diverse prompt types, maintains strong temporal consistency, and demonstrates robust generalization across both human-centric and in-the-wild scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAM2Matting：通用图像与视频抠像
尽管图像抠像取得了显著进展，但视频抠像仍具挑战性，原因在于高层级跟踪（需逐帧理解）与低层级抠像（聚焦极细粒度细节）之间固有的鸿沟。现有方法依赖昂贵且范围狭窄的视频抠像数据集，可能限制跨域泛化能力并削弱跟踪鲁棒性。我们通过SAM2Matting重新思考范式，这是一种从跟踪器到抠像的框架，将视觉对象跟踪器（如SAM2、SAM3）提升为高保真视频抠像系统。具体而言，该框架通过引入区域提案桥接模块和专用抠像头，将基础跟踪器与抠像任务解耦：跟踪器保持无损性能以处理时序一致性，抠像组件则解决细粒度细节。值得注意的是，尽管仅在图像上训练，SAM2Matting在视频抠像任务上仍创下新SOTA，支持多样提示类型，保持强时序一致性，并在以人为中心及野外场景中展现出卓越泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between high-level video tracking and low-level matting, SAM2Matting introduces a tracker-to-matting framework that enhances existing VOS trackers like SAM2 or SAM3 with region-proposal bridges and dedicated matting heads, enabling temporal consistency from tracking while resolving fine-grained details via matting components. Trained solely on image datasets, it achieves state-of-the-art video matting performance, supports diverse prompts, and shows strong generalization across human-centric and in-the-wild scenarios without requiring video-specific training data.
为弥合视频跟踪与低层抠像之间的鸿沟，SAM2Matting提出一种跟踪器到抠像框架，通过为现有VOS跟踪器（如SAM2或SAM3）增加区域提议桥接与专用抠像头，实现跟踪的时序一致性与抠像细节的协同处理。仅在图像数据上训练，该方法在视频抠像任务中达到最先进性能，支持多样提示，保持强时序一致性，并在以人为中心与野外场景中展现出鲁棒泛化能力。
Language-Based Digital Twins for Elderly Cognitive Assistance
Authors: Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini, Mohammad H. Mahoor, Hiroko H. Dodge
First: 2026-06-25T17:45:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:45:53+00:00
Comments: Accepted and published in the Proceedings of the ACM International Conference on PErvasive Technologies Related to Assistive Environments (PETRA 2026). The final published version is available through the ACM Digital Library
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Digital twins have emerged as a promising paradigm for personalized healthcare, enabling modeling of individual behavior and health trajectories. In cognitive health, early detection of Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) remains challenging, where language and conversational patterns serve as non-invasive biomarkers. In this work, we propose a language-based digital twin framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to mimic the conversational behavior of elderly individuals by incorporating stylometric cues and contextual metadata. To evaluate fidelity and cognitive consistency, we introduce a multi-head conditional variational autoencoder (cVAE) that jointly measures reconstruction quality and predicts cognitive scores. Experiments on the I-CONECT dataset show that the digital twin preserves identity-specific characteristics and achieves reconstruction and MoCA prediction errors comparable to real data, while outperforming baseline GPT-generated responses. These results highlight the potential of language-based digital twins as a scalable and non-invasive approach for personalized and continuous cognitive health monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于语言的数字孪生用于老年人认知辅助
数字孪生已成为个性化医疗的有前景范式，能够建模个体行为与健康轨迹。在认知健康领域，轻度认知障碍（MCI）的早期检测仍具挑战性，而语言与对话模式可作为无创生物标志物。本文提出一种基于语言的数字孪生框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）结合文体特征与上下文元数据，模拟老年人对话行为。为评估保真度与认知一致性，我们引入多头条件变分自编码器（cVAE），联合衡量重建质量与预测认知评分。在I-CONECT数据集上的实验表明，该数字孪生能保留个体特征，重建与MoCA预测误差与真实数据相当，且优于基线GPT生成响应。结果凸显了基于语言的数字孪生作为可扩展、无创个性化持续认知健康监测方法的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for non-invasive, scalable tools to detect early-stage Mild Cognitive Impairment in elderly individuals, this study proposes a language-based digital twin framework that uses large language models to simulate conversational behavior by integrating stylometric features and contextual metadata. The framework employs a multi-head conditional variational autoencoder to evaluate both reconstruction fidelity and cognitive prediction accuracy. Experimental results on the I-CONECT dataset demonstrate that the digital twin accurately preserves individual conversational identity and achieves reconstruction and MoCA score prediction errors comparable to real human data, while outperforming baseline GPT-generated responses.
为应对老年人认知健康监测中非侵入式、可扩展的需求，该研究提出一种基于语言的数字孪生框架，利用大语言模型结合文体特征与上下文元数据模拟老年人对话行为。框架采用多头条件变分自编码器评估重建保真度与认知评分预测能力。在I-CONECT数据集上的实验表明，该数字孪生能有效保留个体语言特征，重建与MoCA评分预测误差接近真实数据，且优于基线GPT生成结果。
RoPEMover: Depth-Aware Object Relocation via Positional Embeddings
Authors: Ipek Oztas, Duygu Ceylan, Aybars Bugra Aksoy, Aysegul Dundar
First: 2026-06-25T17:45:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:45:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Moving an object in a single image requires geometry-consistent spatial rearrangement, including handling occlusions, revealing previously unseen regions, and maintaining coherent shadows and reflections. Existing approaches are not well suited to this setting and often fail to preserve such scene-level consistency. We address this problem by introducing a geometry-aware object motion method that operates directly on the positional representations of diffusion transformers. Our key insight is that rotary positional embeddings (RoPE) define a structured spatial field that can be explicitly manipulated to induce controlled motion. We extend 2D RoPE into a depth-aware formulation that encodes 3D spatial structure, enabling consistent object displacement and scene-aware updates. Our model is trained using synthetic data combined with a small set of real images via parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Despite minimal real supervision, it preserves object identity under large spatial displacements, generates plausible content in newly revealed regions, and consistently updates scene-dependent effects such as shadows and illumination. Experimental results on standard object motion benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across all evaluation metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoPEMover：通过位置嵌入实现深度感知物体重定位
在单张图像中移动物体需要几何一致的空间重排，包括处理遮挡、揭示先前不可见区域，并保持连贯的阴影与反射。现有方法难以适应此场景，常无法保持场景级一致性。我们通过引入一种几何感知的物体运动方法解决此问题，该方法直接作用于扩散变换器的位置表示。核心思路是：旋转位置嵌入（RoPE）定义了可显式操控的结构化空间场，从而诱导可控运动。我们将2D RoPE扩展为深度感知形式，编码3D空间结构，实现物体一致位移与场景感知更新。模型通过合成数据与少量真实图像结合参数高效微调训练。尽管真实监督极少，仍能在大范围空间位移下保持物体身份，生成新揭示区域的合理内容，并一致更新依赖场景的效果（如阴影与光照）。在标准物体运动基准测试中，实验结果在所有评估指标上均达到最先进水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of achieving geometry-consistent object relocation in single images while preserving scene-level coherence, this work introduces RoPEMover, a method that manipulates positional embeddings in diffusion transformers to induce controlled object motion. By extending 2D rotary positional embeddings into a depth-aware formulation, the model encodes 3D spatial structure to enable consistent displacement and scene-aware updates like shadows and reflections. Trained on synthetic data with minimal real supervision via parameter-efficient fine-tuning, RoPEMover successfully maintains object identity under large displacements, generates plausible content in occluded regions, and preserves illumination consistency, achieving state-of-the-art performance across standard object motion benchmarks.
为解决单图像中物体移动时保持几何一致性与场景连贯性（如遮挡、阴影和光照）的难题，本文提出RoPEMover方法，通过直接操作扩散Transformer中的旋转位置嵌入（RoPE）实现深度感知的物体位移。该方法将二维RoPE扩展为编码三维空间结构的深度感知形式，从而引导可控的物体移动并同步更新场景元素。模型在合成数据基础上，结合少量真实图像通过参数高效微调训练，即使监督数据极少，仍能保持物体身份、生成合理的新区域内容，并持续更新阴影与光照。实验表明其在多个标准评估指标上达到当前最优性能。
Empowering GUI Agents via Autonomous Experience Exploration and Hindsight Experience Utilization for Task Planning
Authors: Tianyi Men, Zhuoran Jin, Pengfei Cao, Yubo Chen, Kang Liu, Jun Zhao
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-06-25T17:44:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:44:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal web agents can assist humans in operating repetitive GUI tasks, where effective task planning is essential for decomposing complex tasks into executable actions. While small open source MLLMs are cost efficient and privacy preserving compared with commercial large models, they suffer from weak planning and limited cross website generalization. To address these limitations, we introduce the planning experience exploration and utilization (PEEU) method, which autonomously explores environments to discover experiences and utilizes hindsight experience to synthesize strictly aligned, high level training data. To quantitatively analyze the generalization behaviors driving this performance, we propose the task decomposition hierarchical analysis framework (TDHAF) to systematically study compositional generalization across three task granularities: low, middle and high levels. Our analysis reveals that mastering low level atomic skills does not guarantee high level planning competence, while high level task training yields stronger OOD generalization. Experiments on real world benchmarks demonstrate PEEU's superior effectiveness: our 7B model achieves 30.6% accuracy, outperforming the much larger Qwen2.5-VL-32B model. These demonstrate constructing hindsight high level tasks and leveraging experiences is crucial for OOD planning abilities of small MLLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过自主经验探索与事后经验利用赋能GUI代理的任务规划
多模态网络代理可协助人类执行重复的GUI任务，其中有效的任务规划对于将复杂任务分解为可执行动作至关重要。虽然与商业大模型相比，小型开源MLLMs在成本和隐私保护方面更具优势，但其规划能力较弱且跨网站泛化能力有限。为解决这些问题，我们提出了规划经验探索与利用（PEEU）方法，该方法可自主探索环境以发现经验，并利用事后经验合成严格对齐的高层训练数据。为定量分析驱动该性能的泛化行为，我们提出了任务分解层次分析框架（TDHAF），系统研究了低、中、高层三个任务粒度下的组合泛化能力。分析表明，掌握低层原子技能并不能保证高层规划能力，而高层任务训练则能带来更强的OOD泛化能力。在真实世界基准测试中，PEEU展现出卓越效果：我们的7B模型准确率达30.6%，优于规模更大的Qwen2.5-VL-32B模型。这表明构建事后高层任务并利用经验对小型MLLMs的OOD规划能力至关重要。
Summary / 总结
To enhance small multimodal language models' task planning for GUI automation, the paper introduces PEEU, a method that autonomously explores environments to gather experiences and then leverages hindsight to synthesize high-level, aligned training data. Using a task decomposition hierarchical analysis framework, the study reveals that low-level skill mastery alone is insufficient for high-level planning, while high-level task training improves out-of-distribution generalization. Experiments show that the 7B PEEU model achieves 30.6% accuracy, surpassing the larger Qwen2.5-VL-32B model, demonstrating the critical role of experience-driven high-level task synthesis for small models' planning capabilities.
为提升小型多模态语言模型在跨网站GUI任务中的规划能力，本文提出PEEU方法，通过自主探索环境收集经验，并利用事后经验合成高阶对齐训练数据。通过分层分析框架发现，掌握低阶原子操作并不能保证高阶规划能力，而高阶任务训练能显著提升分布外泛化能力。实验表明，7B模型准确率达30.6%，超越更大规模的Qwen2.5-VL-32B模型，证明经验驱动的高阶任务合成对小型模型规划能力至关重要。
Bellman-sufficient Information Complexity
Authors: Yunbei Xu
First: 2026-06-09T17:49:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:42:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We develop Bellman-sufficient information complexity, a formal representation-level framework for sequential decision making. The primitive benchmark is a fixed-truth environment space $Ω$ with unrestricted nonanticipating algorithms. The intrinsic object is a Bellman-sufficient state representation, serving as an interactive notion of sufficient statistics, together with an information index $Y=χ(Ω)$, often the optimal decision or value object rather than the full environment. On the upper-bound side, learning is organized as a dynamic program on the sufficient state, equipped with a logarithmic information potential for the index. On the lower-bound side, a Bellman-Fano certificate uses the same state representation and information index, but propagates separate Bellman recursions for information gain and ghost mass. The central matching statement is therefore a conditional Bellman information-risk sandwich: when the log-penalized Bellman upper value and the ghost-quantile lower certificate close at the same radius, they certify the same complexity scale. Popular algorithms then appear as tractable certificates or relaxations of this common log-potential Bellman program, rather than as separate notions of information complexity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：贝尔曼充分信息复杂度
我们发展了贝尔曼充分信息复杂度，这是一种面向序列决策的正式表示层面框架。基本基准是一个固定真值环境空间 $Ω$，配备无限制的非预知算法。内在对象是贝尔曼充分状态表示，作为交互式充分统计量的概念，以及一个信息指标 $Y=χ(Ω)$，通常为最优决策或价值对象，而非完整环境。在上界方面，学习被组织为在充分状态上的动态规划，配备对数信息势能。在下界方面，贝尔曼-费诺证书使用相同的状态表示和信息指标，但分别传播信息增益和幽灵质量的贝尔曼递归。因此，核心匹配陈述是一个条件贝尔曼信息风险夹层：当对数惩罚贝尔曼上界值与幽灵分位数下界证书在相同半径上接近时，它们认证相同的复杂度尺度。流行算法随后表现为该共同对数势能贝尔曼程序的可处理证书或松弛，而非独立的信息复杂度概念。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a unified framework to analyze information complexity in sequential decision making, the paper introduces Bellman-sufficient information complexity, formalizing state representations that capture sufficient statistics for decision-making. The method constructs upper and lower bounds via dynamic programming on a Bellman-sufficient state, using a logarithmic information potential and a Bellman-Fano certificate that tracks information gain and ghost mass. Experimental results show that when the upper-bound Bellman value and lower-bound ghost-quantile certificate converge at the same radius, they jointly certify the information complexity, and popular algorithms emerge as relaxations or certificates of this shared log-potential Bellman program.
为统一量化序列决策中的信息复杂度，本文提出贝尔曼充分信息复杂度框架，通过贝尔曼递归形式化能充分表征环境的状态表示。方法构建上下界：上界基于充分状态空间上的动态规划与对数信息势，下界采用贝尔曼-费诺证书，通过独立贝尔曼递归处理信息增益与虚质量。核心结果表明，当对数惩罚贝尔曼上界与虚质量下界在相同半径收敛时，二者共同认证同一复杂度尺度，主流算法由此成为该统一贝尔曼程序的可计算证书或松弛解，验证了框架在统一信息复杂度度量上的有效性。
Hallucination in World Models is Predictable and Preventable
Authors: Nicklas Hansen, Xiaolong Wang
Venue: www
First: 2026-06-25T17:38:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:38:45+00:00
Comments: Interactive paper, live demo, code, dataset, and models: https://www.nicklashansen.com/mmbench2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern generative world models render increasingly realistic action-controllable futures, yet they frequently hallucinate: rollouts remain visually fluent while drifting from the ground-truth dynamics. We hypothesize that hallucination concentrates in low-coverage regions of the state-action space, where lightweight data-centric signals can both detect it and guide mitigation. To test this, we introduce MMBench2, a 427-hour, 210-task dataset for visual world modeling with ground-truth actions, rewards, and live simulators, and train a 350M-parameter world model on it. We identify three distinct hallucination modes: perceptual, action-marginalized, and scene-diverging -- each anchored to a different stage of the pipeline, and develop three signals that accurately predict where the model will fail. To close coverage gaps at training time, we develop a coverage-aware sampling technique; to close them online, our hallucination predictors serve as curiosity rewards for targeted data collection, yielding a data-efficient finetuning recipe that adapts the pretrained world model to entirely unseen environments with as few as 50 real environment trajectories. Overall, our findings reveal that hallucination in world models is inherently a data coverage issue, and that the same signals used to detect it can also be used for mitigation. An interactive web version of our paper is available at https://www.nicklashansen.com/mmbench2
中文标题/摘要
标题：世界模型中的幻觉是可预测且可预防的
现代生成式世界模型能渲染越来越逼真且可控制的动作未来，但它们经常产生幻觉：生成轨迹在视觉上流畅，却偏离真实动力学。我们假设幻觉集中在状态-动作空间的低覆盖率区域，轻量级的数据驱动信号既能检测幻觉，也能引导缓解。为此，我们引入MMBench2，一个包含427小时、210个任务的视觉世界建模数据集，提供真实动作、奖励和实时模拟器，并在其上训练了一个3.5亿参数的世界模型。我们识别出三种幻觉模式：感知型、动作边缘化型和场景发散型，每种对应流水线的不同阶段，并开发了三种信号准确预测模型失败位置。为在训练时填补覆盖率缺口，我们设计了覆盖率感知采样技术；为在线填补缺口，幻觉预测器作为好奇心奖励引导定向数据收集，从而获得一种数据高效的微调方案，仅需50条真实环境轨迹即可使预训练世界模型适应完全未见过的环境。总体而言，我们的研究发现表明，世界模型中的幻觉本质上是数据覆盖率问题，且用于检测幻觉的相同信号也可用于缓解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that modern world models hallucinate despite producing visually fluent rollouts, the authors hypothesize that hallucinations arise in low-coverage regions of the state-action space and can be detected and mitigated using lightweight data-centric signals. They introduce MMBench2, a large-scale dataset with ground-truth dynamics, and train a 350M-parameter world model on it. Through analysis, they identify three hallucination modes—perceptual, action-marginalized, and scene-diverging—and develop corresponding predictive signals. To address coverage gaps, they implement coverage-aware sampling during training and use hallucination predictors as curiosity rewards for online data collection, enabling efficient fine-tuning with as few as 50 real trajectories in unseen environments. Their results demonstrate that hallucinations are fundamentally tied to data coverage and that the same signals used for detection can effectively guide mitigation.
鉴于现代世界模型虽能生成视觉流畅的预测但常出现幻觉，作者假设幻觉集中于状态-动作空间的低覆盖区域，可通过轻量级数据驱动信号进行检测与缓解。他们构建了包含真实动态的MMBench2数据集并训练3.5亿参数的世界模型，识别出三种幻觉模式——感知型、动作边缘型和场景偏离型，并为每种模式设计了预测信号。为填补覆盖空缺，他们在训练中采用覆盖感知采样策略，并将幻觉预测器作为好奇心奖励引导在线数据收集，实现仅需50条真实环境轨迹即可高效微调模型适应全新环境。实验表明，幻觉本质源于数据覆盖不足，且用于检测的信号同样可有效用于缓解。
Not All Actions Are Equal: Rethinking Conditioning for Dexterous World Model
Authors: Zizhao Yuan, Zhengtu Liang, Taowen Wang, Qiwei Liang, Yichi Wang, Yunheng Wang, Yuetong Fang, Lusong Li, Zecui Zeng, Renjing Xu
First: 2026-06-25T17:36:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:36:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in action-conditioned world models show promising progress in modeling complex interactions and forecasting future states under diverse action sequences. While these models are often driven by stronger visual representations and model capacity, action conditioning itself remains underexplored. Most existing approaches compress the entire action sequence into a single representation, which works well for low-DoF control but becomes less reliable in high-DoF scenarios. We observe that high-DoF dexterous actions are inherently heterogeneous, spanning multiple orders of magnitude, where large-scale motions coexist with subtle but important signals. When uniformly aggregated, optimization exhibits an imbalance across action components, which hinders the modeling of fine-grained effects and affects action fidelity. We therefore propose DexAC-WM, which treats action conditioning as a structured process rather than global compression. DexAC preserves dimension-level semantics via action tokenization and aligns action signals with visual dynamics through local refinement and global modulation. To address the limited high-level semantic grounding in existing world models, we further introduce a semantic branch that provides rich object-scene priors, which enables world model to capture dynamic visual details while supporting high-DoF action-conditioned video prediction. Experiments on EgoDex and EgoVerse show that combining the semantic branch with DexAC significantly improves FID, FVD, and PCK, demonstrating gains in visual-temporal realism and action-following consistency. We further verify that DexAC extends to other backbones, showing the scalability of our structured action-conditioning design. These results suggest that scaling world models to high-DoF control requires both structured action modeling and semantic grounding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：并非所有动作都等价：重新思考灵巧世界模型中的条件化
近期在动作条件化世界模型方面的进展在建模复杂交互和预测多样化动作序列下的未来状态方面展现出良好前景。尽管这些模型常依赖更强的视觉表征和模型容量，但动作条件化本身仍被忽视。现有方法大多将整个动作序列压缩为单一表示，这在低自由度控制中表现良好，但在高自由度场景下可靠性下降。我们观察到，高自由度灵巧动作本质上具有异质性，跨越多个数量级，大尺度运动与细微但重要的信号并存。当均匀聚合时，优化在动作组件间表现出不平衡，阻碍了细粒度效应的建模并影响动作保真度。因此，我们提出DexAC-WM，将动作条件化视为结构化过程而非全局压缩。DexAC通过动作分词保留维度级语义，并通过局部细化与全局调制将动作信号与视觉动态对齐。为解决现有世界模型中高层语义锚定不足的问题，我们进一步引入语义分支，提供丰富的物体-场景先验，使世界模型在捕捉动态视觉细节的同时支持高自由度动作条件化视频预测。在EgoDex和EgoVerse上的实验表明，结合语义分支与DexAC显著提升了FID、FVD和PCK，证明了视觉-时序真实性和动作跟随一致性提升。我们进一步验证DexAC可扩展至其他骨干网络，表明我们的结构化动作条件化设计具有可扩展性。这些结果表明，将世界模型扩展至高自由度控制需要结构化动作建模与语义锚定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that uniform action compression fails to capture the heterogeneous nature of high-DoF dexterous actions, this work proposes DexAC-WM, a structured action-conditioning method that tokenizes actions at the dimension level and aligns them with visual dynamics via local refinement and global modulation. It further introduces a semantic branch to ground actions in object-scene priors, enhancing visual-temporal realism and action fidelity. Experiments on EgoDex and EgoVerse show that DexAC-WM significantly improves FID, FVD, and PCK compared to baseline methods, and its design is scalable across different world model backbones.
鉴于统一压缩动作序列难以处理高自由度灵巧动作的异质性，作者提出DexAC-WM方法，通过动作维度级分词与局部精调和全局调制对齐视觉动态。为增强语义理解，引入语义分支注入物体场景先验，提升视觉-时间建模能力。在EgoDex和EgoVerse上的实验表明，结合语义分支后FID、FVD和PCK显著提升，视觉真实性和动作一致性增强，且该方法可扩展至不同骨干网络。
Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders
Authors: Nathanaël Jacquier, Maria Vakalopoulou, Mahdi S. Hosseini
First: 2026-06-25T17:34:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:34:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer, despite retaining limitations of its own, such as a budget $k$ that is fixed regardless of input complexity and a tendency to overfit to the training value of $k$. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越硬预算：用于更可解释的Top-k稀疏自编码器的稀疏正则化器
稀疏自编码器（SAEs）已成为解释视觉基础模型表示的主要工具，将它们的多语义激活分解为一组更稀疏、更单语义的特征。Top-k SAE作为当前标准变体，通过其激活函数在架构上强制稀疏性，每个输入仅保留最活跃的k个潜在变量。由于其设计初衷是避免早期SAEs使用的ℓ1惩罚及其已知缺陷，尽管自身仍存在局限（如预算k固定不随输入复杂度变化，且易过度拟合训练时的k值），它尚未与显式稀疏正则化器结合。我们引入两种与Top-k架构兼容的稀疏正则化器，均作用于Top-k选择前的激活值：对未被选中的（非支撑）单元施加ℓ1惩罚，以及一种尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比值惩罚，使编码集中在更少的有效单元上。两种惩罚仅作用于批处理中至少被Top-k操作选中的活跃单元。在两个数据集、三个视觉基础模型及多种k值下，两种正则化器均一致提升单语义性，且不损害重建质量。ℓ1/ℓ2惩罚进一步将信息集中于更少潜在变量，使重建对推理时k值选择更鲁棒，并提升小预算线性探测性能。我们的核心发现是：硬架构稀疏性与软稀疏正则化是互补而非互斥的。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of fixed-budget Top-k sparse autoencoders, such as overfitting to training k-values and lack of interpretability, this work introduces two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-k architecture: an ℓ1 penalty on unselected units and a scale-invariant ℓ1/ℓ2-ratio penalty applied only to batch-active units. Both regularizers enhance monosemanticity without degrading reconstruction quality across multiple vision models and datasets. The ℓ1/ℓ2 penalty further concentrates representation into fewer latents, improving robustness to inference-time k-selection and boosting small-budget linear probing performance, demonstrating that hard architectural sparsity and soft regularization are complementary.
为解决Top-k稀疏自编码器固定预算、易过拟合的问题，本文提出两种与Top-k架构兼容的稀疏正则化方法：对未被选中的激活单元施加$\ell_1$惩罚，以及对激活单元施加尺度不变的$\ell_1/\ell_2$比值惩罚，以集中表征。仅对批次内被选中的单元应用惩罚，两种方法在多个视觉模型和数据集上均提升单义性，且不损害重建质量；其中$\ell_1/\ell_2$惩罚更增强对推理时k值选择的鲁棒性，并提升小预算线性探测性能，表明硬架构稀疏性与软正则化可协同互补。
OctoSense: Self-Supervised Learning for Multimodal Robot Perception
Authors: Anthony Bisulco, Jeremy Wang, Kostas Daniilidis, Randall Balestriero, Pratik Chaudhari
First: 2026-06-25T17:30:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:30:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present OctoSense, an open-source sensor platform with stereo RGB and event cameras, LiDAR, a thermal camera, an inertial measurement unit, RTK-corrected global positioning system, and proprioception (CAN bus data from a car, and joint angles for a quadruped robot). The eponymous OctoSense dataset contains 59 hours of time-synchronized driving data across different types of environments at different times of the day, including situations with highly degraded sensors. We demonstrate multi-modal self-supervised learning using such real-world robotics data, where sensors have different representations, frequencies, latencies and noise. Our approach, a "late-fusion" masked autoencoder, (i) uses modality-specific tokenizers to account for different spatiotemporal characteristics of these sensors, and (ii) caches modality-specific tokens at inference time to process new measurements as they come. This architecture (i) is fast (6.68 ms and 112 ms on NVIDIA 5090 and Orin NX respectively, to compute the representation), (ii) performs better than existing image-only foundation models on tasks such as estimation of optical flow, depth, semantic segmentation, and ego-motion (translation, rotation, and steering angle), and (iii) predicts robustly at nighttime or in situations where sensory data is degraded. See our project page for links to the dataset, code, and supplementary videos: https://abisulco.com/octosense/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OctoSense：面向多模态机器人感知的自监督学习
我们提出了OctoSense，一个开源传感器平台，配备立体RGB相机和事件相机、激光雷达、热成像相机、惯性测量单元、RTK校正的全球定位系统以及本体感觉（来自汽车的CAN总线数据和四足机器人的关节角度）。同名的OctoSense数据集包含59小时在不同环境和不同时段采集的同步驾驶数据，包括传感器严重退化的情况。我们利用此类真实机器人数据，展示了多模态自监督学习，其中传感器具有不同的表示、频率、延迟和噪声。我们的方法是一种“晚期融合”掩码自编码器，(i) 使用模态特定分词器以适应这些传感器不同的时空特性，(ii) 在推理时缓存模态特定分词，以便实时处理新测量数据。该架构(i) 运行速度快（在NVIDIA 5090和Orin NX上分别耗时6.68毫秒和112毫秒计算表示），(ii) 在光流估计、深度估计、语义分割和自运动估计（平移、旋转和转向角）等任务上优于现有仅图像基础模型，(iii) 在夜间或传感器数据退化情况下仍能稳健预测。请访问我们的项目页面获取数据集、代码和补充视频链接：https://abisulco.com/octosense/。
Summary / 总结

The method employs a late-fusion masked autoencoder with modality-specific tokenizers to handle varying spatiotemporal properties of inputs, and caches tokens at inference for real-time processing. Experiments show the model achieves faster inference (6.68 ms on NVIDIA 5090) and outperforms image-only models on tasks like optical flow, depth, semantic segmentation, and ego-motion, while maintaining robustness during nighttime or sensor-degraded scenarios.
为提升机器人在复杂且传感器数据退化环境下对现实世界的感知能力，OctoSense提出一种多模态自监督学习框架，整合了立体RGB、事件相机、激光雷达、热成像、惯性测量单元、RTK-GPS及本体感觉等传感器。该方法采用“晚期融合”掩码自编码器，通过模态特定分词器处理不同时空特性的输入，并在推理时缓存模态令牌以支持实时处理。实验基于59小时同步驾驶数据，结果表明该模型在光流、深度、语义分割和自运动估计等任务上优于仅图像模型，且在夜间或传感器退化场景下仍保持稳定性能，推理速度在NVIDIA 5090和Orin NX上分别达6.68毫秒和112毫秒。
LLM-Based Examination of Eligibility Criteria from Securities Prospectuses at the German Central Bank
Authors: Serhii Hamotskyi, Akash Kumar Gautam, Christian Hänig
Venue: Proceedings of the 7th Financial Narrative Processing Workshop (FNP 2026) at LREC 2026, pp. 1-10, 2026
First: 2026-06-25T17:29:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:29:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Verifying the eligibility of securities as collateral is a key responsibility of the German Central Bank. However, manually verifying these assets against legal and financial criteria within lengthy, semi-structured, and often bilingual prospectuses is a resource-intensive task. While previous efforts utilized traditional Named Entity Recognition (NER) for information extraction, these methods can struggle with OCR noise, linguistic variance, and rigid span-based constraints, and the need for manually annotated training data for each relevant annotation type. In this paper, we present the first case study applying Large Language Models (LLMs) to the eligibility examination process, shifting the paradigm toward a generative Information Extraction pipeline. Our approach decomposes the task into extraction, normalization, and interpretation, allowing for greater flexibility in handling noisy text and interleaved German-English content. We further introduce a value-based evaluation methodology using LLM-as-a-judge, which offers a more semantic assessment than location-based metrics. Our results demonstrate that LLM-based systems achieve high precision (up to 91%) in document-level eligibility, exhibiting a conservative operating profile that minimizes false acceptance.
中文标题/摘要

Motivated by the need to automate the verification of securities eligibility for collateral at the German Central Bank, this study introduces a novel LLM-based pipeline that replaces traditional NER methods, which struggle with OCR noise, linguistic variance, and rigid annotation constraints.
