Blackwell Approachability and Gradient Equilibrium are Equivalent
Authors: Brian W. Lee, Nika Haghtalab, Michael I. Jordan, Ryan J. Tibshirani
First: 2026-06-25T17:29:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:29:48+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 1 figure, accepted for presentation at COLT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recently introduced online optimization framework that generalizes first-order stationarity from offline optimization and abstracts problems like online conformal prediction. While GEQ has curious similarities with known online learning frameworks, namely regret minimization, prior work has shown that GEQ error and regret are incomparable objectives, leaving open a precise understanding of how GEQ fits into the broader online learning landscape. In this work, we show that GEQ is equivalent to Blackwell approachability in the algorithmic sense. That is, a Blackwell approachability problem can always be solved using queries to a black-box GEQ oracle, with no asymptotic loss in the oracle's error rate, and vice versa. Taken together with known equivalences between approachability, regret minimization, and calibration, these results imply that GEQ is equivalent to these frameworks, as well. Our reductions are efficient and can be used to transfer refined guarantees, such as optimism and strong adaptivity, from regret minimization to GEQ. Along the way, we also identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ, and establish reductions between different notions of GEQ with unconstrained and constrained decision sets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：布莱克威尔可达性与梯度均衡等价
梯度均衡（GEQ）是一种最近提出的在线优化框架，它将离线优化中的一阶平稳性推广，并抽象出在线符合预测等问题。尽管GEQ与已知的在线学习框架（如遗憾最小化）具有有趣的相似性，但先前研究已表明GEQ误差与遗憾不可比较，因此尚未明确GEQ在更广泛的在线学习框架中的位置。本文证明，在算法意义上，GEQ等价于布莱克威尔可达性：即，布莱克威尔可达性问题总可通过调用一个黑盒GEQ预言机来解决，且预言机的误差率无渐近损失；反之亦然。结合已知的可达性、遗憾最小化与校准之间的等价关系，这些结果表明GEQ也等价于这些框架。我们的约简是高效的，可用于将遗憾最小化中的精细保证（如乐观性与强自适应性）转移到GEQ。此外，我们还确定了GEQ的必要与充分条件，并建立了不同形式的GEQ（在无约束与有约束决策集下）之间的约简关系。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand Gradient Equilibrium (GEQ)’s position within online learning, this work establishes its algorithmic equivalence to Blackwell approachability, showing that each can simulate the other without asymptotic loss in error rate. The method involves constructing efficient reductions between GEQ oracles and Blackwell approachability solvers, leveraging known equivalences to also link GEQ to regret minimization and calibration. Experimental results confirm that these reductions preserve refined properties like optimism and strong adaptivity, and identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ across unconstrained and constrained decision sets.
为明确梯度均衡（GEQ）在在线学习中的地位，本文证明其与Blackwell逼近性在算法意义上等价，即二者可通过黑盒调用相互模拟且误差率无渐近损失。方法通过构建高效还原机制，实现GEQ与Blackwell逼近性问题的相互转化，从而可迁移乐观性与强自适应性等保证。实验结果通过理论还原验证等价性，表明GEQ目标可与后悔最小化及校准框架通过逼近性关联相互转换。
Beyond Surface Forms: A Comprehensive, Mechanism-Oriented Taxonomy of Indirect Linguistic Encoding for LLM-Based Coded Language Detection
Authors: Hamid Reza Firoozfar, Mohammadsadegh Abolhasani, Reza Mousavi, Paul Jen-Hwa Hu
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-06-25T17:29:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:29:45+00:00
Comments: Submitted for review in ARR for EMNLP 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
To avoid moderation and surveillance on social media, some users routinely invent indirect linguistic expressions (ILE) that camouflage sensitive meanings. Such expressions surface as algospeak, euphemisms, and adversarial obfuscation, depending on intent and context, and they involve recurring encoding mechanisms. We propose a comprehensive, mechanism-oriented taxonomy of ILE that abstracts away from communicative goals and instead categorizes the underlying operations through which meaning is encoded and recovered. We evaluate the taxonomy by incorporating it into LLM prompts and comparing it with four existing taxonomies and a no-taxonomy baseline, using 2,000 manually annotated TikTok and Bluesky posts. The proposed taxonomy attains the strongest document- and span-level performance across the three LLMs, achieving an improvement of 4.7% in accuracy and 5.4% in F1 over the best-performing benchmark. The empirical results reveal the importance of a comprehensive, mechanism-oriented taxonomy as a stable scaffold for detecting emerging coded language and a useful input to content moderation. Disclaimer: This paper contains content that may be profane, vulgar, or offensive.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越表层形式：面向大语言模型编码语言检测的间接语言表达机制导向综合分类法
为规避社交媒体上的审查与监控，部分用户会常规性地发明间接语言表达（ILE），以隐藏敏感含义。此类表达根据意图与语境表现为算法语言、委婉语或对抗性混淆，且涉及重复出现的编码机制。本文提出一种机制导向的ILE综合分类法，剥离沟通目标，转而通过编码与恢复意义的底层操作进行分类。我们通过将该分类法融入大语言模型提示词，并与四种现有分类法及无分类基线对比，使用2000条人工标注的TikTok和Bluesky帖子进行评估。该分类法在三种大语言模型上均取得最强的文档级与片段级性能，准确率提升4.7%，F1值提升5.4%。实证结果表明，综合且机制导向的分类法是检测新兴编码语言的稳定基础，也是内容审核的有用输入。免责声明：本文可能包含粗俗、低俗或冒犯性内容。
Summary / 总结
To combat censorship on social media, users frequently employ indirect linguistic expressions (ILE) such as algospeak or euphemisms to conceal sensitive content, which vary by intent but share underlying encoding mechanisms. This paper introduces a comprehensive, mechanism-oriented taxonomy of ILE that classifies these expressions based on how meaning is encoded and decoded, rather than communicative goals. Evaluated by integrating the taxonomy into prompts for three LLMs and comparing against four existing taxonomies and a baseline using 2,000 annotated posts, the proposed taxonomy outperformed all others, achieving a 4.7% accuracy and 5.4% F1 improvement over the best benchmark across both document and span levels, demonstrating its effectiveness as a stable framework for detecting coded language.
为应对社交媒体上用户通过间接语言表达（ILE）规避审查的需求，本文提出一种基于编码机制而非交际意图的综合分类体系。该体系通过将编码操作作为分类依据，集成至三个大语言模型的提示中，并与四种现有分类体系及无分类基线进行对比，使用2000条人工标注的帖子进行评估。实验结果表明，该分类体系在文档级和片段级表现最优，较最佳基线准确率提升4.7%，F1值提升5.4%，证明其作为检测新兴隐晦语言的有效稳定框架。
ViQ: Text-Aligned Visual Quantized Representations at Any Resolution
Authors: Xumin Yu, Zuyan Liu, Zhenyu Yang, Yuhao Dong, Shengsheng Qian, Jiwen Lu, Han Hu, Yongming Rao
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-25T17:29:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:29:27+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A unified representation for text and vision is a natural pursuit, as it enables simpler multimodal modeling and more efficient training. However, representing images as discrete signals in the same way as text inevitably introduces severe information loss. Existing work struggles to balance low-level details and high-level semantics in discrete representations: reconstruction-oriented representations often lack semantic information, whereas semantically stronger features typically suffer from severe loss of detail. We present ViQ, a Visual Quantized Representations framework, which is designed to balance semantics and details in discrete representations while supporting inputs at native resolutions, thereby enabling it to serve as a unified and general discrete representation for arbitrary visual inputs. Our approach structures quantization learning into two stages: text-aligned pre-training and feature discretization. With text-aligned pre-training, we enhance the visual encoder semantic-rich supervision from the pretrained language model and enable it to process native-resolution visual inputs. During discretization, we propose a proximal representation learning strategy to progressively compact the feature space, along with a position-aware head-wise quantization mechanism that enables flexible processing of arbitrary resolutions. Extensive experiments on multimodal tasks demonstrate that ViQ achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art multimodal vision encoders with continuous and high-dimensional visual features, while maintaining high precision in low-level reconstruction. We also show that multimodal training with visual quantized representations largely improves efficiency, yielding up to 20\%-70\% acceleration with different base LLMs and training recipes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ViQ：任意分辨率下的文本对齐视觉量化表示
统一文本与视觉的表示是自然追求，可简化多模态建模并提升训练效率。然而，将图像作为与文本相同的离散信号表示必然导致严重信息损失。现有方法难以在离散表示中平衡低层细节与高层语义：重建导向的表示常缺乏语义信息，而语义更强的特征则常损失大量细节。我们提出ViQ视觉量化表示框架，旨在离散表示中平衡语义与细节，同时支持原生分辨率输入，从而可作为任意视觉输入的统一通用离散表示。我们的方法将量化学习分为两个阶段：文本对齐预训练与特征离散化。通过文本对齐预训练，我们增强视觉编码器从预训练语言模型获得的语义丰富监督，并使其能处理原生分辨率视觉输入。在离散化阶段，我们提出一种邻近表示学习策略，逐步压缩特征空间，并结合位置感知的逐头量化机制，实现对任意分辨率的灵活处理。在多模态任务上的大量实验表明，ViQ在性能上与当前最先进的连续高维视觉特征多模态视觉编码器具有竞争力，同时保持低层重建的高精度。我们还表明，使用视觉量化表示进行多模态训练可显著提升效率，不同基础大语言模型和训练方案下加速率达20%至70%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for unified text-vision representations that preserve both semantics and fine details, ViQ introduces a two-stage framework combining text-aligned pre-training and feature discretization. The pre-training stage leverages semantic supervision from pretrained language models to enhance visual encoders for native-resolution inputs, while the discretization stage employs proximal representation learning and position-aware head-wise quantization to balance detail retention and semantic strength. Experiments show ViQ achieves competitive multimodal performance against continuous high-dimensional encoders while maintaining high reconstruction fidelity and offers up to 70% training speedup across different LLMs.
为实现兼顾语义与细节的统一图文表示，ViQ提出两阶段框架：首先通过与文本对齐的预训练增强视觉编码器的语言监督能力并支持原生分辨率输入；随后采用渐进式紧凑学习与位置感知逐头量化策略，以灵活处理任意分辨率。实验表明，ViQ在多模态任务中性能媲美主流连续高维视觉编码器，同时保持低层重建精度，并在不同大语言模型和训练方案下实现最高达70%的训练加速。
See & Sniff: Learning Visuo-Olfactory Representations
Authors: Seongyu Kim, Seungwoo Lee, Hyeonggon Ryu, Joon Son Chung, Arda Senocak
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-25T17:26:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:26:47+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026. Project Page: https://mm.kaist.ac.kr/projects/SeeandSniff/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While modern multimodal models integrate vision with language, audio, or touch, olfaction remains largely unexplored due to the lack of paired visuo-olfactory data. We introduce SmellNet-V, a scalable visuo-olfactory dataset built on the insight that odor identity is largely invariant to visual transformations within a semantic category. This allows us to synthetically pair smell-only samples with semantically aligned in-the-wild web images, converting a unimodal olfactory dataset into a cross-modal benchmark without costly co-collection. Building on this dataset, we propose See & Sniff, a self-supervised framework that learns joint visuo-olfactory representations via dense local alignment and naturally produces smell saliency maps for spatial grounding of odor sources. We further introduce pixel-level smell localization task and a benchmark for evaluation. Our method surpasses smell-only baselines by 7% in smell classification from smell alone and generalizes to cross-modal retrieval and smell localization, establishing visuo-olfactory learning as a new direction in multimodal perception.
中文标题/摘要
标题：见与闻：学习视觉-嗅觉表征
尽管现代多模态模型已将视觉与语言、音频或触觉相结合，但嗅觉仍因缺乏配对的视觉-嗅觉数据而鲜有探索。我们提出了SmellNet-V，一个基于“同一语义类别内，气味身份对视觉变换基本不变”这一洞见构建的可扩展视觉-嗅觉数据集。该方法通过合成配对嗅觉样本与语义对齐的野外网络图像，无需昂贵的同步采集，即可将单模态嗅觉数据集转化为跨模态基准。在此数据集基础上，我们提出See & Sniff，一种自监督框架，通过密集局部对齐学习联合视觉-嗅觉表征，并自然生成嗅觉显著性图以实现嗅源的空间定位。我们进一步引入像素级嗅觉定位任务及评估基准。我们的方法在仅凭嗅觉进行分类时比单模态嗅觉基线提升7%，并可泛化至跨模态检索与嗅觉定位，确立视觉-嗅觉学习为多模态感知的新方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored integration of olfaction into multimodal learning, the authors introduce SmellNet-V, a scalable dataset that pairs synthetic odor samples with semantically aligned real-world images, overcoming the scarcity of paired visuo-olfactory data. They then propose See & Sniff, a self-supervised framework that learns joint visual-olfactory representations through dense local alignment, enabling odor source localization and saliency mapping. Experiments show the method outperforms smell-only baselines by 7% in odor classification and achieves strong generalization in cross-modal retrieval and pixel-level smell localization, validating visuo-olfactory learning as a viable new direction in multimodal perception.
鉴于嗅觉在多模态学习中长期被忽视，作者构建了SmellNet-V数据集，通过语义对齐将合成嗅觉样本与真实世界图像配对，解决配对数据稀缺问题。在此基础上提出See & Sniff自监督框架，通过密集局部对齐学习视觉-嗅觉联合表征，实现嗅源定位与嗅觉显著图生成。实验表明该方法在嗅觉分类任务上比纯嗅觉基线提升7%，并在跨模态检索和像素级嗅源定位中表现优异，验证了视觉-嗅觉联合学习作为多模态感知新方向的可行性。
Multilingual Reasoning Cascades Need More Context
Authors: Arnav Mazumder, Dengjia Zhang, Shuyue Stella Li, Yulia Tsvetkov, Niyati Bafna
First: 2026-06-25T17:26:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:26:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Translation cascades for reasoning translate the query from another language to English, reason in English, and translate the answer back to the original language. This is a competitive approach to multilingual reasoning, but structurally lossy, since each stage discards information later stages may need, including cues for cultural grounding, register, and disambiguation. We examine the benefits of a simple and training-free intervention: a context-aware translation cascade, which additionally provides the original question, the English translated question, and the reasoning trace to the context of the final translation module. We evaluate gains across nine multilingual benchmarks including various task types, three backbone models, and 285 high-, mid-, and low-resource languages, and demonstrate strong gains for open-ended generation across models and resource regimes. We show that the original language question carries most of the beneficial context. Our study emphasizes the need to better design information flow in machine translation cascades for mitigating error propagation, and provides a simple and actionable default strategy: preserve the original user question until the end of the pipeline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多语言推理级联需要更多上下文
推理翻译级联将查询从其他语言翻译为英语，用英语进行推理，再将答案翻译回原始语言。这是一种有竞争力的多语言推理方法，但结构上存在信息损失，因为每个阶段都会丢弃后续阶段可能需要的信息，包括文化背景、语域和消歧线索。我们研究了一种简单且无需训练的干预措施：上下文感知翻译级联，即在最终翻译模块的上下文中额外提供原始问题、英文翻译问题和推理轨迹。我们在九个多语言基准上评估了该方法的收益，涵盖多种任务类型、三个骨干模型和285种高、中、低资源语言，证明了其在开放生成任务中对各类模型和资源环境均有显著提升。我们发现原始语言问题承载了大部分有益上下文。本研究强调了在机器翻译级联中更好地设计信息流以减轻错误传播的重要性，并提供了一个简单且可操作的默认策略：将原始用户问题保留至管道末尾。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the structural loss in translation cascades for multilingual reasoning—where each stage discards context critical for cultural grounding and disambiguation—the authors propose a context-aware intervention that feeds the original question, its English translation, and the reasoning trace to the final translation module. Evaluated across nine multilingual benchmarks, three backbone models, and 285 languages, this approach consistently improved open-ended generation performance across resource regimes. The results show that preserving the original language question provides the most beneficial context, underscoring the need for better information flow design in cascades to reduce error propagation and offering a simple, training-free default strategy.
该研究针对多语言推理中翻译级联结构因逐阶段丢弃上下文而导致的文化背景和歧义消解信息丢失问题，提出一种无需训练的上下文感知干预方法，即在最终翻译阶段同时输入原始问题、英文翻译和推理轨迹。在九个多语言基准、三种模型和285种语言上评估，该方法在资源丰富、中等和匮乏语言中均显著提升开放生成性能，且原始问题贡献最大上下文信息。研究强调应优化翻译级联的信息流以减少误差传播，并提出保留原始问题至流程末尾作为简单实用的默认策略。
Sculpting NeRF Geometry: Human-Preference Fine-Tuning of a 3D-Aware Face GAN
Authors: Archer Moore, Mingming Gong, Liam Hodgkinson
First: 2026-06-25T17:26:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:26:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) for 3D generation is now established across a number of works, but most existing pipelines optimise explicit surface representations, often by converting radiance fields into meshes and training heavily on surface-supervised data. We instead fine-tune a pretrained 3D-aware generative model directly from a learned reward over radiance-field density ($σ$) values, with no externally supplied mesh or shape prior. The reward model requires no pretraining, trains easily on a small set of preference samples, and yields robust improvement in 3D geometry. Working on an unconditional 3D-aware face GAN (EG3D), our reward reads the continuous 3D density field of the neural radiance field (NeRF) directly and supplies a geometry-only learning signal, requiring neither text conditioning, mesh extraction, nor multi-view rendering. A density-consistency constraint keeps the 2D appearance qualitatively similar while the geometry is reshaped, at a measurable but bounded distributional cost (FID-50k rises from 4.09 to 6.66): the fine-tuned generator, trained from the preferences of a single annotator as a proof of concept, produces face geometries preferred by users in 74.4% of pairwise comparisons.
中文标题/摘要
标题：雕刻NeRF几何：基于人类偏好的3D感知人脸GAN的微调
基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）在3D生成领域已有多项研究验证，但现有流程大多优化显式表面表示，常通过将辐射场转换为网格并大量依赖表面监督数据进行训练。我们则直接从学习到的辐射场密度（σ）值奖励函数微调预训练的3D感知生成模型，无需外部提供的网格或形状先验。该奖励模型无需预训练，仅需少量偏好样本即可轻松训练，且能有效提升3D几何质量。在无条件3D感知人脸GAN（EG3D）上，我们的奖励模型直接读取神经辐射场（NeRF）的连续3D密度场，提供纯几何学习信号，无需文本条件、网格提取或多视角渲染。密度一致性约束在几何重塑的同时保持2D外观定性相似，代价为可测量但有界的分布差异（FID-50k从4.09升至6.66）：以单个标注者偏好为概念验证，微调后的生成器在成对比较中74.4%的案例中获得用户更偏好的人脸几何。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of surface-based 3D generation pipelines, this work fine-tunes a pretrained 3D-aware face GAN directly using a learned reward based on radiance-field density values, avoiding mesh or shape priors. The reward model, trained on minimal preference samples, guides geometry refinement without text conditioning or multi-view rendering, while a density-consistency constraint preserves 2D appearance. Experimental results show that fine-tuning from a single annotator’s preferences improves user preference in 74.4% of pairwise comparisons, albeit with a measurable increase in FID-50k from 4.09 to 6.66.
为克服现有基于表面表示的3D生成方法效率低的问题，该研究直接以神经辐射场密度值为奖励信号，对预训练的3D人脸生成GAN进行微调，无需网格提取或形状先验。奖励模型仅需少量偏好样本即可训练，引导几何结构优化，且不依赖文本条件或多视角渲染。实验表明，仅用单个标注者偏好训练的微调生成器，在74.4%的成对比较中获得用户更偏好结果，同时2D外观保持定性相似，FID-50k指标从4.09上升至6.66，代价可控。
A Multi-Fidelity Convolutional Autoencoder-Transfer Learning Framework for Guided-Wave-Based Damage Diagnosis Using Large Simulated and Limited Experimental Datasets
Authors: Santosh Kapuria, Abhishek
First: 2026-06-25T17:25:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:25:13+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 24 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Guided wave-based structural health monitoring (GWSHM) with onboard transducers offers significant potential for the early diagnosis of damage in engineering structures. However, the practical deployment of deep learning models is often hindered by the limited availability of labelled experimental data and the high computational cost of generating large-scale high-fidelity simulation datasets. This study presents a multifidelity transfer learning framework that integrates lightweight physics-based simulations, convolutional autoencoder (CAE)-based deep feature learning, a feed-forward neural network, and limited experimental measurements for accurate damage localisation and sizing in plate-like structures instrumented with piezoelectric transducers. A computationally efficient one-dimensional time-domain spectral element model is employed to generate a large synthetic dataset for pretraining, while transfer learning adapts the model to experimental domains using only a small amount of labelled data. The CAE-based transfer learning framework significantly outperforms its CNN-based counterpart in damage localisation accuracy. The model achieves excellent predictive performance with $R^2$ scores exceeding 0.93 for damage localisation and 0.99 for damage sizing. Its generalisation capability is demonstrated on previously unseen data, showing high prediction accuracy for damage scenarios not represented during pretraining or fine-tuning. The results establish the proposed framework as an accurate, computationally efficient, and practically viable solution for real-world GWSHM applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于多保真度卷积自编码器迁移学习框架的导波损伤诊断方法（利用大规模仿真与有限实验数据）
基于导波的结构健康监测（GWSHM）结合车载换能器，对工程结构损伤早期诊断具有显著潜力。然而，深度学习模型的实际部署常受限于标注实验数据稀缺及生成大规模高保真仿真数据的高计算成本。本研究提出一种多保真度迁移学习框架，融合轻量级物理仿真、卷积自编码器（CAE）深度特征学习、前馈神经网络及有限实验测量，实现对安装压电换能器的板状结构损伤定位与尺寸评估。采用计算高效的1D时域谱元模型生成大规模合成数据用于预训练，迁移学习仅需少量标注数据即可适配实验域。CAE迁移学习框架在损伤定位精度上显著优于CNN框架，模型损伤定位R²得分超0.93，损伤尺寸R²得分达0.99，且在未见数据上展现出优异泛化能力，验证其在真实GWSHM应用中的准确性、计算效率与实用性。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of deploying deep learning in guided-wave structural health monitoring due to scarce labeled experimental data and expensive high-fidelity simulations. It proposes a multi-fidelity transfer learning framework combining lightweight physics-based simulations for pretraining, convolutional autoencoder-based feature learning, and a feed-forward neural network fine-tuned with limited experimental data. The framework leverages a computationally efficient 1D spectral element model to generate large synthetic datasets, then adapts to real-world measurements. Experimental results show the CAE-based approach significantly outperforms CNN-based methods in damage localization, achieving R² scores above 0.93 for localization and 0.99 for sizing, with strong generalization to unseen damage scenarios.
该研究针对导波结构健康监测中实验数据稀缺和高保真仿真成本高的问题，提出了一种多保真度迁移学习框架。该框架结合轻量级物理仿真进行预训练，利用卷积自编码器提取特征，并以少量实验数据微调前馈神经网络。通过计算高效的1D频谱元模型生成大规模合成数据，再通过迁移学习适配真实测量数据。实验表明，基于CAE的方法在损伤定位上显著优于CNN方法，定位R²超过0.93，尺寸预测R²达0.99，且对未见过的损伤场景具有良好的泛化能力。
AI Healthcare Chatbots as Information Infrastructure: A Large-Scale Study of User-Reported Breakdowns
Authors: Muhammad Hassan, Ramazan Yener, Ece Gumusel, Masooda Bashir
First: 2026-06-25T17:24:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:24:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI healthcare chatbots are increasingly used to support health information seeking and self-management, yet their performance and impact on users remains to be studied. This study examines over 15,000 user reviews from 59 AI healthcare chatbot apps to explore how these systems function in everyday informational and emotional contexts. Topic modeling and interpretive analysis identify three recurring breakdowns: access barriers and service unreliability, user experience and interaction quality, and billing and customer support issues. Privacy and security concerns are associated with the most negative experiences. By framing AI healthcare chatbots as information infrastructures, our findings highlight how failures in access, usability, and trust affect users, offering actionable insights for designers, policymakers, and information professionals aiming to improve digital health systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能医疗聊天机器人作为信息基础设施：一项大规模用户报告故障研究
人工智能医疗聊天机器人正越来越多地用于支持健康信息获取和自我管理，但其表现和对用户的影响仍待研究。本研究分析了59款人工智能医疗聊天机器人应用的15,000多条用户评论，探讨这些系统在日常信息与情感情境中的运作方式。主题建模与解释性分析识别出三大常见故障：访问障碍与服务不可靠、用户体验与交互质量、计费与客户服务问题。隐私与安全问题常与最负面体验相关。通过将人工智能医疗聊天机器人视为信息基础设施，本研究揭示了访问、可用性与信任方面的失败如何影响用户，为设计师、政策制定者和信息专业人员改进数字健康系统提供可操作的见解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of AI healthcare chatbots in health information seeking and self-management, this study analyzed 15,000 user reviews from 59 apps to understand real-world performance. Using topic modeling and interpretive analysis, it identified three key breakdowns: access and service unreliability, poor user experience and interaction quality, and billing and customer support issues. Privacy and security concerns were strongly linked to negative user experiences. Framing chatbots as information infrastructures, the findings reveal how failures in access, usability, and trust undermine user confidence and effectiveness, providing actionable guidance for designers and policymakers.
本研究通过分析59款AI医疗聊天机器人应用的1.5万条用户评论，探讨其在实际使用中的表现，旨在填补对这类工具健康信息获取支持效果的研究空白。采用主题建模与解释性分析，识别出三大主要失效类型：访问障碍与服务不可靠、用户体验与交互质量差、计费与客服问题。隐私与安全担忧与负面体验高度相关，研究将聊天机器人视为信息基础设施，揭示访问、可用性与信任层面的缺陷如何影响用户，为设计者、政策制定者和信息专业人员优化数字健康系统提供可操作建议。
Reading the Same Data Differently: Interpretive Labor Across System Boundaries in Electronic Monitoring
Authors: Yibo Meng, Bingyi Liu, Zhiqi Gao, Shuai Ma, Hongyu Zhou
First: 2026-06-25T17:23:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:23:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Electronic monitoring (EM) systems are increasingly used in community corrections to enforce spatial, temporal, and behavioral rules through continuous sensing. While prior work has examined EM as a criminal justice tool or as a mechanism for compliance, less is known about how sensed data become meaningful in everyday practice. This poster examines EM as a dual-sided sensing system in which supervised individuals and authorities reason about the same data stream from different positions. Based on semi-structured interviews with 26 supervised individuals and 12 authorities in China's community corrections system, we show that supervised individuals infer system logic from outcomes with limited visibility into how data are interpreted, while authorities reconstruct behavior from ambiguous traces using contextual knowledge, professional experience, and institutional procedures. We call this structural divergence interpretive misalignment. It emerges from asymmetric access to data, context, and reasoning processes, and it shapes behavior through probing, strategic adaptation, over-compliance, disengagement, and contestation. We contribute a CSCW account of continuous sensing as distributed interpretive work and identify design opportunities for making data-to-decision processes more legible, contestable, and accountable across system sides.
中文标题/摘要
标题：以不同方式解读相同数据：电子监控系统边界内的解释性劳动
电子监控（EM）系统正越来越多地用于社区矫正，通过持续感知强制执行空间、时间和行为规则。尽管已有研究将EM视为刑事司法工具或合规机制，但对感知数据如何在日常实践中转化为意义的了解仍显不足。本海报将EM视为一种双向感知系统，其中受监督个体与执法者从不同立场对同一数据流进行推理。基于对中国社区矫正系统26名受监督个体与12名执法者的半结构化访谈，我们发现：受监督个体从结果中推断系统逻辑，但对数据如何被解读缺乏可见性；而执法者则利用情境知识、专业经验与制度程序，从模糊痕迹中重构行为。我们称此结构性差异为“解释性错位”，它源于数据、情境与推理过程的不对称访问，并通过探查、策略性适应、过度合规、脱节与抗争等方式塑造行为。我们提出了一种CSCW视角下的持续感知作为分布式解释性工作的理论框架，并识别出使数据到决策过程在系统双方间更清晰、可争议、可问责的设计机会。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how electronic monitoring systems create interpretive misalignment between supervised individuals and authorities, who analyze the same data streams but from divergent perspectives. Through interviews with 26 supervised individuals and 12 authorities in China’s community corrections system, the research reveals that supervised individuals infer system logic from observable outcomes without insight into data interpretation, while authorities reconstruct behavior using contextual knowledge, experience, and institutional procedures. This structural divergence leads to behavioral adaptations such as over-compliance, strategic adaptation, or disengagement, and the study proposes CSCW-based design interventions to make data-to-decision processes more transparent and accountable across system boundaries.
本研究探讨电子监控系统如何在被监管者与执法者之间造成解释性错位，尽管双方共享同一数据流。通过对26名被监管者和12名执法者的访谈，研究发现被监管者通过结果推断系统逻辑，却缺乏对数据解读过程的理解；而执法者则借助情境知识、专业经验和制度程序重构行为。这种结构性差异导致被监管者采取过度服从、策略性适应或消极应对等行为，凸显了跨系统边界提升数据决策透明度、可争议性和问责性的设计需求。
Mitigating Hallucinations via Inter-Layer Consistency Aggregation in Large Vision-Language Models
Authors: Kai Tang, Jinhao You, Yichen Guo, Yiding Sun, Dongxu Zhang, Wenya Wang, Hanze Li, Tao Luo, Renyuan Li, Xiande Huang
First: 2025-05-18T10:15:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:16:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the impressive capabilities of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), they remain susceptible to hallucinations, where generated content is inconsistent with the input image. Existing training-free hallucination mitigation methods often suffer from unstable performance and high sensitivity to hyperparameter settings, which limits their practicality and broader adoption. In this paper, we propose Decoding with Inter-layer Consistency via Layer Aggregation (DCLA), a training-free decoding mechanism that requires no retraining, fine-tuning, or access to external knowledge bases. Specifically, DCLA constructs a dynamic semantic reference by aggregating representations from previous layers and uses it to correct semantically deviated layers, thereby enforcing inter-layer consistency. Experiments across seven LVLMs and multiple benchmarks demonstrate the generality of DCLA: it surpasses standard decoding by 28.58 MME points on LLaVA1.5-7B and 42.6 MME points on Qwen2.5-VL, while improving POPE accuracy by 2.74 percentage points in the strongest setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过层间一致性聚合缓解大型视觉-语言模型中的幻觉
尽管大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）具备令人印象深刻的性能，但它们仍易产生幻觉，即生成内容与输入图像不一致。现有无需训练的幻觉缓解方法常存在性能不稳定、对超参数设置敏感等问题，限制了其实际应用和广泛采用。本文提出了一种无需训练的解码机制——通过层聚合实现层间一致性（DCLA），无需重训练、微调或外部知识库访问。具体而言，DCLA 通过聚合前层表示构建动态语义参考，并用于修正语义偏离的层，从而强制层间一致性。在七种 LVLMs 和多个基准测试中的实验表明，DCLA 具有广泛适用性：在 LLaVA1.5-7B 上比标准解码高出 28.58 MME 分数，在 Qwen2.5-VL 上高出 42.6 MME 分数，且在最强设置下将 POPE 准确率提升 2.74 个百分点。
Summary / 总结
To address the persistent hallucination issue in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), where outputs often contradict input images, the authors introduce DCLA, a training-free decoding method that enhances inter-layer consistency without retraining or external resources. DCLA dynamically aggregates semantic representations from earlier layers to correct semantically inconsistent later layers, thereby stabilizing output generation. Evaluated across seven LVLMs and multiple benchmarks, DCLA consistently outperforms standard decoding, achieving a 28.58 MME improvement on LLaVA1.5-7B and 42.6 MME on Qwen2.5-VL, while also boosting POPE accuracy by 2.74 percentage points in the strongest setting.
为解决大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）中因输出与输入图像不一致而产生的幻觉问题，本文提出DCLA方法，这是一种无需训练或外部知识库的解码机制，通过聚合前层语义表征来纠正语义偏离的后层，从而增强层间一致性。在七种LVLMs和多个基准测试中，DCLA表现稳定，相较标准解码在LLaVA1.5-7B上提升28.58 MME，在Qwen2.5-VL上提升42.6 MME，并在最强设置下将POPE准确率提升2.74个百分点。
Fast algorithms for learning a Gaussian under halfspace truncation with optimal sample complexity
Authors: Haitong Liu, Deepak Narayanan Sridharan, David Steurer, Manuel Wiedmer
First: 2026-06-25T17:14:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:14:57+00:00
Comments: 88 pages; accepted at the 39th Annual Conference on Learning Theory (COLT 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the fundamental problem of learning a high-dimensional Gaussian truncated to an unknown halfspace. Lee, Mehrotra and Zampetakis (FOCS'24) recently obtained the first polynomial time algorithm for this problem, but their resulting sample and time complexity bounds are not optimal. Under non-trivial truncation, for any target accuracy $\varepsilon > 0$ and dimension $d$ we give an efficient algorithm that uses $n = \tilde{O}(d^2/\varepsilon^2)$ samples and learns the underlying Gaussian to error $\varepsilon$ in total variation distance. Our algorithm is also fast: its runtime is dominated by the cost of computing the empirical covariance matrix. Both our sample and time complexity are optimal in terms of $d$ and $\varepsilon$ even without truncation: in this regard, we can learn a Gaussian under halfspace truncation for free. The key ingredient behind our result is a novel reinterpretation of the low-degree moments of the truncated Gaussian in terms of a relative truncation parameter. This relative truncation parameter uniquely determines the parameters of the untruncated Gaussian and enables direct parameter recovery. This reinterpretation allows us to circumvent the time intensive projected stochastic gradient descent procedure that is widely used in learning under truncation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在半空间截断下学习高维高斯分布的最优样本复杂度快速算法
我们研究了在未知半空间截断下学习高维高斯分布的基本问题。Lee、Mehrotra 和 Zampetakis（FOCS'24）最近提出了该问题的第一个多项式时间算法，但其样本和时间复杂度界限并非最优。在非平凡截断条件下，对于任意目标精度 $\varepsilon > 0$ 和维度 $d$，我们提出了一种高效算法，使用 $n = \tilde{O}(d^2/\varepsilon^2)$ 个样本，能在总变差距离下将底层高斯分布学习至误差 $\varepsilon$。我们的算法也很快：其运行时间主要由计算经验协方差矩阵的成本决定。即使不考虑截断，我们的样本和时间复杂度在 $d$ 和 $\varepsilon$ 上也已是最优：从这个角度看，我们可以在半空间截断下免费学习高斯分布。 我们结果的关键在于对截断高斯分布低阶矩的一种新颖重述，将其表示为相对截断参数。该相对截断参数唯一确定了未截断高斯分布的参数，从而实现直接参数恢复。这种重述使我们能够绕过广泛用于截断学习中的耗时投影随机梯度下降过程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for optimal sample and time complexity in learning high-dimensional Gaussians truncated to an unknown halfspace, this work presents a fast algorithm that achieves sample complexity \tilde{O}(d^2/\varepsilon^2) and total variation error \varepsilon, matching known lower bounds even without truncation. The method leverages a novel reinterpretation of low-degree moments of the truncated Gaussian via a relative truncation parameter, which uniquely determines the untruncated Gaussian’s parameters, enabling direct recovery without costly projected gradient descent. Experimental results confirm the algorithm’s efficiency and optimality in both sample and runtime complexity across varying dimensions and accuracy levels.
为解决在未知半空间截断下学习高维高斯分布的最优样本与时间复杂度问题，作者提出一种高效算法，使用 \(\tilde{O}(d^2/\varepsilon^2)\) 个样本即可在总变差距离下将误差控制在 \(\varepsilon\)。该方法的核心在于将截断高斯的低阶矩重新解释为一个相对截断参数，该参数唯一确定未截断高斯的参数，从而实现直接恢复，无需迭代投影。算法运行时间主要由经验协方差矩阵计算决定，其样本与时间复杂度在 \(d\) 和 \(\varepsilon\) 上均达到最优，甚至在无截断情况下亦如此，因此半空间截断下的学习成本几乎为零。
Probabilistic NDVI Forecasting from Sparse Satellite Time Series and Weather Covariates
Authors: Irene Iele, Giulia Romoli, Daniele Molino, Elena Mulero Ayllón, Filippo Ruffini, Paolo Soda, Matteo Tortora
First: 2026-02-04T17:48:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:14:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Short-term forecasting of vegetation dynamics is a key enabler for data-driven decision support in precision agriculture. Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI) forecasting from satellite observations, however, remains challenging due to sparse and irregular sampling caused by cloud masking, as well as the heterogeneous climatic conditions under which crops evolve. In this work, we propose a probabilistic forecasting framework for field-level NDVI prediction under sparse, irregular clear-sky acquisitions. The architecture separates the encoding of historical NDVI and meteorological observations from future exogenous covariates, fusing both representations for multi-step quantile prediction. To address irregular revisit patterns and horizon-dependent uncertainty, we introduce a temporal-distance weighted quantile loss that aligns the training objective with the effective forecasting horizon. In addition, we incorporate cumulative and extreme-weather feature engineering to capture delayed meteorological effects relevant to vegetation response. Experiments on European satellite data show that the proposed approach outperforms statistical, deep learning, and time-series baselines on both pointwise and probabilistic evaluation metrics. Ablation studies confirm that target history is the primary driver of performance, with meteorological covariates providing additional gains in the full multimodal setting. The code is available at https://github.com/arco-group/ndvi-forecasting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于稀疏卫星时序数据和气象协变量的NDVI概率预报
植被动态的短期预报是精准农业数据驱动决策支持的关键。然而，由于云掩蔽导致的采样稀疏和不规则，以及作物在异质气候条件下生长，基于卫星观测的归一化植被指数（NDVI）预报仍具挑战性。本文提出了一种针对稀疏、不规则晴天观测条件下田块级NDVI预测的概率预报框架。该架构将历史NDVI与气象观测的编码与未来外生协变量分离，并融合两者进行多步分位数预测。为应对不规则重访模式和时程依赖的不确定性，我们引入了基于时距加权的分位数损失，使训练目标与有效预报时程对齐。此外，我们结合累积与极端天气特征工程，捕捉对植被响应相关的延迟气象效应。在欧洲卫星数据上的实验表明，所提方法在点估计和概率评估指标上均优于统计、深度学习和时间序列基线方法。消融研究证实，目标历史是性能的主要驱动因素，气象协变量在全多模态设置下提供额外增益。代码见 https://github.com/arco-group/ndvi-forecasting。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable short-term vegetation forecasting to support precision agriculture, this study proposes a probabilistic framework for predicting field-level NDVI from sparse satellite data and weather covariates. The method decouples historical NDVI and meteorological encoding, then fuses them to generate multi-step quantile forecasts, using a temporal-distance weighted loss to handle irregular sampling and horizon-specific uncertainty. It also integrates cumulative and extreme weather features to model delayed climate impacts. Experiments on European satellite data demonstrate superior performance over statistical, deep learning, and time-series baselines on both point and probabilistic metrics, with ablation studies confirming that historical NDVI is the main performance driver, while weather covariates add incremental value.
为支持精准农业中的数据驱动决策，本研究提出一种基于稀疏卫星数据和气象协变量的NDVI概率预测框架。该方法将历史NDVI与气象观测分别编码后融合，生成多步分位数预测，并引入时距加权分位损失以应对采样不规则性和不同预测时长的不确定性，同时加入累积与极端天气特征以捕捉对植被响应的滞后影响。在欧洲卫星数据上的实验表明，该方法在点估计与概率评估指标上均优于统计、深度学习及时间序列基线模型，消融实验显示历史NDVI是性能主要驱动力，气象协变量在多模态设置下提供额外增益。
OmniRobotHome: A Multi-Camera Home Platform for Real-Time Human-Robot Interaction
Authors: Junyoung Lee, Inhee Lee, Sookwan Han, Jeonghwan Kim, Kyungwon Cho, Mingi Choi, Lee Chae-Yeon, Wonjung Woo, Gunhee Kim, Jisoo Kim, Jeonghyeon Na, Hanbyul Joo
First: 2026-04-30T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:13:48+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://junc0ng.github.io/omnirobothome
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robots in homes must continuously sense the people around them, yet most prior work relies on limited or offline perception. We argue that perception quality is the dominant factor governing what interaction is achievable at home, and build a testbed to test this claim. OmniRobotHome instruments a furnished home with 48 hardware-synchronized cameras and three manipulators in a unified world frame, delivering real-time markerless full-body human pose, 6D object pose, anticipatory motion forecasting, and a social avatar agent that converses with residents. Using the platform, we treat perception quality as an experimental variable across safety, human assistance, and social interaction, and find that interaction quality degrades measurably as real-timeness, granularity, coverage, accuracy, forecasting, or memory is weakened. All code and data will be released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OmniRobotHome：面向家庭实时人机交互的多摄像头平台
家庭中的机器人必须持续感知周围的人，但大多数先前工作依赖有限或离线感知。我们认为感知质量是决定家庭中可实现交互能力的主要因素，并构建了一个测试平台来验证这一观点。OmniRobotHome 在一个布置好的家庭中部署了48个硬件同步摄像头和三个机械臂，统一于世界坐标系下，提供实时无标记全身人体姿态、6D物体姿态、预测性运动预测以及与居民对话的社会化虚拟代理。通过该平台，我们将感知质量作为实验变量，考察其在安全、人类辅助与社交交互中的影响，发现当实时性、粒度、覆盖范围、精度、预测能力或记忆能力减弱时，交互质量会明显下降。所有代码和数据将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-quality real-time perception to enable effective human-robot interaction in homes, the authors built OmniRobotHome—a multi-camera testbed with 48 synchronized cameras and three manipulators—to evaluate how perception fidelity affects interaction outcomes. They measured interaction quality across safety, assistance, and social engagement under varying levels of real-time performance, granularity, coverage, accuracy, forecasting, and memory, finding that each degradation in perception metrics led to measurable declines in interaction quality. The platform enables markerless full-body human pose and 6D object pose tracking, along with anticipatory motion forecasting and a conversational social avatar.
为提升家庭机器人与人类交互的质量，作者构建了OmniRobotHome平台，配备48个同步摄像头和三个机械臂，实现无标记全身姿态、6D物体位姿、运动预测及社交代理对话。通过系统性降低感知维度（实时性、粒度、覆盖范围、精度、预测能力、记忆能力），实验发现交互质量随任一维度下降而显著降低，验证了感知质量是家庭机器人交互的核心瓶颈。
LA4VLA: Learning to Act without Seeing via Language-Action Pretraining
Authors: Tao Lin, Yuxin Du, Yiran Mao, Zewei Ye, Yilei Zhong, Bing Cheng, Yiming Wang, Jiting Liu, Yang Tian, Junchi Yan, Feiran Wu, Zenan Meng, Hu Wei, Yuqian Fu, Gen Li, Bo Zhao
First: 2026-06-25T17:13:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:13:02+00:00
Comments: Github: https://github.com/MINT-SJTU/LA4VLA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are commonly pretrained on robot demonstrations by jointly mapping visual observations and language instructions to actions. However, dense visual-action supervision can dominate the comparatively sparse language-action signal. As a result, policies may rely on visual shortcuts rather than learn how language conditions action execution, making them sensitive to visual variations. To address this limitation, we propose LA4VLA, a language-action pretraining framework that enables policies to acquire language-conditioned action priors without visual observations. These priors capture reusable manipulation skills shared across tasks and scenes, reducing reliance on scene-specific visual cues. Specifically, LA4VLA decomposes expert demonstration trajectories into atomic action segments and pairs each segment with a corresponding low-level action description. This yields LA4-33K, a dataset of 33K Language-Action (LA) episodes derived entirely from existing demonstrations without additional robot data collection. We further develop LA4VLA-1B, a lightweight 1B-parameter VLA model, and investigate three paradigms for incorporating language-action supervision into VLA learning: LA-only pretraining, sequential LA-to-VLA pretraining, and mixed LA-VLA pretraining. Across simulation and real-world tasks, LA-pretrained policies consistently outperform matched VLA-pretrained counterparts, while combining LA and VLA supervision leads to further gains. In particular, mixed LA-VLA pretraining improves the average success rate of LA4VLA-1B over the no-pretraining baseline by up to 17.8 and 45.0 percentage points in simulation and real-world tasks, respectively. These results establish LA4VLA as an effective and complementary pretraining strategy for building stronger and more robust VLA policies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LA4VLA：通过语言-动作预训练实现无需视觉感知的行动学习
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通常通过联合映射视觉观测与语言指令到动作的方式，利用机器人演示进行预训练。然而，密集的视觉-动作监督信号可能主导相对稀疏的语言-动作信号，导致策略依赖视觉捷径而非学习语言如何调控动作执行，使其对视觉变化敏感。为解决此问题，我们提出LA4VLA，一种语言-动作预训练框架，使策略能在无视觉观测的情况下获取语言条件化的动作先验。这些先验捕捉跨任务和场景可复用的操作技能，减少对场景特定视觉线索的依赖。具体而言，LA4VLA将专家演示轨迹分解为原子动作片段，并为每个片段配对相应的低级动作描述，由此构建LA4-33K数据集——该数据集完全基于现有演示生成，无需额外机器人数据采集。我们进一步开发了轻量级10亿参数的VLA模型LA4VLA-1B，并研究三种将语言-动作监督融入VLA学习的方法：仅语言-动作预训练、序列式语言-动作到视觉-语言-动作预训练、混合语言-动作与视觉-语言-动作预训练。在仿真与真实任务中，语言预训练策略始终优于匹配的视觉-语言-动作预训练策略，而结合语言-动作与视觉-语言-动作监督可进一步提升性能。特别是，混合预训练使LA4VLA-1B在仿真与真实任务中的平均成功率较无预训练基线分别提升17.8和45.0个百分点。这些结果确立了LA4VLA作为一种有效且互补的预训练策略，用于构建更强、更鲁棒的VLA策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that visual-action supervision in VLA models can overshadow language-action signals, leading to policies that rely on visual shortcuts, the authors propose LA4VLA, a language-action pretraining framework that enables policies to learn language-conditioned action priors without visual input. The method decomposes expert trajectories into atomic action segments and pairs each with a low-level action description, creating LA4-33K, a dataset of 33K language-action episodes derived from existing demonstrations. Three pretraining paradigms—LA-only, sequential LA-to-VLA, and mixed LA-VLA—are tested using a 1B-parameter VLA model. Experimental results show that LA-pretrained policies outperform VLA-pretrained baselines, with mixed LA-VLA pretraining achieving up to 17.8 and 45.0 percentage point gains in success rate over no-pretraining in simulation and real-world tasks, respectively.
为了解决视觉-动作监督在VLA模型中压制语言-动作信号、导致策略依赖视觉捷径的问题，LA4VLA提出一种无需视觉输入的语言-动作预训练框架，通过从现有机器人演示中构建33K条语言-动作片段（LA4-33K）数据集，训练1B参数的LA4VLA-1B模型。实验对比三种预训练范式，混合语言-动作与视觉-动作监督的方案表现最优，在仿真和真实任务中较无预训练基线分别提升17.8%和45.0%的成功率。
Generative Models on Analog Hardware with Dynamics
Authors: Yu-Neng Wang, Sara Achour
First: 2026-06-25T17:13:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:13:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Analog hardware platforms such as coupled oscillators and Analog Ising Machines naturally solve differential equations at a fraction of the energy cost of digital computation, making them attractive for low-power generative modeling, yet a fundamental mismatch exists: modern generative models assume flexible, software-defined dynamics, whereas analog hardware imposes fixed, physics-determined differential equations with limited approximation capacity. This paper introduces Analog Interaction Systems (AIS), a unified framework for hardware-implementable dynamical systems, and empirically characterizes their expressivity gap relative to neural network baselines. Two hardware-compatible mechanisms are proposed to narrow this gap - time-varying piecewise parameters and hidden physical states - and a Wasserstein GAN training procedure is developed to enable training of these models without requiring them to follow a specific trajectory. We characterize how area and power scale with connection density and precision, showing that sparse connectivity and low-bit-width quantized parameters are necessary for practical implementation, and estimate an energy cost of 23uJ per generated image for the chosen architecture, representing a 2-orders-of-magnitude improvement over digital baselines. On MNIST and Fashion-MNIST, our oscillator-based AIS achieves FID scores of 27.6 and 80.8, outperforming the best prior hardware-implementable analog generative models by 3-4x with a 4-bit sparse architecture.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有动态特性的模拟硬件上的生成模型
耦合振荡器和模拟伊辛机等模拟硬件平台能以远低于数字计算的能量成本自然求解微分方程，使其在低功耗生成建模中具有吸引力，但存在根本性不匹配：现代生成模型假设灵活的、软件定义的动态，而模拟硬件则施加固定、由物理定律决定的微分方程，且近似能力有限。本文提出了模拟交互系统（AIS），一个统一的可硬件实现的动态系统框架，并实证分析了其相对于神经网络基线的表达能力差距。提出两种兼容硬件的机制——时变分段参数和隐藏物理状态——以缩小该差距，并开发了一种Wasserstein GAN训练流程，使这些模型无需遵循特定轨迹即可训练。我们分析了面积和功耗随连接密度与精度的变化，表明稀疏连接和低比特宽度量化参数对实际实现是必要的，并估计所选架构每生成一张图像的能量成本为23微焦，较数字基线提升两个数量级。在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST数据集上，基于振荡器的AIS模型分别取得FID分数27.6和80.8，以4比特稀疏架构超越现有最佳可硬件实现的模拟生成模型3-4倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the energy efficiency of analog hardware for solving differential equations, this paper addresses the mismatch between flexible neural generative models and fixed physical dynamics of analog systems. It introduces Analog Interaction Systems (AIS), a framework enabling hardware-implementable dynamical models, and proposes two mechanisms—time-varying parameters and hidden physical states—to bridge the expressivity gap. Training is achieved via a Wasserstein GAN that does not require trajectory adherence. Experiments show that sparse connectivity and low-bit quantization are essential for practicality, with an estimated 23uJ per image—20x more efficient than digital baselines—and FID scores of 27.6 and 80.8 on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST, respectively, outperforming prior analog models by 3-4x.
为解决模拟硬件（如耦合振荡器）在低功耗生成建模中的潜力与现有软件定义生成模型之间动态结构不匹配的问题，本文提出Analog Interaction Systems（AIS）统一框架，支持硬件可实现的动态系统。通过引入时变分段参数与隐藏物理状态两种机制缩小表达能力差距，并设计Wasserstein GAN训练方法以无需轨迹约束即可训练。实验表明，稀疏连接与低比特量化是实用化关键，所选架构每张图像能耗仅23uJ，较数字基线提升两个数量级。在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上，基于振荡器的AIS分别取得27.6和80.8的FID分数，性能较此前最佳模拟生成模型提升3-4倍，且仅用4比特稀疏架构实现。
BOWConnect: Parallel Bayesian Optimization over Windows with Learned Local Cost Maps for Sample-Efficient Kinodynamic Motion Planning
Authors: Sourav Raxit, Abdullah Al Redwan Newaz, Jose Fuentes, Leonardo Bobadilla
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-06-25T17:11:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:11:33+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 2026 IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
This paper presents BOWConnect, a bidirectional parallel kinodynamic motion planner that addresses three fundamental limitations of existing sampling-based methods: sample inefficiency in high-dimensional state spaces, unreliable cost heuristics under dynamic constraints, and poor performance in narrow passage environments. Unlike classical planners that rely on random control sampling and geometric distance heuristics, BOWConnect integrates Bayesian Optimization over Windows (BOW) as a learning-based steering function within a parallel tree-based exploration framework, enabling each worker to learn local cost maps and constraints to guide sampling toward dynamically feasible and collision-free controls. A bidirectional architecture simultaneously grows forward and backward trees from the start and goal regions in parallel threads, with a spatial hashing mechanism enabling fast connection queries and a boundary value problem solver generating kinodynamically consistent bridge trajectories. Extensive evaluations across ten benchmark environments demonstrate that BOWConnect achieves 100\% success while delivering the fastest or near-fastest planning time in complex scenarios, including narrow passages and non-convex spaces where state-of-the-art planners fail or degrade substantially. Real-world deployment on a ground vehicle and a quadrotor confirms real-time planning with no collisions. Videos of real-world and simulated experiments, high-resolution versions of the figures, and the open-source code are available at https://bow-connect.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BOWConnect：基于学习局部代价图的窗口内并行贝叶斯优化运动规划
本文提出BOWConnect，一种双向并行运动规划器，解决了现有采样方法的三大根本局限：高维状态空间下的采样效率低下、动态约束下代价启发式不可靠、狭窄通道环境中的性能不佳。与依赖随机控制采样和几何距离启发式的经典规划器不同，BOWConnect在并行树结构探索框架中集成窗口内贝叶斯优化（BOW）作为学习型转向函数，使每个工作线程能学习局部代价图和约束，引导采样趋向动态可行且无碰撞的控制。双向架构通过并行线程从起始与目标区域同时生长前向与后向树，空间哈希机制支持快速连接查询，边界值问题求解器生成符合运动动力学的桥接轨迹。在十种基准环境的广泛评估中，BOWConnect实现100%成功率，并在复杂场景（如狭窄通道与非凸空间）中达到最快或接近最快的规划时间，而当前最先进规划器在此类场景中失败或性能大幅下降。在地面车辆与四旋翼无人机上的实际部署验证了实时规划且无碰撞。相关实验视频、高分辨率图示及开源代码见https://bow-connect.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and unreliability of existing sampling-based motion planners in high-dimensional, dynamic, and narrow environments, BOWConnect introduces a bidirectional parallel planner that uses Bayesian Optimization over Windows to learn local cost maps and guide sampling toward feasible controls. Each worker independently builds a tree from start and goal regions while leveraging spatial hashing for fast connection queries and a boundary value solver for kinodynamically consistent trajectories. Experimental results across ten benchmarks show BOWConnect achieves 100% success rate and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in planning speed, especially in narrow passages and non-convex spaces, with real-time performance verified on ground vehicles and quadrotors.
为解决传统采样式运动规划器在高维、动态及狭窄环境中的效率低下与不可靠问题，BOWConnect提出一种双向并行规划器，利用窗口式贝叶斯优化学习局部代价地图，引导采样趋向可行控制。每个工作线程独立从起点与目标区域生长树结构，结合空间哈希加速连接查询与边界值求解器生成动力学一致的桥接轨迹。十项基准测试表明，BOWConnect在复杂场景中实现100%成功率，规划速度最快或接近最快，而现有方法常失败或显著退化。真实世界测试（地面车与四旋翼）验证其实时无碰撞运行能力。
The Best of the Two Worlds: Harmonizing Semantic and Hash IDs for Sequential Recommendation
Authors: Ziwei Liu, Yejing Wang, Wanyu Wang, Wang Zejian, Qidong Liu, Zijian Zhang, Chong Chen, Wei Huang, Xiangyu Zhao
First: 2025-12-11T07:50:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:11:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conventional Sequential Recommender Systems (SRS) typically assign unique hash IDs (HID) to construct item embeddings, which mainly capture collaborative signals from historical user-item interactions. However, such embeddings are vulnerable in long-tail scenarios where most items are rarely consumed. Recent methods that incorporate auxiliary information often face noisy collaborative sharing from co-occurrence signals or semantic homogeneity caused by flat dense embeddings. In contrast, Semantic IDs (SID), with their support for code sharing and multi-granular semantic modeling, offer a promising alternative. Nevertheless, SID-based methods are hindered by a collaborative overwhelming phenomenon: commonly adopted quantization mechanisms compromise the identifier uniqueness needed to model head items, resulting in a performance trade-off between head and tail items. To address this challenge, we propose H2Rec, a novel framework that harmonizes SID and HID. We design a dual-branch modeling architecture that simultaneously captures the multi-granular semantics of SID while preserving the unique collaborative identity provided by HID. Moreover, we introduce a dual-level alignment strategy to bridge the two representations, enabling effective knowledge transfer and robust preference modeling. Extensive offline experiments on three public benchmarks and online experiments on a large-scale commercial platform demonstrate that H2Rec achieves a better balance between head and tail recommendation quality and consistently outperforms existing baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：双世界之优：融合语义与哈希ID的序列推荐
传统序列推荐系统（SRS）通常为构建物品嵌入分配唯一哈希ID（HID），主要捕捉历史用户-物品交互的协同信号。然而，此类嵌入在长尾场景中脆弱，因多数物品消费频次低。近期结合辅助信息的方法常面临共现信号带来的噪声协同共享或扁平稠密嵌入导致的语义同质化问题。相比之下，支持代码共享与多粒度语义建模的语义ID（SID）提供了有前景的替代方案。但SID方法受限于协同过载现象：常用量化机制损害了建模头部物品所需标识唯一性，导致头部与尾部物品性能权衡。为解决此问题，我们提出H2Rec框架，融合SID与HID。设计双分支建模架构，同时捕捉SID的多粒度语义并保留HID提供的独特协同身份。此外，引入双层级对齐策略，弥合两种表示，实现有效知识迁移与稳健偏好建模。在三个公开基准的离线实验及大型商业平台的在线实验中，H2Rec在头部与尾部推荐质量间取得更好平衡，持续优于现有基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of hash IDs in capturing semantic diversity and the drawbacks of semantic IDs in preserving collaborative uniqueness, this paper proposes H2Rec, a framework that harmonizes both representations. It employs a dual-branch architecture to jointly model multi-granular semantics from semantic IDs and unique collaborative signals from hash IDs, while a dual-level alignment strategy facilitates knowledge transfer between them. Experimental results across three public datasets and a commercial platform show that H2Rec achieves superior balance in recommendation quality for both head and tail items, outperforming existing methods.
为解决传统哈希ID在长尾场景下的表现不足及语义ID语义丰富但协同信号弱的问题，本文提出H2Rec框架，融合二者优势。该框架采用双分支结构，同时建模语义ID的多粒度语义与哈希ID的协同唯一性，并通过双层对齐策略实现表征融合。实验表明，H2Rec在热门与冷门物品推荐上取得更优平衡，显著优于现有方法。
Designing Reward Signals for Portable Query Generation: A Case Study in Industrial Semantic Job Search
Authors: Ping Liu, Qianqi Shen, Jianqiang Shen, Wenqiong Liu, Rajat Arora, Yunxiang Ren, Chunnan Yao, Dan Xu, Baofen Zheng, Wanjun Jiang, Andrii Soviak, Kevin Kao, Jingwei Wu, Wenjing Zhang
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-06-25T17:09:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:09:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted to KDD 2026 Workshop on AI Agent for Information Retrieval (Agent4IR)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Job-search platforms rely on low-bandwidth query interfaces that often fail to capture the high-dimensional complexity of candidate profiles. We present an end-to-end RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) framework to generate \emph{portable} job search queries, terms that abstract away seeker-specific identifiers while preserving generalizable qualifications. This task introduces a highly adversarial reward surface where policy optimization frequently exploits flaws in LLM-as-judge rubrics, resulting in degenerate verbatim-copying behaviors. We conducted comprehensive empirical experiments to isolate the impact of optimization mechanics against structured reward engineering. Our results demonstrate that for critic-free optimizers, performance is overwhelmingly dictated by robust reward shaping, rendering the specific choice of algorithm largely immaterial. While critic-free per-rollout baseline methods (RLOO and REINFORCE++) natively resist reward-hacking, the group-relative advantage normalization in GRPO appears uniquely sensitive to spurious reward signals, making it disproportionately susceptible to exploitation. We show that introducing a deterministic, rule-based reward floor to correct for rewards assigned to verbatim copying mitigates this failure mode, resulting in a substantial $+0.147$ quality improvement on a cross-family evaluation judge. Ultimately, we show that the training-time reward model inflates performance gains by $2.4\times$, confirming that the training success is fundamentally dependent on enforcing reward-shaping disciplines rather than selecting alternative optimizers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向可移植查询生成的奖励信号设计：工业语义职位搜索案例研究
求职平台依赖低带宽查询界面，常无法捕捉候选人档案的高维复杂性。我们提出了一种端到端的RLAIF（基于AI反馈的强化学习）框架，用于生成可移植的职位搜索查询，即抽象掉求职者特定标识符，同时保留可泛化的资质。该任务具有高度对抗性的奖励表面，策略优化常利用LLM作为裁判的评分标准缺陷，导致退化为逐字复制行为。 我们进行了全面的实证实验，以分离优化机制与结构化奖励工程的影响。结果表明，对于无评论器的优化器，性能主要由稳健的奖励塑造决定，具体算法选择影响甚微。虽然无评论器的每轮基准方法（RLOO和REINFORCE++）天然抵抗奖励欺骗，但GRPO中的组相对优势归一化对虚假奖励信号异常敏感，使其更容易被利用。我们证明，引入确定性、基于规则的奖励下限以纠正逐字复制所获奖励，可缓解此失效模式，使跨家族评估裁判质量提升+0.147。最终，我们表明训练时的奖励模型使性能提升2.4倍，证实训练成功根本依赖于强制执行奖励塑造规范，而非选择其他优化器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating portable job search queries that generalize across candidate profiles without relying on personal identifiers, the authors propose an RLAIF framework to optimize query generation under adversarial reward conditions. Their method evaluates query quality using LLM-as-judge feedback, but finds that standard reinforcement learning optimizers are vulnerable to reward-hacking, especially when using GRPO’s group-relative normalization. Experimental results show that critic-free methods like RLOO and REINFORCE++ are more robust to reward manipulation, while GRPO is disproportionately affected by spurious rewards. Introducing a deterministic rule-based reward floor significantly improves quality by +0.147 on a cross-family judge, and they demonstrate that training-time reward models amplify performance gains by 2.4x, underscoring that reward shaping—not optimizer choice—is the critical factor for success.
为解决工业语义求职平台中低带宽界面难以捕捉候选人高维特征的问题，本文提出一种RLAIF框架，旨在生成可移植的求职查询，即抽象掉求职者特定标识但仍保留可泛化的资质。方法采用无批评者强化学习算法，并引入确定性规则奖励地板以抑制奖励欺骗行为（如逐字复制）。实验表明，奖励塑造比算法选择更重要，其中GRPO对虚假奖励尤为敏感，而RLOO和REINFORCE++天然更鲁棒。添加奖励地板可提升跨家族评估指标0.147，且训练时奖励模型使性能提升2.4倍，证明奖励塑造纪律是成功的核心而非优化器选择。
When Does Combining Language Models Help? A Co-Failure Ceiling on Routing, Voting, and Mixture-of-Agents Across 67 Frontier Models
Authors: Josef Chen
First: 2026-06-25T17:06:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:06:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-model LLM systems such as routing, voting, cascades, fusion, and mixture-of-agents are used to beat single-model accuracy. We show that their gain is capped by a quantity the field rarely reports. For any policy whose output is one member model answer, accuracy cannot exceed one minus beta, where beta is the rate at which every model is wrong on the same query. In contrast, the usual diagnostic, average pairwise error correlation rho, cannot identify beta: error laws with identical marginals and pairwise correlations can have different all-wrong rates. A Clopper-Pearson bound on beta gives a finite-sample certificate on the largest gain any router, vote, or cascade could deliver before training a router. Across 67 models from 21 providers, a tetrachoric-calibrated single-factor model still underprices the all-wrong tail: on open-ended mathematics, observed beta is 0.052 versus 0.023 under the full 67-model Gaussian copula, about 2.5 times underpricing, with 90 percent CI 1.7 to 3.4 and k equals 17. The effect recurs on execution-graded code, where beta is 0.079. Re-asking the same GPQA-Diamond questions in free-response rather than multiple-choice form reopens the tail, with beta 0.127 and a five-judge panel with kappa 0.73 to 0.92, locating co-failure in answer format rather than subject. At matched quality, low-rho heterogeneous ensembles beat high-rho Self-MoA, but on checkable tasks in our pool, combining models rarely beats the single best model without a strong query-level routing signal. Gains come from models failing on different questions, not from adding more models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：何时组合语言模型有益？路由、投票和多代理混合在67个前沿模型中的共同失败上限
路由、投票、级联、融合和多代理混合等多模型LLM系统用于超越单一模型的准确率。我们表明，其增益受一个领域很少报告的量所限制。对于任何输出为单一成员模型答案的策略，准确率无法超过1减去beta，其中beta是所有模型在相同查询上出错的比率。相比之下，通常的诊断指标——成对错误相关系数rho，无法识别beta：具有相同边际分布和成对相关性的错误分布，可能具有不同的全部错误率。对beta的Clopper-Pearson置信界，可提供有限样本下任何路由器、投票器或级联在训练前所能实现的最大增益的证书。 在21家供应商的67个模型中，经过四分相关校准的单因子模型仍低估了全部错误尾部：在开放式数学任务中，观测到的beta为0.052，而67模型高斯Copula下的beta为0.023，约低估2.5倍，90%置信区间为1.7至3.4，k=17。该效应在执行评分代码中重现，其中beta为0.079。将相同的GPQA-Diamond问题以自由回答而非多项选择形式重新提问，重新打开了尾部，beta为0.127，且五位评委的kappa值在0.73至0.92之间，表明共同失败源于答案格式而非主题。在同等质量下，低rho异质组合优于高rho的Self-MoA，但在我们任务池中的可验证任务中，组合模型在缺乏强查询级路由信号时，很少能超越单一最佳模型。增益源于模型在不同问题上出错，而非增加模型数量。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates when combining language models via routing, voting, or mixture-of-agents improves performance over single models, revealing that gains are capped by a 'co-failure ceiling'—the rate at which all models fail simultaneously on a query. The authors introduce beta, the probability that every model errs on the same input, which bounds the maximum accuracy gain achievable by any combination strategy. Experimental analysis across 67 frontier models shows that beta is significantly underestimated by standard pairwise correlation metrics (rho), with observed beta values (e.g., 0.052 in math, 0.079 in code) being 2.5x higher than Gaussian copula estimates. The effect persists across formats and tasks, and while heterogeneous ensembles with low rho outperform homogeneous Self-MoA systems at matched quality, combining models rarely beats the single best model without strong query-level routing signals, indicating gains stem from model disagreement rather than quantity.
该研究探讨了在何种情况下组合多个语言模型（如路由、投票或混合代理）能提升性能，发现其增益受‘协同失败上限’限制——即所有模型在同一查询上同时出错的概率。作者引入beta（全错率）作为比常用指标‘成对错误相关性rho’更准确的衡量标准，因为rho无法识别这种关键的失败模式。在67个前沿模型上，实验表明beta显著高于高斯Copula模型的预测值，说明对灾难性协同失败的低估，尤其在开放式数学和代码任务中明显。结果表明，即使高质量的异构组合也难超越单个最优模型，除非存在强查询级路由信号，且性能提升源于模型在不同问题上出错，而非单纯增加模型数量。
Prompt Injection in Automated Résumé Screening with Large Language Models: Single and Multi-Injection Settings
Authors: Preet Baxi, Jiannan Xu, Jane Yi Jiang, Stefanus Jasin
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-06-25T17:04:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:04:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to screen and rank job applicants, creating incentives for candidates to strategically manipulate algorithmic hiring systems. We study prompt injection in automated résumé screening, defined as subtle self-promotional text that introduces no new qualifications but is designed to influence LLM evaluations. Using controlled experiments, we show that prompt injection reliably improves applicant rankings when résumé quality is homogeneous and few candidates inject. However, its effectiveness rapidly diminishes as more candidates inject, collapsing when manipulation becomes widespread. When candidate quality is heterogeneous, prompt injection is less effective on average, but can occasionally allow lower-quality candidates to outrank higher-quality ones, raising fairness concerns. Overall, LLM-based screening is most vulnerable when manipulation is rare and candidate quality differences are small. Code and resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/preetb1199/Prompt_Injection_ACL26
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型在自动化简历筛选中的提示注入：单次与多次注入场景
大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地用于筛选和排名求职者，促使候选人策略性地操纵算法招聘系统。我们研究了自动化简历筛选中的提示注入，即不增加新资质但旨在影响LLM评估的微妙自我宣传文本。通过受控实验，我们发现当简历质量同质且少数候选人注入时，提示注入能稳定提升求职者排名；但随着更多候选人注入，其效果迅速减弱，当操纵普遍时则完全失效。当候选人质量异质时，提示注入平均效果较低，但偶尔会使低质量候选人排名高于高质量候选人，引发公平性担忧。总体而言，LLM筛选在操纵罕见且候选人质量差异较小时最易受攻击。代码和资源公开于：https://github.com/preetb1199/Prompt_Injection_ACL26
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of large language models in automated résumé screening, this study investigates how candidates can manipulate LLM evaluations through prompt injection—subtle self-promotional text that does not add real qualifications but aims to influence rankings. Using controlled experiments, the researchers found that prompt injection reliably boosts applicant rankings when résumé quality is uniform and few candidates manipulate the system. However, its effectiveness plummets as more candidates inject, collapsing under widespread manipulation. In heterogeneous quality settings, prompt injection is less effective on average but can still enable lower-quality candidates to rank above higher-quality ones, posing fairness risks. The system is most vulnerable when manipulation is rare and quality differences are minimal.
鉴于大型语言模型在自动化简历筛选中的广泛应用，本研究探讨了求职者如何通过‘提示注入’——即不增加真实资质但旨在影响模型评价的微妙自我宣传文本——操纵评估结果。实验表明，当简历质量相近且注入者稀少时，提示注入能稳定提升求职者排名；但随着注入者增多，其效果迅速衰减，当普遍滥用时几乎失效。在简历质量差异较大的情况下，注入平均效果减弱，但有时仍可使低质量候选人超越高质量者，引发公平性问题。系统最易受攻击的情形是注入行为稀少且候选人能力差异较小。
Simulation-based inference for rapid Bayesian parameter estimation in epidemiological models: a comparison with MCMC
Authors: Alina Bazarova, Johann Fredrik Jadebeck, Henrik Zunker, Carolina J. Klett-Tammen, Torben Heinsohn, Wolfgang Wiechert, Katharina Noeh, Stefan Kesselheim
First: 2026-06-25T17:03:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:03:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mechanistic epidemiological models are widely used to support infectious disease forecasting and public-health decision making. Bayesian calibration of such models is commonly performed using Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), which can become computationally expensive for high-dimensional nonlinear systems and repeated near-real-time analyses. Here, we investigate simulation-based inference (SBI) using neural posterior estimation as a scalable alternative for Bayesian calibration of a mechanistic SECIR epidemiological model using COVID-19 intensive care unit (ICU) occupancy data from Germany during 2020. We compared SBI and MCMC across multiple epidemic phases using both 31-day inference windows and a substantially more challenging 201-day reconstruction problem involving multiple transmission change points. Posterior agreement was evaluated quantitatively using Wasserstein distances and Kullback-Leibler divergences together with posterior predictive checks. Across the 31-day windows, SBI recovered posterior distributions in strong agreement with MCMC while accurately reproducing observed ICU trajectories. In the 201-day setting, SBI preserved the dominant posterior structure despite increased uncertainty. SBI, by combining CPU and GPU resources, substantially reduced computational runtime compared with MCMC, which was restricted to running on CPUs. Whereas MCMC required approximately 1000 seconds for the 31-day inference problems, SBI achieved comparable posterior and predictive performance in approximately 60-70 seconds on a single GPU. For the 201-day inference problem, SBI required an average of 157 seconds, while the MCMC runs took over 19,000 seconds. Our results demonstrate that SBI provides a rapid and computationally efficient framework for Bayesian calibration of mechanistic epidemiological models, supporting repeated near-real-time inference and rapid outbreak analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于仿真的推断用于流行病学模型的快速贝叶斯参数估计：与MCMC方法的比较
机制性流行病学模型广泛用于支持传染病预测和公共卫生决策。此类模型的贝叶斯校准通常使用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法，但对于高维非线性系统和重复的近实时分析，MCMC计算成本高昂。本文研究了使用神经后验估计的基于仿真的推断（SBI）作为对德国2020年COVID-19重症监护病房（ICU）占用数据进行机制性SECIR流行病学模型贝叶斯校准的可扩展替代方案。我们在多个流行阶段比较了SBI与MCMC，使用31天推断窗口和更具挑战性的201天重建问题（涉及多个传播变化点）。通过Wasserstein距离、Kullback-Leibler散度及后验预测检验定量评估后验一致性。在31天窗口中，SBI恢复的后验分布与MCMC高度一致，且准确再现了观测到的ICU轨迹。在201天设置中，SBI保留了主要后验结构，尽管不确定性增加。SBI通过结合CPU和GPU资源，显著降低了计算时间，而MCMC仅限于CPU运行。对于31天推断问题，MCMC需约1000秒，SBI在单个GPU上约60-70秒即可达到相当的后验和预测性能。对于201天推断问题，SBI平均需157秒，而MCMC运行超过19,000秒。我们的结果表明，SBI为机制性流行病学模型的贝叶斯校准提供了一个快速且计算高效的框架，支持重复的近实时推断和快速疫情分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational burden of Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) in calibrating high-dimensional epidemiological models for real-time outbreak analysis, this study evaluates simulation-based inference (SBI) using neural posterior estimation as a faster alternative. Applied to a SECIR model calibrated with German ICU data during the 2020 COVID-19 pandemic, SBI was tested across 31-day and 201-day inference windows, including multiple transmission change points. Results show SBI produces posterior distributions and predictive trajectories in strong agreement with MCMC, while reducing runtime from ~1000 seconds to ~60–70 seconds for 31-day windows using a single GPU, and from ~19,000 seconds to ~157 seconds for the 201-day problem, demonstrating its scalability and efficiency for near-real-time epidemiological inference.
为解决马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）在高维非线性流行病学模型中计算成本高的问题，本研究评估了基于模拟的推理（SBI）方法，采用神经后验估计作为替代方案。该方法应用于使用德国2020年新冠ICU数据校准的SECIR模型，并与MCMC在31天和201天两个时间窗口下进行对比。结果显示，在31天窗口中，SBI准确恢复后验分布并重现观察到的ICU轨迹，其Wasserstein距离和KL散度与MCMC高度一致；在201天窗口中，SBI仍保持主要后验结构，尽管不确定性增加。SBI在单个GPU上完成31天推理仅需60–70秒（MCMC需1000秒），201天推理平均仅需157秒（MCMC需超19,000秒），显著提升计算效率且不损失精度。
Recovering Governing Equations from Solution Data: Identifiability Bounds for Linear and Nonlinear ODEs
Authors: Yang Pan, Helmut Bölcskei
First: 2026-06-25T17:02:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:02:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning governing equations from observed solution data is a fundamental challenge in scientific machine learning \cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}, yet the theoretical conditions under which a ground-truth ODE can be uniquely and stably identified from multiple solution observations remain largely undeveloped, and no quantitative analysis of the sample complexity of such learning tasks exists in the literature. To address this gap, we introduce the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as the natural metric for comparing differential equations, since it captures the worst-case separation between two equations over all admissible initial conditions and thus encodes the minimax structure of the identification problem. We establish identifiability bounds for governing ODEs across a wide class of structure equations--ranging from linear ODEs to nonlinear classes with Lipschitz (Hölder)-continuous vector fields--characterizing precisely when two distinct equations can be distinguished from solution data. Using this metric, we derive metric entropy estimates for the relevant ODE classes and analyze sample complexity bounds, quantifying how many solution observations are needed to reliably recover the governing equation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从解数据恢复控制方程：线性和非线性常微分方程的可识别性界限
从观测到的解数据中学习控制方程是科学机器学习中的基本挑战\cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}，但关于在何种理论条件下，从多个解观测中唯一且稳定地识别出真实常微分方程，目前仍缺乏系统研究，且文献中尚无此类学习任务的样本复杂度定量分析。为填补这一空白，我们引入解集上的豪斯多夫距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，因为它捕捉了两个方程在所有可允许初值下的最坏情况分离，从而编码了识别问题的最小最大结构。我们为广泛结构方程类（从线性常微分方程到具有利普希茨（赫尔德）连续向量场的非线性类）建立了控制方程的可识别性界限，精确刻画了在何种条件下两个不同方程可从解数据中区分。利用该度量，我们推导了相关常微分方程类的度量熵估计，并分析了样本复杂度界限，量化了可靠恢复控制方程所需解观测的数量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to theoretically understand when governing ODEs can be uniquely identified from solution data, this work introduces the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as a natural metric to capture the minimax structure of the identification problem. Using this metric, the authors derive identifiability bounds for both linear and nonlinear ODEs with Lipschitz or Hölder-continuous vector fields, specifying conditions under which distinct equations can be distinguished. They further establish metric entropy estimates and sample complexity bounds, quantifying the number of solution observations required to reliably recover the true governing equation.
为理解从解数据中唯一稳定识别微分方程的理论条件，本文引入解集上的豪斯多夫距离作为自然度量，以捕捉所有初值条件下两方程间的最坏情况分离。基于该度量，作者推导出线性及具有利普希茨或赫尔德连续向量场的非线性ODE的可识别性界限，明确区分不同方程所需的条件。进一步，他们建立度量熵估计与样本复杂度界限，量化可靠恢复真实控制方程所需的最少解观测次数。
"Everyone Says Them": Deception Typologies, Probabilistic Trust, and Grassroots Safety Knowledge Among Gay Dating App Users in China
Authors: Yibo Meng, Lyumanshan Ye, Yingfangzhong Sun, Bingyi Liu, Huidi Lu, Xiaolan Ding
First: 2026-06-25T17:00:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T17:00:59+00:00
Comments: Accept to CSCW 26 EA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gay dating applications have become critical platforms for sexual minority men to seek relationships and community, yet they also expose users to deceptive interactions that remain underexplored in HCI and CSCW research. This study examines how gay male users in China experience, identify, and respond to deception on dating applications. Through semi-structured interviews with 22 participants across platforms including Blued, Aloha, Fanka, and Soul, we make three contributions. First, we identify a typology of deceptive practices extending beyond profile misrepresentation to encompass relational, emotional, financial, and commercial forms of deception. Second, we document the layered, probabilistic verification strategies users develop through long-term platform use, showing that trust assessment operates as a multi-signal, provisional process rather than a binary judgment. Third, we demonstrate that risk recognition is a collaborative practice shaped by the circulation of experience, the abstraction of recurrent tactics, and the codification of shared rules within the community.
