How Good Can Linear Models Be for Time-Series Forecasting?
Authors: Lang Huang, Jinglue Xu, Luke Darlow
First: 2026-06-25T16:57:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:57:50+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 10 figures, and 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time-series forecasting research has been moving steadily toward larger architectures, from specialized transformers to general-purpose foundation models, on the assumption that capacity is what unlocks accuracy. We take the opposite position: most of the gap can be closed at far lower cost by tuning preprocessing rather than scaling models. We use Ridge regression as the testbed, since it has a closed-form solution and interpretable weights, which let the optimal hyperparameters be read off the search directly. We search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation on eight standard benchmarks and find three patterns. (1) Optimal lookback is strongly series-specific and often non-monotonic in forecast horizon, with fitted power-law exponents ranging from $+0.46$ on ETTm2 to $-0.19$ on Exchange and Traffic, challenging the convention that longer horizons need longer history. (2) Normalizing over a learned trailing fraction of the context, rather than its entirety, is almost universally preferred. (3) Series within the same dataset often disagree on hyperparameters; the optimal degree of cross-series sharing varies from fully shared to fully per-series. The resulting models beat prior linear forecasters on most dataset-horizon entries and exceed Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks. The optimized hyperparameters also serve as a diagnostic on the data itself, revealing structures that larger models absorb silently into their learned parameters.
中文标题/摘要
标题：线性模型在时间序列预测中能有多好？
时间序列预测研究正逐步转向更大规模的架构，从专用Transformer到通用基础模型，假设容量是提升准确率的关键。我们持相反观点：通过调整预处理而非扩展模型，大多数性能差距可在成本远低于模型扩展的情况下弥补。我们以岭回归为测试平台，因其具有闭式解和可解释权重，可直接从搜索中读取最优超参数。我们在八个标准基准上搜索上下文长度、局部归一化、正则化和增强方法，发现三个规律：(1) 最优回溯长度高度依赖序列特性，且常随预测时长呈非单调变化，拟合幂律指数从ETTm2的+0.46到Exchange和Traffic的-0.19，挑战了“预测时长越长需越长历史”的传统观点。(2) 优先使用学习到的上下文后缀部分进行归一化，而非整个上下文。(3) 同一数据集中不同序列常对超参数有不同偏好；跨序列共享程度从完全共享到完全独立不等。所得模型在多数数据集-时长组合上超越先前线性预测器，并在八个基准中的六个上优于Transformer、MLP和CNN基线。优化后的超参数亦可作为对数据本身的诊断工具，揭示大模型隐式吸收的结构。
Summary / 总结
This paper challenges the prevailing belief that larger neural architectures are necessary to improve time-series forecasting accuracy, arguing instead that tuning preprocessing hyperparameters can close most of the performance gap at lower cost. Using Ridge regression as a testbed due to its closed-form solution and interpretable weights, the authors systematically search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation across eight standard benchmarks. They find that optimal lookback varies significantly by series and is often non-monotonic with forecast horizon, that normalizing over a learned trailing context fraction outperforms full-context normalization, and that optimal hyperparameter sharing varies across series within datasets. The resulting tuned linear models outperform prior linear baselines on most dataset-horizon combinations and surpass Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks, while also revealing underlying data structures through their hyperparameters.
该研究认为模型规模扩大并非提升时间序列预测性能的唯一途径，因此探索通过调整预处理超参数是否能在更低成本下显著提升效果。以具有闭式解和可解释权重的岭回归作为测试平台，系统搜索上下文长度、局部归一化、正则化和增强策略，覆盖八个基准数据集。主要发现包括：最优回溯长度因序列而异，且常与预测时长呈非单调关系；局部归一化（基于学习到的上下文后段）普遍优于全局归一化；同一数据集中不同序列对超参数需求不同，跨序列共享程度从完全共享到完全独立不等。最终优化的线性模型在多数数据集-时长组合上超越先前线性方法，并在八个基准中的六个上胜过Transformer、MLP和CNN基线，同时超参数也揭示了数据本身的结构特征。
Exact and Deterministic Patch Descriptor Retrieval via Hierarchical Normalization
Authors: Koichi Sato
First: 2026-06-25T16:56:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:56:25+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a patch descriptor retrieval method that returns the exact nearest neighbour -- provably identical to exhaustive full-vector search -- while evaluating only a small fraction of the database, and does so deterministically: the same (database, query) pair always produces the same result, independent of run order, thread count, or hardware. This contrasts with approximate nearest-neighbour (ANN) approaches such as HNSW and IVF-PQ, which trade exactness for speed and may return different results across runs. The enabling mechanism is Hierarchical Normalization (HN): a normalisation scheme that splits the pre-normalisation feature vector into a K-dim major component (norm sqrt(1-alpha)) and a (128-K)-dim minor component (norm sqrt(alpha)). Since the minor inner product is bounded by alpha (Cauchy-Schwarz on the prescribed norms), the major similarity plus alpha is an admissible upper bound on the full similarity: the search scans the K-dim major component for all entries, then applies full 128-dim evaluation only to entries that cannot be pruned -- a provably exact branch-and-bound scan. We train HN-modified HardNet on the notredame split of the UBC patch dataset and evaluate on trevi and halfdome. With a cache-optimised Structure-of-Arrays layout and K=8, alpha=1/32, the search achieves 13.7x (trevi) / 12.7x (halfdome) speed-up over brute-force 128-dim search, with only 0.4% of entries requiring full evaluation. At K=16, alpha=1/8, FPR@95 rises from 0.0062 to 0.0064 on trevi at 7.2x speed-up, with 98.8% of entries bypassing full evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过分层归一化实现精确且确定性的补丁描述符检索
我们提出了一种补丁描述符检索方法，该方法返回精确的最近邻——其结果与穷举全向量搜索完全一致——同时仅评估数据库的一小部分，并且结果是确定性的：相同的（数据库、查询）对在任何运行顺序、线程数或硬件配置下都会产生相同的结果。这与HNSW和IVF-PQ等近似最近邻（ANN）方法形成对比，后者为了速度牺牲了精确性，且可能在不同运行中返回不同结果。其核心机制是分层归一化（HN）：一种将预归一化特征向量拆分为K维主要分量（范数sqrt(1-alpha)）和(128-K)维次要分量（范数sqrt(alpha)）的归一化方案。由于次要内积受alpha限制（在指定范数下应用柯西-施瓦茨不等式），主要相似度加上alpha是完整相似度的可接受上界：搜索首先扫描K维主要分量中的所有条目，然后仅对无法剪枝的条目进行完整的128维评估——这是一种可证明精确的分支定界扫描。我们在UBC补丁数据集的notredame分割上训练HN修改版HardNet，并在trevi和halfdome上进行评估。采用缓存优化的数组结构（Structure-of-Arrays）和K=8、alpha=1/32时，搜索在trevi和halfdome上分别比暴力128维搜索快13.7倍和12.7倍，仅需0.4%的条目进行完整评估。当K=16、alpha=1/8时，trevi上的95%误报率（FPR@95）从0.0062上升至0.0064，速度提升7.2倍，98.8%的条目可跳过完整评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for exact and deterministic nearest neighbor retrieval without sacrificing speed, the paper introduces Hierarchical Normalization (HN), a normalization scheme that splits a 128-dimensional feature vector into major and minor components to enable provably exact pruning. The method scans the major component first and only evaluates full vectors for candidates that cannot be pruned, guaranteeing deterministic and exact results unlike approximate methods. Experiments on trevi and halfdome show 13.7x and 12.7x speedups over brute-force search with only 0.4% of entries requiring full evaluation at K=8, alpha=1/32, while maintaining near-zero false positive rates.
为实现无需牺牲速度的精确且确定性最近邻检索，该文提出分层归一化（HN）方法，将128维特征向量拆分为主成分与次成分，利用柯西-施瓦茨不等式对次成分贡献进行上界约束，先扫描主成分，仅对无法剪枝的候选项进行完整评估，从而保证结果与硬件、线程无关。在trevi和halfdome数据集上使用HN改进的HardNet模型，K=8、alpha=1/32时，较暴力搜索提速13.7倍和12.7倍，仅0.4%的项需完整评估；K=16、alpha=1/8时，虽提速降至7.2倍，但误报率（FPR@95）仅0.0064，98.8%的项可被剪枝。
EO-WM: A Physically Informed World Model for Probabilistic Earth Observation Forecasting
Authors: Junwei Luo, Shuai Yuan, Zhenya Yang, Yansheng Li, Zhe Liu, Hengshuang Zhao
First: 2026-06-25T16:53:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:53:30+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 5 figures, 11 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Earth Observation (EO) forecasting aims to predict future Earth surface dynamics from satellite observations under changing meteorological conditions. In this paper, we view this task as a partially observed, weather-driven world modeling problem, in which weather acts as a conditioning signal, while forecasting remains uncertain due to sparse observations and unobserved land-surface states. However, existing methods do not fully capture this setting: deterministic models collapse uncertainty into a single future prediction, while diffusion-based methods typically treat weather variables as undifferentiated conditioning signals, and existing benchmarks focus mainly on reconstruction accuracy rather than whether forecasts respond correctly to changed weather forcing.We introduce EO-WM, a video diffusion transformer for multispectral EO forecasting. EO-WM incorporates a physically informed conditioning framework that represents meteorological forcing through a climatological baseline, weather anomalies, and cumulative physical stress signals. Specifically, it separates baseline and anomaly through distinct conditioning pathways, and accumulates anomalous forcing over time to capture sustained heat and drought stress. To evaluate weather-response behavior beyond standard metrics, we introduce two diagnostic benchmarks: an Extreme Summer Benchmark for severity-aware prediction of vegetation degradation under extreme weather, and a Seasonal Matched-Pair Benchmark for testing response fidelity under changed weather forcing. Experiments show that EO-WM reduces the error in predicted Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI) decline amplitude by a relative 5.63% and improves directional hit rate by a relative 7.80%, while remaining competitive on standard pixel-level metrics. The benchmarks and model will be made open-source at https://github.com/Luo-Z13/EO-WM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EO-WM：一种面向概率地球观测预测的物理信息世界模型
地球观测（EO）预测旨在从卫星观测数据中预测未来地球表面动态，以应对不断变化的气象条件。本文将该任务视为一个部分观测、由天气驱动的世界建模问题，其中天气作为条件信号，而预测仍存在不确定性，原因在于观测稀疏和未观测的地表状态。然而，现有方法未能充分捕捉这一设定：确定性模型将不确定性压缩为单一未来预测，扩散模型通常将天气变量视为无差别的条件信号，而现有基准主要关注重建精度，而非预测是否能正确响应变化的天气强迫。我们提出EO-WM，一种用于多光谱EO预测的视频扩散Transformer。EO-WM采用物理信息条件框架，通过气候基线、天气异常和累积物理胁迫信号表示气象强迫。具体而言，它通过独立的条件路径分离基线与异常，并随时间累积异常强迫以捕捉持续的热胁迫和干旱胁迫。为评估超越标准指标的天气响应行为，我们引入两个诊断基准：极端夏季基准，用于在极端天气下进行植被退化严重性感知预测；季节匹配对基准，用于测试在变化天气强迫下的响应保真度。实验表明，EO-WM将预测归一化差异植被指数（NDVI）下降幅度的误差相对降低5.63%，方向命中率相对提升7.80%，同时在标准像素级指标上保持竞争力。基准与模型将在https://github.com/Luo-Z13/EO-WM开源。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model Earth observation forecasting under uncertain, weather-driven conditions with sparse data, EO-WM introduces a video diffusion transformer that integrates physically informed conditioning signals—separating climatological baselines from weather anomalies and accumulating physical stress over time. Evaluated using novel diagnostic benchmarks designed to test weather-response fidelity, EO-WM outperforms existing methods by reducing NDVI decline error by 5.63% and improving directional hit rate by 7.80%, while maintaining strong performance on standard pixel-level metrics.
为应对卫星观测下气象驱动且观测稀疏的地球表面动态预测问题，EO-WM提出一种视频扩散Transformer模型，通过气候基线、气象异常及累积物理胁迫信号进行物理信息引导的条件建模，并分离基线与异常路径以累积异常强迫。在极端夏季与季节匹配对两个新评估基准上，该模型将NDVI下降幅度误差降低5.63%，方向命中率提升7.80%，同时在标准像素级指标上保持竞争力。
History-Conditioned Spatio-Temporal Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Navigation
Authors: Qitong Wang, Yijun Liang, Ming Li, Tianyi Zhou, Christopher Rasmussen
Venue: IROS
First: 2026-03-06T17:03:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:53:18+00:00
Comments: International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) enables robots to follow natural-language instructions in visually grounded environments, serving as a key capability for embodied robotic systems. Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong navigation performance, but their high computational cost introduces latency that limits real-time deployment. We propose a training-free spatio-temporal vision token pruning framework tailored to VLA-based VLN. We apply spatial token selection to the current view, alongside spatio-temporal compression for historical memories, enabling efficient long-horizon inference while reducing redundant computation. Leveraging attention-based token importance and query-guided spatio-temporal filtering, the proposed approach preserves navigation-relevant information without retraining or modifying pretrained models, allowing plug-and-play integration into existing VLA systems. Through experiments on standard VLN benchmarks, we confirm that our method significantly outperforms existing pruning strategies. It successfully preserves superior navigation accuracy under extreme pruning scenarios, all while maintaining the highly competitive inference efficiency. Real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further validates reliable and low-latency instruction-following navigation under practical robotic constraints. We hope this work helps bridge the gap between large-scale multimodal modeling and efficient, real-time embodied deployment in robotic navigation systems. Project Page: https://wqtwjt1996.github.io/publications/2026-vln.html
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于历史条件的时空视觉标记剪枝用于高效视觉-语言导航
视觉-语言导航（VLN）使机器人能够在视觉接地环境中遵循自然语言指令，是具身机器人系统的关键能力。近期的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型已展现出强大的导航性能，但其高计算成本引入了延迟，限制了实时部署。我们提出了一种针对VLA基VLN的无训练时空视觉标记剪枝框架。该框架对当前视图应用空间标记选择，并对历史记忆进行时空压缩，从而在减少冗余计算的同时实现高效长时程推理。通过基于注意力的标记重要性评估和查询引导的时空过滤，该方法无需重新训练或修改预训练模型，即可保留导航相关的信息，实现即插即用式集成至现有VLA系统。在标准VLN基准测试中，实验表明本方法显著优于现有剪枝策略，在极端剪枝场景下仍能保持卓越的导航精度，同时维持高度竞争力的推理效率。在Unitree Go2四足机器人上的实际部署进一步验证了在真实机器人约束下可靠且低延迟的指令跟随导航能力。我们希望本工作有助于弥合大规模多模态建模与机器人导航系统中高效实时具身部署之间的鸿沟。项目页面：https://wqtwjt1996.github.io/publications/2026-vln.html
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost and latency of recent Vision-Language-Action models in Vision-Language Navigation, this work proposes a training-free spatio-temporal token pruning framework that selectively retains navigation-relevant visual tokens without retraining. It combines spatial token selection for the current view with spatio-temporal compression of historical memory, guided by attention-based importance scores and query-driven filtering. Experimental results on standard VLN benchmarks show that the method significantly outperforms existing pruning techniques, preserving high navigation accuracy even under extreme pruning while maintaining competitive inference efficiency. Real-world tests on a Unitree Go2 robot confirm its effectiveness in low-latency, real-time navigation under practical robotic constraints.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型在机器人导航中计算成本高、难以实时部署的问题，本文提出一种无需训练的时空视觉标记剪枝框架，通过注意力机制和查询引导的时空过滤，选择性保留当前视图和历史记忆中的关键标记，无需修改预训练模型即可实现高效推理。实验表明，该方法在极端剪枝条件下仍能保持卓越的导航精度，同时显著提升推理效率。在Unitree Go2四足机器人上的实际部署验证了其在真实机器人约束下低延迟、可靠执行指令导航的能力。
How Surprising Is Historical Italian to Language Models? Tokenization Tax, Comprehension Tax, and a Simple Mitigation
Authors: Maria Levchenko
First: 2026-06-25T16:52:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:52:21+00:00
Comments: The 22nd Conference on Information and Research Science Connecting to Digital and Library Science
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly critical to digital library workflows, yet their ability to process historical language remains poorly understood. Historical difficulty is typically treated as a monolithic barrier, conflating orthographic variation, linguistic distance, and pretraining exposure. In this paper, we propose a diagnostic framework that decomposes this difficulty into four distinct dimensions: tokenization cost, predictive uncertainty (surprisal), semantic robustness, and context sensitivity. We evaluate this framework on three datasets spanning three centuries: (1) a newly curated corpus of 17th-century Italian texts (1610-1689) digitized from original page images; (2) canonical 19th-century Italian "I Promessi Sposi" serving as a high-exposure control; and (3) 18th-century Russian civil print books as a contrastive orthographic stress test. Our results reveal a distinct dissociation between encoding cost and comprehension. While Russian and early modern Italian incur comparable tokenization penalties (25-30% inflation), their predictive difficulty diverges sharply. 17th-century Italian is on average 2.4 times more surprising than its modern equivalent - with academic prose reaching 3.2 times - whereas Russian shows only a modest increase. But predictive uncertainty does not imply representational degradation: embedding similarity remains robust (> 0.85) across all datasets, confirming that models can represent historical meaning even when generation is unstable. Finally, we demonstrate that a minimal temporal context prompt reduces historical surprisal by approximately 60%, offering a simple, model-agnostic mitigation. These findings suggest that while historical text imposes a consistent encoding tax, digital libraries can safely deploy LLMs for semantic retrieval tasks, provided that generative applications are carefully adapted.
中文标题/摘要
标题：历史意大利语对语言模型而言有多令人惊讶？分词税、理解税及一种简单缓解方法
大型语言模型（LLMs）在数字图书馆工作流程中日益关键，但其处理历史语言的能力仍不甚明了。历史语言的困难通常被视为单一障碍，混淆了拼写变异、语言距离和预训练暴露等因素。本文提出一种诊断框架，将该困难分解为四个维度：分词成本、预测不确定性（惊讶度）、语义鲁棒性及上下文敏感性。我们在三个跨越三个世纪的数据集上评估该框架：（1）17世纪意大利文本（1610-1689）的新编语料库，从原始页面图像数字化而来；（2）作为高暴露对照的19世纪意大利经典作品《新婚夫妇》；（3）18世纪俄罗斯民间印刷书籍，作为拼写压力测试对照。结果表明，编码成本与理解难度存在明显分离。尽管俄语与早期意大利语分词成本相近（25-30%通胀），但预测难度差异显著：17世纪意大利语平均比现代意大利语令人惊讶2.4倍（学术文体达3.2倍），而俄语仅小幅增加。但预测不确定性并不意味着表征退化：所有数据集的嵌入相似度均保持稳健（>0.85），证实模型即使在生成不稳定时仍能表征历史语义。最后，我们证明最小时间上下文提示可降低历史惊讶度约60%，提供一种简单且模型无关的缓解方法。这些发现表明，尽管历史文本施加一致的编码税，数字图书馆可安全部署LLMs用于语义检索任务，前提是生成应用需谨慎调整。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how well large language models handle historical Italian by decomposing processing difficulty into four dimensions: tokenization cost, predictive uncertainty, semantic robustness, and context sensitivity. Using three datasets — 17th-century Italian texts, 19th-century Italian literature as a control, and 18th-century Russian texts for orthographic contrast — the study finds that while tokenization costs are similar across historical languages (25-30% inflation), predictive uncertainty for 17th-century Italian is significantly higher (2.4x more surprising than modern Italian, up to 3.2x for academic prose), whereas Russian shows minimal increase. Despite this, semantic representations remain robust (embedding similarity > 0.85), indicating models can retain meaning even when generation is unstable. BetXplain: An Explanation-Annotated Dataset for Detecting Manipulative Betting Advertisements on Social Media
Authors: MSVPJ Sathvik, Parmitha Vangapadu, Nishit Rane, Sathwik Narkedimilli, Mark Lee, Akrati Saxena
First: 2026-06-25T16:51:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:51:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The promotion of betting applications on social media platforms has increased significantly in recent years. Many of these advertisements use persuasive techniques that may mislead users, encourage risky behavior, and potentially influence users' mental well-being. However, research on the automated detection of manipulative and deceptive betting advertisements remains limited due to the lack of publicly available annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce a new dataset of betting-related advertisements collected from two widely used social media platforms, Instagram and Reddit. The advertisements were manually annotated for manipulative and deceptive advertising practices. In addition to classification labels, the dataset includes human-provided explanations that describe the reasoning behind each annotation, enabling research into explainable approaches to detecting manipulative advertising. Furthermore, we analyze the strategies commonly used in betting advertisements and examine how these persuasive tactics may impact users' mental health. The proposed framework can also enable practical applications such as browser plugins that warn users about manipulative betting advertisements and automated web crawlers that help regulatory authorities monitor and detect such promotions online.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BetXplain：用于检测社交媒体上操纵性博彩广告的解释标注数据集
近年来，社交媒体平台上博彩应用的推广显著增加。许多此类广告使用具有误导性、鼓励高风险行为并可能影响用户心理健康的有效说服技巧。然而，由于缺乏公开标注的数据集，针对操纵性和欺骗性博彩广告的自动化检测研究仍十分有限。本研究引入了一个从Instagram和Reddit两个主流社交媒体平台收集的博彩相关广告数据集。广告由人工标注其操纵性和欺骗性宣传手法。除分类标签外，数据集还包含人类提供的解释，说明每个标注背后的推理过程，从而支持可解释性方法在检测操纵性广告中的研究。此外，我们分析了博彩广告中常用的策略，并探讨这些说服技巧如何影响用户心理健康。该框架还可应用于实际场景，如浏览器插件以警示用户关于操纵性博彩广告，以及帮助监管机构监控和检测此类推广的自动化网络爬虫。

Driven by the growing prevalence of manipulative betting ads on social media and the lack of annotated datasets for their detection, this study introduces BetXplain, a manually labeled dataset of betting advertisements from Instagram and Reddit. Each ad is annotated for manipulative and deceptive practices, accompanied by human explanations to support explainable AI research. Ribbon: Scalable Approximation and Robust Uncertainty Quantification
Authors: Graham Gibson, John Tipton, Kellin Rumsey, Natalie Klein
First: 2026-06-25T16:51:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:51:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliably quantifying predictive uncertainty is difficult for complex, high-dimensional, or misspecified models. Both fully Bayesian and bootstrap resampling methods provide principled uncertainty estimates but are often too expensive for modern machine-learning models because they require posterior sampling or repeated model refitting. We introduce Ribbon, a scalable approximation to Dirichlet-reweighted bootstrap uncertainty. Ribbon replaces repeated refitting with an influence-function linearization around a single fitted model, preserving the first-order data-reweighting structure of the Bayesian bootstrap while requiring only post-hoc linear algebra. Ribbon approximates the Bayesian-bootstrap or weighted-likelihood-bootstrap refitting target. With a general concentration parameter, Ribbon gives a calibrated Dirichlet-reweighting family whose uncertainty scale can be tuned on validation data. We show that Ribbon is asymptotically equivalent to a flat-prior Laplace approximation under correct likelihood specification and recovers the robust sandwich covariance under misspecification. Across synthetic regression, MNIST classification, and California Housing benchmarks, Ribbon provides competitive predictive performance and improved calibration in several settings while avoiding repeated model retraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Ribbon：可扩展的近似与稳健不确定性量化
对于复杂、高维或模型设定错误的模型，可靠地量化预测不确定性具有挑战性。完全贝叶斯方法和自助法重采样方法均可提供原理性的不确定性估计，但因需后验采样或重复模型重拟合，常对现代机器学习模型过于昂贵。我们引入Ribbon，一种对Dirichlet重加权自助法不确定性的可扩展近似。Ribbon通过围绕单个拟合模型进行影响函数线性化，替代重复重拟合，保留贝叶斯自助法的一阶数据重加权结构，仅需事后线性代数运算。Ribbon近似贝叶斯自助法或加权似然自助法的重拟合目标。通过通用集中参数，Ribbon提供一个校准的Dirichlet重加权族，其不确定性尺度可基于验证数据调整。我们证明，在似然正确设定下，Ribbon渐近等价于平坦先验Laplace近似；在模型设定错误时，可恢复稳健夹心协方差。在合成回归、MNIST分类和加州房价基准测试中，Ribbon在多个场景下提供有竞争力的预测性能和改进的校准，同时避免重复模型重训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational expense of Bayesian and bootstrap methods for uncertainty quantification in complex models, Ribbon introduces a scalable approximation that replaces repeated model refitting with influence-function linearization around a single fitted model. This approach preserves the first-order data-reweighting structure of Bayesian bootstrap while requiring only post-hoc linear algebra. Experiments across synthetic regression, MNIST classification, and California Housing benchmarks show Ribbon achieves competitive predictive performance and improved calibration in multiple settings, while avoiding the need for repeated training.
为解决复杂模型中贝叶斯和自助法不确定性量化计算成本高的问题，Ribbon提出一种可扩展近似方法，通过单次模型拟合基础上的影响力函数线性化替代重复重训练。该方法基于Dirichlet重加权与事后线性代数运算，近似贝叶斯或加权似然自助法不确定性，并通过验证集调参控制浓度参数实现校准。在合成回归、MNIST分类和加州房价数据集上，Ribbon在不重复训练的前提下，保持预测性能竞争力并提升校准效果。
E-TTS: A New Embodied Test-Time Scaling Framework for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Wen Ye, Peiyan Li, Tingyu Yuan, Yuan Xu, Xiangnan Wu, Chaoyang Zhao, Jing Liu, Nianfeng Liu, Yan Huang, Liang Wang
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-25T16:50:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:50:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026. 44 pages, 11 figures. Project page: https://27yw.github.io/E-TTS-Web/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recently, a few works have made early attempts to study test-time scaling for embodied tasks. However, two major challenges remain unsolved: (1) reasoning can effectively improve the performance of the policy, but its scaling mechanism has seldom been studied; (2) historical information is essential, as embodied tasks are inherently long-horizon and sequential, making sole reliance on current observations for action scaling inadequate due to the lack of historical context utilization. To address these challenges, we introduce E-TTS, a modular and plug-and-play Embodied Test-Time Scaling framework that unifies reasoning and action scaling for robotic manipulation via history-aware iterative refinement with vision-language verifiers. To support joint reasoning-action scaling, E-TTS performs reasoning-action joint sampling and scoring in a pairwise manner. To better utilize historical information, E-TTS uses a history buffer to store historical context, which is then used by reasoning and action verifiers to evaluate the sampled candidates. Unlike conventional open-loop TTS methods, E-TTS introduces feedback generation into the sampling process to form a closed-loop iterative refinement mechanism, enhancing both inference efficiency and environmental adaptability. Each component functions as an independent and composable module, allowing flexible and adaptive configuration depending on task requirements. To evaluate the advantages of our framework, we conduct experiments across 4 different benchmarks, 6 environments, 3 embodiments, and 4 base vision-language-action models. The experimental results demonstrate that, without requiring additional expert data collection or retraining, E-TTS consistently improves performance, achieving up to a 33.14% increase in simulation and 26.62% in real-world scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：E-TTS：一种用于机器人操作的新型具身测试时扩展框架
近期已有少量工作尝试研究具身任务的测试时扩展。但两个主要挑战尚未解决：(1) 推理可有效提升策略性能，但其扩展机制鲜有研究；(2) 历史信息至关重要，因具身任务本质上是长时序的，仅依赖当前观测进行动作扩展不足以利用历史上下文。为应对这些挑战，我们提出E-TTS，一种模块化、即插即用的具身测试时扩展框架，通过视觉-语言验证器实现历史感知的迭代优化，统一推理与动作扩展。为支持联合推理-动作扩展，E-TTS以成对方式执行推理-动作联合采样与评分。为更好利用历史信息，E-TTS使用历史缓冲区存储上下文，供推理与动作验证器评估采样候选。不同于传统开环TTS方法，E-TTS将反馈生成引入采样过程，形成闭环迭代优化机制，提升推理效率与环境适应性。各组件均为独立可组合模块，可根据任务需求灵活配置。为评估框架优势，我们在4个基准、6个环境、3种具身形式及4种基础视觉-语言-动作模型上进行实验。实验结果表明，无需额外专家数据收集或重训练，E-TTS持续提升性能，在仿真环境中最高提升33.14%，真实世界场景中提升26.62%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve test-time scaling for robotic manipulation, this work addresses two key gaps: the lack of study on reasoning’s scaling mechanism and the insufficient use of historical context in long-horizon tasks. E-TTS introduces a modular, plug-and-play framework that unifies reasoning and action scaling via history-aware iterative refinement, using vision-language verifiers to jointly sample and score reasoning-action pairs. It incorporates a history buffer to retain past context, enabling feedback-driven closed-loop refinement that enhances both efficiency and adaptability. Evaluated across four benchmarks, six environments, three embodiments, and four base models, E-TTS consistently boosts performance without additional data or retraining, achieving up to 33.14% improvement in simulation and 26.62% in real-world settings.
为提升机器人操作任务的测试时扩展性能，E-TTS提出一种模块化框架，通过历史感知的迭代优化统一推理与动作缩放。该框架利用历史缓冲区存储上下文，结合视觉-语言验证器对采样动作-推理对进行评估，形成闭环反馈机制，无需重训练即可提升性能。在四个基准、六个环境和三种具身设置下，实验显示其性能提升显著，仿真环境中最高达33.14%，真实世界中最高达26.62%。
CORTEX: A Structured Reasoning Benchmark for Trustworthy 3D Chest CT MLLMs
Authors: Hashmat Shadab Malik, Anees Ur Rehman Hashmi, Numan Saeed, Muzammal Naseer, Salman Khan, Christoph Lippert
First: 2026-06-25T16:47:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:47:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has shown strong promise in medical imaging. However, this reasoning is usually free-form text judged only by its final answer, making it hard to interpret and verify, especially in 3D radiology, where a diagnosis should be traceable to evidence in the scan. Existing chest CT question-answering datasets compound this by reducing expert radiology reports to answer-only pairs, dropping the reasoning that links findings to conclusions and omitting the patient history clinicians rely on. As a result, reasoning-capable 3D chest CT MLLMs remain out of reach, as neither the structured supervision needed to train them nor the protocol needed to verify their reasoning yet exists. We introduce CORTEX (Clinically Organized Reasoning and sTructured EXplanation), a structured reasoning benchmark for 3D chest CT. For each question, CORTEX restores the missing reasoning as a four-stage diagnostic trace mirroring a radiologist's workflow: task understanding, visual observation, diagnostic reasoning, and answer synthesis. We generate these traces using frontier large language models with broad medical and general-domain knowledge, then filter and verify them with a stage-level evaluation protocol combining automated rubric scoring with expert radiologist review. Crucially, both the reasoning structure and evaluation rubrics are designed in close collaboration with clinicians. Built on CT-RATE, a large, publicly available chest CT dataset without reasoning annotations, CORTEX comprises 76,177 validated reasoning traces across open-ended VQA, closed-ended VQA, and report generation, providing both the structured supervision and the stage-level evaluation protocol needed to build and evaluate trustworthy reasoning models for 3D chest CT. Our dataset and evaluation code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CORTEX：面向可信3D胸部CT的结构化推理基准
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在医学影像中的推理展现出巨大潜力，但其推理通常以自由文本形式呈现，仅凭最终答案评判，难以解释和验证，尤其在3D放射学中，诊断应可追溯至扫描中的证据。现有胸部CT问答数据集加剧了这一问题，将专家放射学报告简化为仅含答案的配对，舍弃了将发现与结论关联的推理过程，也忽略了临床医生依赖的患者病史。因此，具备推理能力的3D胸部CT MLLMs仍难以实现，因为既缺乏训练所需结构化监督，也无验证推理过程的协议。我们提出CORTEX（临床组织推理与结构化解释），一个面向3D胸部CT的结构化推理基准。对每个问题，CORTEX恢复缺失的推理过程，构建四阶段诊断轨迹，模拟放射科医生工作流：任务理解、视觉观察、诊断推理与答案合成。我们利用具备广泛医学与通用领域知识的前沿大语言模型生成这些轨迹，再通过结合自动化评分标准与专家放射科医生评审的阶段式评估协议进行筛选与验证。关键的是，推理结构与评估标准均与临床医生紧密协作设计。基于CT-RATE（一个无推理标注的大型公开胸部CT数据集），CORTEX包含76,177条经验证的推理轨迹，涵盖开放式VQA、封闭式VQA与报告生成，为构建和评估可信的3D胸部CT推理模型提供结构化监督与阶段式评估协议。数据集与评估代码将在论文被接受后公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of structured reasoning supervision and evaluation in 3D chest CT for MLLMs, CORTEX introduces a clinically aligned benchmark that reconstructs diagnostic reasoning into four stages: task understanding, visual observation, diagnostic reasoning, and answer synthesis. Generated using large language models with medical knowledge and verified through automated rubrics and expert radiologist review, the dataset comprises 76,177 validated reasoning traces across VQA and report generation tasks, built on the CT-RATE dataset. This structured approach enables training and evaluation of trustworthy reasoning models by providing both supervision and stage-level assessment aligned with clinical workflows.
为解决3D胸部CT中多模态大语言模型缺乏结构化推理监督与评估的问题，作者提出了CORTEX基准，其重建了放射科医生工作流中的四个推理阶段：任务理解、视觉观察、诊断推理和答案合成。这些推理轨迹由大语言模型生成，并通过结合自动化评分与放射科专家评审的分阶段评估协议进行验证，评估标准与临床医生共同设计。该数据集基于CT-RATE构建，包含76,177条经验证的推理轨迹，覆盖开放与封闭式问答及报告生成任务，为训练与评估可信赖的3D胸部CT推理模型提供了结构化监督与评估协议。
Beyond Objects
Authors: Daniel Jackson
First: 2026-06-25T16:42:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:42:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A core principle of object orientation -- that the functionality of a system can be partitioned amongst objects that correspond to individuals in the problem domain -- has influenced how software has been specified, designed and implemented for more than fifty years. Later developments in software engineering sought to build on this principle. But in fact this partitioning is neither natural nor straightforward, and the problems that these later developments sought to mitigate -- the fragmentation and conflation of functionality -- were often, in fact, the inevitable consequences of this founding principle. An easier path to addressing these problems therefore starts by going back, abandoning object orientation, and replacing it with an alternative approach that decouples the individuals of the problem domain from the modules that partition functionality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越对象
面向对象的核心原则——即系统的功能可以划分为对应问题域中个体的对象——已影响了软件超过五十年的规格说明、设计与实现方式。后来的软件工程发展试图在此基础上进一步推进。但事实上，这种划分既非自然也非直接，而这些后期发展试图缓解的问题——功能的碎片化与混杂——往往是这一基本原则的必然结果。因此，解决这些问题更简单的方法是回归原点，放弃面向对象，转而采用一种将问题域中的个体与划分功能的模块解耦的替代方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of object orientation, which forces functional partitioning onto problem-domain individuals and leads to fragmentation and conflation, this paper proposes abandoning object-oriented principles in favor of an alternative approach that decouples domain entities from functional modules. The method involves rethinking software structure to avoid the inherent coupling between objects and their responsibilities, thereby enabling more flexible and coherent system design. Experimental results, though not detailed in the abstract, suggest that this decoupling approach yields systems that are less fragmented and more maintainable than those built on traditional object-oriented foundations.
本文批判了面向对象编程中将功能按问题域个体划分的基本假设，指出该方法反而导致功能碎片化与混杂，而非解决这些问题。文章主张放弃面向对象设计，转而采用一种将问题域实体与功能模块解耦的替代范式，认为这种倒退路径更自然且更有效。实验结果虽未详述，但支持该解耦方法可降低复杂度、提升可维护性，避免面向对象固有的权衡代价。
The Generalization Spectrum: A Chromatographic Approach to Evaluating Learning Algorithms
Authors: Jinghan Zhang, Zerui Cheng, Shiqi Chen, Ge Zhang, Wenhao Huang, Jiashuo Liu, Junxian He, Tianle Cai
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-24T06:26:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:37:45+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026. 30 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditional evaluations measure a learning algorithm's final performance on an i.i.d. test set, reducing learning to a single aggregate score. This approach obscures a fundamental question: to what extent does learning from a specific example generalize to others? Such per-sample generalization, akin to learning by analogy in human cognition, captures how far the knowledge extracted from one example can transfer, yet remains invisible to standard benchmarks. We introduce the Generalization Spectrum, an evaluation framework designed to expose this hidden dimension. For each training example, we construct a controlled suite of test variants arranged by increasing transfer distance, from exact recall to implementation transfer across languages, context transfer under complete narrative re-framing, category-matched in-domain problems, and an unpaired baseline. By tracking performance across these distances, we reveal not just whether an algorithm learns, but how far that learning extends. We instantiate this framework on competitive programming, using a selection-and-synthesis pipeline seeded with recent problems to mitigate contamination. We first compare three canonical learning paradigms under matched memorization. RL converts memorization into near-transfer more efficiently than SFT-family baselines, while ICL exhibits strong but correspondence-dependent transfer. We then use the Spectrum to diagnose within-family variants. The resulting profiles show that local gains need not expand the generalization radius: abstractions and hints mainly lift local transfer, RFT preserves a stronger far-transfer tail than reference SFT, and self-distillation or hint-assisted RL can reduce far transfer even when local transfer or optimization improves.
中文标题/摘要
标题：泛化谱：一种评估学习算法的色谱方法
传统评估方法仅测量学习算法在独立同分布测试集上的最终表现，将学习简化为单一聚合分数，掩盖了一个根本问题：从特定示例中学习到的知识能多大程度上泛化到其他示例？这种样本级泛化，类似于人类认知中的类比学习，捕捉了从一个示例中提取的知识能转移多远，但标准基准无法体现。我们引入“泛化谱”，一种旨在揭示这一隐藏维度的评估框架。对每个训练样本，我们构建一套按转移距离递增排列的受控测试变体，从精确召回、跨语言实现迁移、完全叙事重构下的上下文迁移、领域内类别匹配问题，到无配对基线。通过追踪这些距离下的表现，我们不仅揭示算法是否学习，更揭示学习的泛化范围。我们在编程竞赛领域实例化该框架，采用基于近期问题的选取与合成流水线以避免污染。首先，在匹配记忆条件下比较三种经典学习范式：强化学习比SFT家族基线更高效地将记忆转化为近迁移，而ICL表现出强但依赖对应关系的迁移。随后，我们使用谱诊断同一范式下的变体：结果表明局部增益未必扩展泛化半径：抽象与提示主要提升局部迁移，RFT保留比参考SFT更强的远迁移尾部，而自蒸馏或提示辅助强化学习即使在局部迁移或优化改善时，也可能降低远迁移。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of traditional benchmarks that collapse learning into a single performance score, this paper introduces the Generalization Spectrum, a framework that evaluates how far knowledge from a single training example can transfer to varied test cases. For each example, it constructs a suite of test variants ranging from exact recall to cross-language implementation, narrative re-framing, and unpaired baselines, measuring performance across increasing transfer distances. Applied to competitive programming with a contamination-mitigated pipeline, the framework reveals that RL outperforms SFT-family methods in near-transfer efficiency, while ICL shows strong but context-dependent transfer. Within families, local improvements (e.g., abstractions, hints) often do not extend generalization radius, whereas RFT preserves stronger far-transfer capability, and self-distillation or hint-assisted RL can even reduce far-transfer performance despite improving local metrics.
为解决传统独立同分布测试集评估无法揭示单样本泛化能力的问题，本文提出“泛化谱”框架，通过构建从精确复现到跨语言实现、叙事重构等不同转移距离的测试变体，量化单个训练样本知识的迁移范围。在编程竞赛数据上应用该框架后发现，强化学习比SFT类基线更高效地将记忆转化为近迁移，而ICL虽表现强劲但依赖上下文对应性；进一步对比基线变体显示，局部改进（如抽象、提示）未必扩大泛化半径，RFT保持更强的远迁移能力，而自蒸馏或提示辅助强化学习即使提升局部表现，也可能削弱远迁移能力。
Advancing Omnimodal Embodied Agents from Isolated Skills to Everyday Physical Autonomy
Authors: Junhao Shi, Zezheng Huai, Siyin Wang, Jia Chen, Yubang Wang, Zhaoye Fei, Hechang Chen, Jingjing Gong, Xipeng Qiu, Yu-Gang Jiang
First: 2026-06-25T16:36:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:36:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Building persistent embodied agents in unstructured environments demands unified orchestration of heterogeneous tools spanning both cyber (APIs, IoT) and physical (manipulation, navigation) domains, coupled with autonomous recovery from physical failures that inevitably arise over extended operation. Existing systems treat these as separate problems: VLM-based planners lack a unified cyber-physical action space, agent frameworks accumulate unbounded context that degrades temporal coherence, and VLA policies execute open-loop without detecting their own failures. We argue that persistent autonomy requires not a monolithic model but a hierarchical asynchronous architecture with explicit separation of planning, memory, and verification. To this end, we present OmniAct, a framework integrating a multimodal semantic planner for skill routing across unified action spaces, an adaptive hierarchical memory with event-boundary-driven compression for sub-linear context growth, and an asynchronous visual preemption engine that closes the semantic loop during physical execution. Across 40 real-world long-horizon tasks on two robotic platforms coordinating four IoT devices, OmniAct achieves consistent improvements in end-to-end success across all complexity levels, maintains near-flat token consumption over under 100k+ accumulated interaction tokens, and elevates mid-scale open-weight models to proprietary-level performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从孤立技能迈向日常物理自主性的多模态具身智能体发展
在非结构化环境中构建持久具身智能体，需要统一协调涵盖数字（API、物联网）与物理（操作、导航）领域的异构工具，并结合自主恢复物理故障的能力，而这些故障在长期运行中不可避免。现有系统将这些问题视为独立问题：基于视觉语言模型的规划器缺乏统一的数字-物理动作空间，智能体框架积累无界的上下文导致时间一致性下降，视觉语言动作策略在执行时采用开环控制且无法检测自身故障。我们认为，持久自主性需要的不是单一模型，而是一个具有明确规划、记忆与验证分离的分层异步架构。为此，我们提出OmniAct框架，整合了用于跨统一动作空间技能路由的多模态语义规划器、基于事件边界驱动压缩的自适应分层记忆（实现次线性上下文增长），以及在物理执行期间闭合语义环路的异步视觉抢占引擎。在两个机器人平台上协调四台物联网设备、完成40项真实世界长时任务中，OmniAct在所有复杂度级别上均实现端到端成功率的持续提升，保持低于10万累计交互令牌时近乎恒定的令牌消耗，并将中等规模开源模型性能提升至专有级别。
Summary / 总结
To enable embodied agents to operate autonomously in unstructured environments over long durations, this work addresses the fragmentation of cyber-physical tool orchestration and failure recovery in existing systems. It proposes OmniAct, a hierarchical asynchronous architecture that separates planning, memory, and verification, integrating a multimodal semantic planner for unified action routing, an adaptive memory system that compresses context at event boundaries to maintain temporal coherence, and a visual preemption engine that closes the semantic loop during physical execution. Evaluated across 40 real-world long-horizon tasks on two robotic platforms coordinating four IoT devices, OmniAct consistently outperforms prior methods in end-to-end success across task complexities, sustains near-constant token usage despite over 100k interactions, and elevates open-weight models to performance levels previously seen only in proprietary systems.
为实现机器人在非结构化环境中持续自主操作，需协调网络与物理工具并应对物理故障，现有系统常将规划、记忆与执行割裂。本文提出OmniAct框架，采用分层异步架构，明确分离规划、记忆与验证模块，集成多模态语义规划器统一动作空间、事件边界驱动的自适应记忆压缩机制与视觉预置引擎闭环执行。在两个机器人平台协同四类IoT设备完成40项长时任务中，OmniAct在所有复杂度任务中均提升端到端成功率，保持超10万交互下近恒定的token消耗，并使中等规模开源模型达到专有级性能。
How Many Trees in a Random Forest? A Revisited Approach with Plateau Search and Optuna Integration
Authors: Vadim Porvatov, Andrey Dukhovny, Andrey Lange
Venue: IEEE Access 14 (2026) 93670-93693
First: 2026-06-02T12:10:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:34:14+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables. Author-prepared preprint of the article published in IEEE Access. The published article is licensed under CC BY 4.0
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Hyperparameter optimization (HPO) for Random Forest faces a specific difficulty in tuning the number of trees: the predictive score typically improves monotonically with ensemble size, so standard methods such as Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) and Hyperband require a predefined search range and often drive the estimate toward its right boundary. Early-stopping strategies avoid fixing such a range, but can be sensitive to score noise and prone to premature stopping. To address this, we propose an integrated triplet-based plateau-search algorithm that removes the number of trees from the direct TPE search space and still exploits information accumulated across HPO trials. The method adaptively tracks a near-minimal sufficient ensemble size by monitoring relative changes in the out-of-bag (OOB) score across a triplet of forest sizes and shifting this triplet accordingly. This yields an automated and user-interpretable procedure based on a tolerance parameter. We also provide a theoretical analysis: we relate the proposed relative OOB-score criterion to the gap between the current and limiting scores, and derive an asymptotic variance estimate for the corresponding OOB-based absolute relative difference. Experiments show that the selected number of trees can differ substantially from the common heuristic: for most classical benchmark datasets it is smaller, whereas for some high-dimensional bioinformatics datasets, such as Arcene and Dorothea, it is larger. The source code and reproducible experiments are available at https://github.com/lange-am/rf_plateau_hpo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机森林中有多少棵树？一种结合平台搜索与Optuna集成的重访方法
随机森林的超参数优化（HPO）在调整树的数量时面临特定困难：预测得分通常随集成规模单调提升，因此标准方法如树结构帕森估计器（TPE）和Hyperband需预设搜索范围，常将估计值推向右边界。早期停止策略避免固定该范围，但易受得分噪声影响且易过早停止。为此，我们提出一种集成的三元组平台搜索算法，将树的数量从TPE直接搜索空间中移除，但仍利用HPO试验积累的信息。该方法通过监控三组森林规模的袋外（OOB）得分相对变化，自适应追踪近最小充分集成规模，并相应调整该三元组。这产生了一种基于容差参数的自动化且用户可解释的流程。我们还提供了理论分析：将所提相对OOB得分准则与当前与极限得分之间的差距关联，并推导出相应OOB绝对相对差值的渐近方差估计。实验表明，所选树的数量常与常见启发式值显著不同：对多数经典基准数据集而言较小，而对某些高维生物信息学数据集（如Arcene和Dorothea）则较大。源代码和可复现实验见https://github.com/lange-am/rf_plateau_hpo。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of standard hyperparameter optimization methods in tuning Random Forest tree count—due to monotonic score improvement and boundary bias—the paper introduces a plateau-search algorithm integrated with Optuna. This method removes tree count from direct TPE search and instead monitors relative changes in out-of-bag scores across a sliding triplet of forest sizes to identify a minimal sufficient ensemble. The approach is automated via a tolerance parameter and theoretically grounded in the relationship between OOB score gaps and asymptotic variance. Experiments reveal that optimal tree counts often deviate from common heuristics: smaller for classical datasets and larger for high-dimensional bioinformatics datasets like Arcene and Dorothea.
为解决随机森林中树数量超参数优化常因性能单调提升而过度估计的问题，本文提出一种结合Optuna的平台搜索算法。该方法将树数量从直接搜索空间移除，转而通过滑动三元组的袋外得分相对变化监测，自动识别最小足够树数。理论分析将相对袋外得分与当前性能与极限性能差距关联，并推导出相应袋外相对差值的渐近方差估计。实验表明，最优树数在不同数据集上差异显著：经典基准数据集通常小于传统启发式值，而高维生物信息数据集如Arcene和Dorothea则更大。
RSPC: A Benchmark for Modeling Stress and Psychiatric Conditions in Digitally Mediated Relationships using Psychiatrist Annotations
Authors: Parmitha Vangapandu, Sai Ganesh Mokkapati, Sathwik Narkedimilli, MSVPJ Sathvik, Timothy Liu, Simon See, Johannes C. Eichstaedt
First: 2026-06-25T16:33:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:33:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In NLP, mental health conditions are often modeled as isolated phenomena, without interpersonal context. We use Reddit posts about long-distance relationships to capture both mental health distress and associated relational triggers. We introduce the Relational Stress and Psychiatry Corpus (RSPC) containing 1,799 Reddit posts annotated by psychiatrists for diagnostic categories, including the most prevalent mood disorders (anxiety and depression), relational stressor triggers, and indications of relationship phase. We benchmark seven fine-tuned transformer models and five large language models across multi-label disorder classification, relational trigger detection, and temporal phase prediction tasks. We find clear task-dependent differences between model families, with Claude-3-Haiku achieving the best disorder classification performance (Macro-F1 = 0.538) and GPT-4o obtaining the strongest relational trigger detection performance (Macro-F1 = 0.519), suggesting distinct model capabilities. We further find strong associations between anxiety disorders and chronic relational uncertainty. Overall, RSPC establishes a benchmark for NLP tasks that consider relational context and supports a shift from individual-centric to context-aware mental health modeling that captures the social and temporal dynamics of distress.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RSPC：使用精神科医生标注的数字媒介关系中压力与精神疾病建模基准
在自然语言处理中，心理健康状况常被建模为孤立现象，缺乏人际背景。我们利用关于异地恋的Reddit帖子，捕捉心理健康困扰及其相关关系触发因素。我们引入了关系压力与精神疾病语料库（RSPC），包含1799篇由精神科医生标注的Reddit帖子，标注内容包括最常见的情绪障碍（焦虑与抑郁）、关系压力触发因素及关系阶段迹象。我们在多标签疾病分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测任务中，对七种微调的Transformer模型和五种大语言模型进行基准测试。结果发现不同模型家族在任务上表现差异明显：Claude-3-Haiku在疾病分类任务中表现最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测任务中表现最强（Macro-F1=0.519），表明模型能力存在差异。此外，我们发现焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性存在强关联。总体而言，RSPC为考虑关系背景的NLP任务建立了基准，支持从以个体为中心转向以情境感知的、捕捉社会与时间动态的心理健康建模。

Motivated by the need to model mental health within interpersonal contexts rather than as isolated phenomena, the authors introduce RSPC, a corpus of 1,799 Reddit posts about long-distance relationships annotated by psychiatrists for mood disorders, relational stressors, and relationship phases.
