为解决自然语言处理中将心理健康问题孤立建模的问题，本研究构建了RSPC语料库，包含1799篇由精神科医生标注的关于异地恋的Reddit帖子，标注内容涵盖情绪障碍、关系压力触发因素及关系阶段。研究对七种微调的Transformer模型和五种大语言模型在多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测和阶段预测三项任务上进行基准测试。结果显示，模型在不同任务中表现各异：Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类中表现最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在触发检测中表现最优（Macro-F1=0.519），表明模型能力存在任务差异。研究还发现焦虑障碍与长期关系不确定性高度相关，支持在心理健康建模中纳入关系语境的重要性。
Effective Covariance Dynamics in Solvable High-Dimensional GANs
Authors: Andrew Bond, Zafer Doğan
First: 2026-06-25T16:33:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:33:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study a solvable high-dimensional model of generative adversarial network (GAN) training in which a linear generator learns a low-dimensional subspace from data with structured latent covariance. Prior solvable GAN analyses assume unconditional signals with diagonal latent covariance; we extend the multi-feature discriminator setting to class-dependent, correlated, and non-zero-mean latent structure. For the quadratic energy discriminator, all such heterogeneity enters the dynamics through a probability-weighted effective second moment. We prove that the stochastic microscopic training process converges, in the high-dimensional limit, to deterministic ordinary differential equations governed by this effective covariance. In the matched-covariance specialization, the stability analysis yields a mode-wise solvable interval determined by the learning rates and noise level: learning begins when the leading effective eigenvalue crosses the lower threshold, while full recovery requires all relevant effective modes to remain within the interval. This reveals a signal-boosting mechanism: low-rank correlations can lift weak directions above the learnability threshold, whereas overly strong correlations destabilize recovery. Numerical simulations validate the ODE, phase boundary, and boosting mechanism. Experiments on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 further show that informed generator covariance improves alignment with the data-driven reference subspace.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可解高维GAN中的有效协方差动力学
我们研究了一种可解的高维生成对抗网络（GAN）训练模型，其中线性生成器从具有结构化潜在协方差的数据中学习低维子空间。此前可解GAN分析假设无条件信号且潜在协方差为对角矩阵；我们将其扩展至多特征判别器设置，支持类别依赖、相关且非零均值的潜在结构。对于二次能量判别器，所有此类异质性通过概率加权的有效二阶矩进入动力学。我们证明，在高维极限下，随机微观训练过程收敛于由该有效协方差所支配的确定性常微分方程。在匹配协方差特化情形下，稳定性分析给出由学习率和噪声水平决定的可解区间：当主导有效特征值跨越下限阈值时训练开始，而完全恢复要求所有相关有效模式保持在该区间内。这揭示了一种信号增强机制：低秩相关性可将弱方向提升至可学习阈值之上，而过强相关性则会破坏恢复稳定性。数值模拟验证了ODE、相边界及增强机制。在MNIST、FashionMNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验进一步表明，有信息的生成器协方差可提升与数据驱动参考子空间的对齐程度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how structured latent covariance affects GAN training in high dimensions, this work analyzes a solvable model where a linear generator learns a low-dimensional subspace from data with correlated, non-zero-mean latent features. Using a quadratic energy discriminator, the authors derive deterministic ordinary differential equations governing the training dynamics, where all heterogeneity is captured by an effective second moment weighted by probability. They show that convergence and stability depend on the effective eigenvalues of this moment, with learning initiating when the largest eigenvalue crosses a lower threshold and full recovery requiring all relevant modes to stay within a learning-rate and noise-dependent interval. Numerical simulations and experiments on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 confirm the ODE predictions and demonstrate that aligning generator covariance with data-driven structure improves alignment and performance.
为理解高维数据中结构化潜在协方差如何影响GAN训练，本文研究了一个可解模型，其中线性生成器从具有相关、非零均值潜在特征的数据中学习低维子空间。采用二次能量判别器，作者推导出由概率加权有效二阶矩所支配的确定性常微分方程，描述训练动态。研究表明，收敛与稳定性取决于该有效协方差的特征值，学习始于最大特征值跨越下限，完全恢复需所有相关模式保持在稳定区间内。数值模拟与在MNIST、FashionMNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验验证了理论预测，并表明生成器协方差与数据驱动参考子空间对齐可提升学习效果。
Sutra: Tensor-Op RNNs as a Compilation Target for Vector Symbolic Architectures
Authors: Emma Leonhart
Venue: NeurIPS
First: 2026-05-20T09:04:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:31:06+00:00
Comments: Modified NeurIPS submission, see AI declaration and replication materials at end of paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sutra is a typed, purely functional programming language whose compiled forward pass is a PyTorch neural network. The compiler beta-reduces the whole program -- primitives, control flow, string I/O -- to one fused tensor-op graph over a frozen embedding substrate. Rotation binding, unbind, bundle, polynomial Kleene three-valued logic, and tail-recursive loops all lower to tensor operations; the Kleene connectives are Lagrange-interpolated polynomials exact on the {-1, 0, +1} truth grid. Validation is one fact tested two ways. (1) The same program runs on four frozen embeddings spanning two modalities -- three text encoders (nomic-embed-text, all-minilm, mxbai-embed-large) and one protein language model (ESM-2) -- and decodes bundles at 100% accuracy through width k=8 on every substrate, where the textbook Hadamard product has already collapsed (2.5% on mxbai-embed-large, 7.5% on all-minilm). (2) PyTorch autograd flows through the actually compiled graph: a fuzzy-rule classifier written in .su trains from random init (18.7 +/- 9.5%; chance = 20%, five classes) to 100.0 +/- 0.0% (three seeds) by backpropagating through the emitted graph, the symbolic source unmodified. A weighted variant additionally trains a scalar cosine gain and writes it back into the .su source as a numeric literal; recompiling reproduces the trained behaviour to ~2e-7 per logit, so the trained model is itself legible, recompilable code. The same artifact is therefore both a logic program and a trainable neural network.
中文标题/摘要
标题：经文：将张量操作RNN作为向量符号架构的编译目标
Sutra是一种类型化、纯函数式编程语言，其编译后的前向传播等价于一个PyTorch神经网络。编译器对整个程序——包括基本操作、控制流和字符串I/O——进行β归约，生成一个在冻结嵌入基底上的融合张量操作图。旋转绑定、解绑、捆绑、多项式Kleene三值逻辑及尾递归循环均降级为张量操作；Kleene连接词是精确在{-1, 0, +1}真值网格上的拉格朗日插值多项式。验证通过两种方式完成：(1) 相同程序在四种覆盖两种模态的冻结嵌入上运行——三个文本编码器（nomic-embed-text、all-minilm、mxbai-embed-large）和一个蛋白质语言模型（ESM-2）——并在每个基底上以宽度k=8实现100%的捆绑解码准确率，而教科书式Hadamard乘积在mxbai-embed-large上仅达2.5%，在all-minilm上仅达7.5%。(2) PyTorch自动微分可流经实际编译后的图：一个用.su编写的模糊规则分类器从随机初始化（18.7±9.5%；随机概率=20%，五类）通过反向传播训练至100.0±0.0%（三个种子），且符号源代码未作修改。加权变体还训练一个标量余弦增益并将其写回.su源文件作为数值字面量；重新编译可复现训练行为，误差约2e-7每logit，因此训练后的模型本身即为可读、可重编译的代码。同一产物既是逻辑程序，也是可训练的神经网络。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unify symbolic logic programming with neural network training, Sutra compiles a purely functional language into a single tensor-operation graph that executes as a PyTorch model. The compiler reduces all program constructs—including control flow, I/O, and symbolic operations like rotation, bundle, and Kleene logic—to fused tensor ops over frozen embeddings, with Kleene connectives implemented via Lagrange interpolation over {-1, 0, +1}. Experiments show that the same program runs with 100% accuracy across four different embeddings (three text encoders and one protein model) using k=8, outperforming the Hadamard product baseline. Additionally, a fuzzy-rule classifier trained via backpropagation through the compiled graph achieves perfect accuracy, and a weighted variant can learn and write back a scalar cosine gain, enabling recompilation of the trained model with near-identical behavior.
为统一符号逻辑与神经网络训练，Sutra 是一种类型化函数式语言，可将整个程序（含控制流和 I/O）编译为单一融合的张量操作图，运行于冻结的嵌入空间之上。其核心创新在于将符号构造（如旋转、解绑、捆绑、Kleene 逻辑，通过 {-1, 0, +1} 真值网格上的拉格朗日插值多项式表示）直接映射为张量操作。实验表明，同一程序在四种不同冻结嵌入（三个文本编码器和一个蛋白质语言模型）上均以宽度 k=8 实现 100% 解码准确率，显著优于 Hadamard 乘积基线。此外，PyTorch 自动微分可成功通过编译后的图进行反向传播，使模糊规则分类器从随机初始化训练至 100% 准确率，且加权变体可将学习到的标量余弦增益写回源代码，重新编译后行为可精确复现至约 2e-7 的 logits 级别，实现逻辑程序与可训练神经网络的统一。
The Geometry of Updates: Fisher Alignment at Vocabulary Scale
Authors: John Sweeney
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-25T16:30:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:30:27+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026), PMLR 306. 64 pages total (main paper plus appendix), 4 figures, 29 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training-free source selection for LLM families with shared vocabularies arises in scientific string domains such as SMILES, protein, and genomic sequences, where candidate corpora share a tokenizer but differ in prediction targets. This creates an activation-dark regime: representation-similarity metrics can be uninformative without assumptions about label-conditioned error geometry, while classical update-geometry metrics are computationally prohibitive at vocabulary scale. We show that, in a shared-output head setting, representation metrics (e.g., CKA) are non-identifiable for transfer; models can share identical representations yet have orthogonal head updates. The key identity is that head Fisher alignment is exactly a cosine between kernel mean embeddings in the joint activation-error space, exposing activation, error, and coupling factors rather than requiring a materialized Fisher matrix. FisherSketch estimates this cosine directly in a single streaming pass, making K=128,256 head Fisher alignment practical with a 16 KB task signature (m=4096) and a 192 KB per-task streaming state, small enough to store next to a model hash, but encoding transfer-relevant update structure. Beyond source selection, the same signatures and marginals provide a diagnostic instrument for studying whether LLM task similarity is driven by activations, errors, or their coupling; shared-parameter and internal-layer validations, together with Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift experiments, show that FisherSketch remains informative when activation similarity cannot distinguish tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：更新的几何学：词汇规模下的费舍尔对齐
在SMILES、蛋白质和基因组序列等科学字符串领域，具有共享词汇表的LLM家族面临训练无关的源选择问题，其中候选语料库共享分词器但预测目标不同。这导致激活暗区：在无标签条件误差几何假设下，表示相似性度量无用，而经典更新几何度量在词汇规模下计算代价过高。我们证明，在共享输出头设置下，表示度量（如CKA）对迁移不可识别：模型可共享相同表示，但头部更新正交。关键恒等式是头部费舍尔对齐恰好是联合激活-误差空间中核均值嵌入的余弦值，揭示激活、误差及耦合因子，无需显式费舍尔矩阵。FisherSketch在单次流式通过中直接估计该余弦值，使K=128,256头部费舍尔对齐在16 KB任务签名（m=4096）和每任务192 KB流式状态（足够存储于模型哈希旁）下可行，编码迁移相关更新结构。除源选择外，相同签名与边际值还提供诊断工具，用于研究LLM任务相似性是否由激活、误差或其耦合驱动；共享参数与内部层验证，结合Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift实验表明，即使激活相似性无法区分任务，FisherSketch仍具信息量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of training-free source selection for LLMs sharing vocabularies but differing in prediction targets—common in scientific domains like SMILES or genomics—the paper introduces FisherSketch, a method that estimates head Fisher alignment via cosine similarity between kernel mean embeddings in joint activation-error space, avoiding computationally heavy Fisher matrix computation. Experiments show FisherSketch enables practical K=128,256 head alignment with minimal memory (16 KB task signature, 192 KB streaming state), and remains informative even when activation similarity fails to distinguish tasks, as validated through Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift experiments and internal-layer analyses.
为解决共享词汇但预测目标不同的LLM家族在科学字符串领域（如SMILES、蛋白质、基因组序列）中的无训练源选择问题，本文提出FisherSketch方法，通过在联合激活-误差空间中计算核均值嵌入的余弦相似度，直接估计头部Fisher对齐，避免昂贵的Fisher矩阵计算。该方法仅需16KB任务签名和192KB每任务流状态，即可在词汇规模下高效实现，且可与模型哈希相邻存储。实验表明，FisherSketch能有效区分任务相似性，其依据是更新结构而非激活相似性，即使激活相似性无法区分任务时，该方法仍保持诊断有效性。
Learning to Explain Air Traffic Situation
Authors: Hong-ah Chai, Seokbin Yoon, Keumjin Lee
First: 2025-02-15T11:03:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:26:48+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 3 figures, minor revisions to address reviewer feedback for final submission to the First US-Europe Air Transportation Research and Development (ATRD) Symposium
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding how air traffic controllers construct a mental 'picture' of complex air traffic situations is crucial but remains a challenge due to the inherently intricate, high-dimensional interactions between aircraft, pilots, and controllers. Previous work on modeling the strategies of air traffic controllers and their mental image of traffic situations often centers on specific air traffic control tasks or pairwise interactions between aircraft, neglecting to capture the comprehensive dynamics of an air traffic situation. To address this issue, we propose a machine learning-based framework for explaining air traffic situations. Specifically, we employ a Transformer-based multi-agent trajectory model that encapsulates both the spatio-temporal movement of aircraft and social interaction between them. By deriving attention scores from the model, we can quantify the influence of individual aircraft on overall traffic dynamics. This provides explainable insights into how air traffic controllers perceive and understand the traffic situation. Trained on real-world air traffic surveillance data collected from the terminal airspace around Incheon International Airport in South Korea, our framework effectively explicates air traffic situations. This could potentially support and enhance the decision-making and situational awareness of air traffic controllers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习解释空中交通状况
理解空中交通管制员如何构建复杂空中交通状况的‘心理图景’至关重要，但由于飞机、飞行员与管制员之间固有的复杂、高维交互，这一问题仍具挑战性。以往关于建模空中交通管制员策略及其对交通状况心理图景的研究，往往聚焦于特定管制任务或飞机间的成对交互，未能捕捉空中交通状况的整体动态。为解决此问题，我们提出一种基于机器学习的空中交通状况解释框架。具体而言，我们采用基于Transformer的多智能体轨迹模型，同时捕捉飞机的时空运动与彼此间的社会交互。通过从模型中推导注意力分数，可量化单架飞机对整体交通动态的影响，从而提供可解释的洞见，揭示管制员如何感知与理解交通状况。该框架在韩国仁川国际机场终端空域采集的真实空中交通监视数据上训练，能有效解释空中交通状况，有望支持并提升空中交通管制员的决策与态势感知能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how air traffic controllers mentally model complex airspace dynamics, this study proposes a Transformer-based multi-agent trajectory model to explain air traffic situations. The method captures spatio-temporal aircraft movements and social interactions, using attention scores to quantify each aircraft’s influence on overall traffic patterns. Evaluated on real surveillance data from Incheon International Airport’s terminal airspace, the framework successfully generates interpretable explanations of traffic dynamics, offering potential support for controller decision-making and situational awareness.
为理解空管人员如何构建复杂空域态势的思维模型，本文提出一种基于Transformer的多智能体轨迹模型，用于解释空域态势。该方法捕捉飞机的时空运动与交互关系，通过注意力分数量化各飞机对整体交通态势的影响。在韩国仁川国际机场终端空域的真实监控数据上验证，该框架有效解析了空域动态，可为管制员决策与态势感知提供可解释的辅助支持。
HumanoidUMI: Bridging Robot-Free Demonstrations and Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation
Authors: Hongwu Wang, Chenhao Yu, Youhao Hu, Jiachen Zhang, Yuanyuan Li, Shaqi Luo
First: 2026-06-25T16:23:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:23:56+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
High-quality demonstration data are essential for humanoid robot skill learning, especially for whole-body behaviors that require coordinated perception, locomotion, and manipulation. Existing data-collection methods largely rely on robot teleoperation, which is constrained by hardware accessibility, operator expertise, and limited efficiency. Inspired by the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI), we propose HumanoidUMI, a portable and robot-free framework for humanoid whole-body data collection. HumanoidUMI uses lightweight VR devices and UMI-inspired grippers to collect sparse human keypoint trajectories, wrist-view observations, and gripper actions. These demonstrations train a high-level policy to predict future keypoints, which are retargeted to robot-native whole-body references and executed by a whole-body controller. Experiments in five real-world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework and validate the collected demonstrations for transferable humanoid whole-body skill learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HumanoidUMI：连接无机器人演示与人形全身操作
高质量的演示数据对人形机器人技能学习至关重要，尤其是需要协调感知、运动与操作的全身行为。现有数据采集方法主要依赖机器人遥操作，受限于硬件可及性、操作员专业水平和效率低下。受通用操作界面（UMI）启发，我们提出HumanoidUMI，一种便携且无需机器人参与的人形全身数据采集框架。HumanoidUMI利用轻量级VR设备和受UMI启发的夹持器，采集稀疏的人体关键点轨迹、手腕视角观测和夹持器动作。这些演示数据训练高层策略预测未来关键点，再将其重定向至机器人原生全身参考并由全身控制器执行。在五个真实场景中的实验验证了该框架的有效性，并证明所收集的演示数据适用于可迁移的人形全身技能学习。
Summary / 总结
To address the inefficiency and hardware dependency of traditional robot teleoperation for collecting humanoid whole-body demonstration data, HumanoidUMI introduces a portable, robot-free framework inspired by the Universal Manipulation Interface. It leverages lightweight VR devices and UMI-style grippers to capture sparse human keypoint trajectories, wrist-view observations, and gripper actions without requiring a robot. These demonstrations are used to train a high-level policy that predicts future keypoints, which are then retargeted to robot-native whole-body references and executed via a whole-body controller. Experiments across five real-world scenarios confirm the framework’s effectiveness in enabling transferable whole-body skill learning for humanoid robots.
为解决依赖机器人操作的数据采集在人形机器人全身操控中的局限性，HumanoidUMI 提出了一种便携式、无需机器人参与的框架，受 Universal Manipulation Interface 启发。该框架利用轻量级 VR 设备和 UMI 风格夹爪，采集稀疏的人体关键点轨迹、手腕视角观测和夹爪动作，无需机器人即可完成数据收集。这些演示数据用于训练高层策略，预测未来关键点并将其重定向至机器人原生的全身参考坐标系，最终由全身控制器执行。在五个真实场景中的实验验证了该框架的有效性，证明其可实现可迁移的人形机器人全身技能学习。
LMs as Task-Specific Knowledge Bases: An Interpretability Analysis
Authors: Amit Elhelo, Amir Globerson, Mor Geva
First: 2026-06-25T16:22:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:22:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models (LMs) capture large amounts of factual knowledge applicable to a wide range of tasks, motivating the view of their parameters as a knowledge base. An important property of knowledge bases is that different queries for the same fact return consistent results, drawing on a single source of truth. We investigate whether LMs satisfy this property through behavioral and mechanistic analyses. Our results suggest that they encode knowledge in a task-specific manner. Behaviorally, facts acquired on one task frequently fail to co-emerge on others during training. Parameter localization experiments suggest a mechanistic explanation, revealing distinct parameter subsets underlying different tasks for the same fact. Finally, we show that chain-of-thought reasoning draws part of its effectiveness from engaging task-specific parameters beyond those tied to the evaluation task. Our findings suggest that what the model knows and how it is asked are intertwined in parameter space, undermining the "knowledge base" analogy and carrying implications for the reliability and controllability of factual knowledge in LMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型作为任务特定知识库：可解释性分析
语言模型（LMs）捕获大量适用于广泛任务的事实性知识，这促使人们将其参数视为知识库。知识库的一个重要特性是，针对同一事实的不同查询应返回一致的结果，依赖单一真实来源。我们通过行为和机制分析探究LMs是否满足这一特性。结果表明，LMs以任务特定方式编码知识。行为上，一个任务中习得的事实常在其他任务训练中无法共同出现。参数定位实验提供了机制解释，揭示同一事实背后存在不同任务对应的参数子集。最后，我们证明链式思维推理的部分有效性源于激活了与评估任务无关的任务特定参数。我们的发现表明，模型所知内容与其被询问方式在参数空间中紧密交织，削弱了“知识库”类比，并对LMs中事实性知识的可靠性与可控性产生影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the idea that language models function as task-specific knowledge bases, this study investigates whether LMs maintain consistent factual responses across different tasks, as true knowledge bases should. Through behavioral and mechanistic analyses, the authors find that LMs encode facts in task-specific parameter subsets, meaning facts learned in one task often fail to co-emerge in others during training. Parameter localization experiments further reveal distinct neural mechanisms for the same fact across tasks, and chain-of-thought reasoning relies on task-specific parameters beyond those directly relevant to the evaluation task. These results challenge the knowledge base analogy, suggesting that factual knowledge in LMs is tightly coupled with how it is queried, raising concerns about reliability and controllability.
该研究受语言模型可视为任务特定知识库这一观点启发，旨在探究模型是否能在不同任务中对同一事实保持一致的检索能力，这是知识库的核心特性。通过行为与机制分析发现，模型在不同任务中学习到的事实常无法同时激活，表明知识是任务特定编码的。参数定位实验进一步揭示，同一事实背后存在支持不同任务的独立参数子集，说明知识并非全局存储而是任务绑定的。研究还表明，思维链推理的有效性部分源于调用与评估任务无关的任务特定参数，说明知识检索与任务提问在参数空间中紧密耦合，挑战了知识库类比，对模型事实知识的可靠性与可控性提出重要启示。
SignSparK: Efficient Multilingual Sign Language Production via Sparse Keyframe Learning
Authors: Jianhe Low, Alexandre Symeonidis-Herzig, Maksym Ivashechkin, Ozge Mercanoglu Sincan, Richard Bowden
Venue: ECCV
First: 2026-03-11T06:02:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:22:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted at European Conference on Computer Vision (ECCV) 2026. Project page available: https://cogvis-cvssp.github.io/papers/signspark/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Sign Language Production (SLP) faces a fundamental trade-off: direct text-to-pose models suffer from regression-to-the-mean effects, while dictionary-retrieval methods produce disjointed transitions. To resolve this, we propose a novel training paradigm that leverages sparse keyframes to capture the underlying kinematic distribution of human signing. By generating dense motion from discrete anchors, our approach mitigates regression-to-the-mean while ensuring fluid articulation. To achieve this at scale, we introduce FAST, an ultra-efficient sign segmentation model that automatically mines precise temporal boundaries. We then present SignSparK, a Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) framework that utilizes these temporal anchors to synthesize 3D signing sequences. This keyframe-driven formulation also unlocks Keyframe-to-Pose (KF2P) generation, making precise spatiotemporal editing of signing sequences possible. Furthermore, SignSparK scales across four distinct sign languages, constituting the largest multilingual SLP framework to date, and integrates 3D Gaussian Splatting for photorealistic rendering. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that SignSparK achieves state-of-the-art across diverse SLP tasks and multilingual benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/JianHe0628/SignSparK.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SignSparK：通过稀疏关键帧学习实现高效的多语言手语生成
手语生成（SLP）面临根本性权衡：直接文本到姿态模型易受回归均值效应影响，而字典检索方法则产生不连贯的过渡。为解决此问题，我们提出一种新颖的训练范式，利用稀疏关键帧捕捉人类手语的底层运动分布。通过从离散锚点生成密集运动，我们的方法缓解了回归均值效应，同时确保流畅的表达。为实现规模化，我们引入FAST，一种超高效的手语分割模型，可自动挖掘精确的时间边界。随后，我们提出SignSparK，一个条件流匹配（CFM）框架，利用这些时间锚点合成三维手语序列。该关键帧驱动的框架还支持关键帧到姿态（KF2P）生成，使手语序列的精确时空编辑成为可能。此外，SignSparK支持四种不同手语，是迄今为止规模最大的多语言SLP框架，并集成三维高斯点渲染以实现逼真视觉效果。大量评估表明，SignSparK在多种SLP任务和多语言基准测试中均达到最先进水平。代码开源于https://github.com/JianHe0628/SignSparK。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing sign language production methods—either regressing to mean poses or producing disjointed motions—the paper introduces SignSparK, a framework that uses sparse keyframes to capture human signing kinematics. It employs FAST for efficient temporal segmentation and a Conditional Flow Matching model to generate dense 3D motion from these anchors, enabling fluid articulation and precise spatiotemporal editing. Evaluated across four sign languages, SignSparK achieves state-of-the-art performance in multilingual sign language generation and rendering, with photorealistic output via 3D Gaussian Splatting.
为解决现有手语生成方法存在的回归均值或动作不连贯问题，该论文提出SignSparK框架，通过稀疏关键帧捕捉人类手语的运动分布。结合FAST模型实现高效时序分割，并采用条件流匹配生成稠密3D动作，实现流畅表达与精准时空编辑。在四种手语上评估，SignSparK在多语言基准上达到当前最优性能，并支持通过3D高斯点渲染实现逼真视觉效果，展现出更强的可扩展性与生成质量。
From Celebrities to Anyone: Characterizing AI Nudification Content, Technology, and Community Dynamics on 4chan
Authors: Chi Cui, Yixin Wu, Yang Zhang
First: 2026-06-25T16:21:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:21:38+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 13 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI nudification uses generative models to create synthetic non-consensual sexually explicit imagery (SNEACI) of real individuals. Prior work has examined dedicated nudification platforms and model repositories, finding that most targets are female celebrities. However, the anonymous content community, where SNEACI is actively requested, generated, and exchanged, remains unexplored. In this work, we present a large-scale study of AI nudification in the wild, identifying 24,105 SNEACI items. We find a significant shift in target demographics: non-celebrity individuals now account for 55.8\% of targets, compared to only 4.7\% in prior studies, indicating that AI nudification has expanded from targeting public figures to increasingly harming individuals within users' own social circles. Meanwhile, open-source models dominate production, with Stable Diffusion family generating 42.7\% of images and Wan generating 66.5\% of videos, all driven by thousands of shared fine-tuned models and accessible tutorials. Yet the ecosystem runs on a small cohort of active producers, with the most prolific producing 780 items, drives community engagement, shapes target demographics, and disseminates technical knowledge that lowers barriers for new producers. Our work provides an empirical understanding of how AI nudification operates in the wild, revealing the mechanisms that sustain this ecosystem and highlighting the urgent need for interventions in platform governance, technical safeguards, and affected individual protection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从名人到普通人：4chan上AI裸体化内容、技术与社区动态的特征分析
AI裸体化利用生成模型创建真实个体的非自愿性合成色情图像（SNEACI）。此前研究主要考察专用裸体化平台和模型库，发现多数目标为女性名人。然而，SNEACI被积极请求、生成和交换的匿名内容社区尚未被探索。本研究对野生AI裸体化进行大规模分析，识别出24,105项SNEACI内容。我们发现目标人群显著变化：非名人个体占比达55.8%，而此前研究仅为4.7%，表明AI裸体化已从针对公众人物扩展至用户社交圈内个体。同时，开源模型主导生产，Stable Diffusion系列生成42.7%的图像，Wan模型生成66.5%的视频，均由数千个共享微调模型和可访问教程驱动。但该生态依赖少数活跃生产者，最活跃者产出780项内容，主导社区互动、塑造目标人群分布并传播降低新生产者门槛的技术知识。本研究揭示AI裸体化在野生环境中的运作机制，凸显平台治理、技术防护及受害者保护亟需干预。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how AI nudification operates beyond dedicated platforms, this study investigates the anonymous online community of 4chan, where synthetic non-consensual explicit imagery is actively created and shared. Through a large-scale analysis of 24,155 such images and videos, the research finds that non-celebrity targets now constitute the majority (55.8%) of victims, a stark contrast to prior studies that focused on celebrities. The study identifies Stable Diffusion and Wan as dominant generative models, with open-source tools and shared fine-tuned models enabling widespread participation. However, the ecosystem is sustained by a small group of prolific producers, who generate the majority of content and drive community dynamics, while also lowering technical barriers for newcomers. The findings reveal systemic vulnerabilities in platform governance and highlight the urgent need for technical safeguards and victim protection.
本研究旨在揭示AI换脸生成非自愿色情图像在匿名社区中的运作机制，通过对4chan上24,105件此类内容的分析发现，非名人个体已成为主要目标（占比55.8%），远超以往聚焦名人目标的研究。主流生成模型为Stable Diffusion系列和Wan，开源工具与共享微调模型推动了广泛参与，但整个生态由少数高产创作者维持，他们不仅产出大量内容，还主导社区互动并传播技术知识，大幅降低了新创作者的门槛。
Bridging Talk and Thought: Understanding Dialogue Dynamics Across Collaborative Problem-Solving Contexts
Authors: Zhengyuan Liu, Stella Xin Yin, Min-Yen Kan, Nancy F. Chen
First: 2026-06-25T16:20:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:20:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a conceptual framework for analyzing dialogue in collaborative problem-solving contexts, with an emphasis on the emerging dynamics of human-AI and multi-agent collaboration. As intelligent systems become active agents capable of autonomous reasoning and strategic cooperation, understanding the dialogic interaction during collaborative problem solving is increasingly important for optimizing and evaluating such partnerships. Our framework addresses key limitations in current analytical approaches through a hierarchical two-layer coding scheme that integrates cognitive and non-cognitive problem solving with metacognitive regulatory mechanisms. We demonstrate its effectiveness and generalizability across nine datasets spanning multiple domains, and provide insights into how humans and agents coordinate their knowledge, skills, and efforts to solve complex problems, showing in particular that metacognitive regulation can be an essential discriminator of deeper collaboration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：连接对话与思维：理解协作问题解决情境中的对话动态
我们提出了一种用于分析协作问题解决情境中对话的概念框架，重点在于人类-人工智能及多智能体协作中涌现的动态。随着智能系统成为具备自主推理与战略合作能力的主动代理，理解协作问题解决过程中的对话互动对于优化和评估此类伙伴关系日益重要。我们的框架通过整合认知与非认知问题解决及元认知调节机制的分层双层编码方案，解决了当前分析方法的关键局限。我们在涵盖多个领域的九个数据集上验证了该框架的有效性与普适性，并揭示了人类与智能体如何协调其知识、技能与努力以解决复杂问题，特别指出元认知调节可作为深度协作的重要区分因素。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand dialogue dynamics in human-AI and multi-agent collaborative problem-solving, this study introduces a hierarchical two-layer coding framework that integrates cognitive, non-cognitive problem-solving processes with metacognitive regulation. Applied across nine diverse datasets, the framework reveals how humans and agents coordinate knowledge and effort, with metacognitive regulation emerging as a key indicator distinguishing deeper, more effective collaboration. Experimental results demonstrate the framework’s generalizability and utility in evaluating and optimizing collaborative systems.
为理解人类与AI在协作解决问题时的对话动态，该研究提出了一种分层双层编码框架，整合认知、非认知与元认知维度。在九个跨领域的数据集上验证后发现，元认知调节（如自我监控与策略调整）是区分深度协作与浅层互动的关键因素，且该框架能稳定应用于不同领域与智能体类型。
A hardware-safety-gated system for LLM-written native ARTIQ control code on a trapped-ion platform
Authors: Duanyang Wang, Lu Qi, Yuanheng Xie, Norbert M. Linke, Kenneth R. Brown
First: 2026-06-25T16:19:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:19:23+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large-language-model (LLM) agents can write and run experimental control code. This allows laboratory work to be conducted autonomously. However, this autonomy raises a safety problem that prior work has not addressed. Unchecked code can damage the apparatus, and there is no formal, per-operation boundary between human authorization/supervision, and agent decisions. We present a control system that places an LLM agent in the loop of a trapped-ion experiment while enforcing such a boundary. The agent controls the existing Advanced Real-Time Infrastructure for Quantum physics (ARTIQ) stack through tools provided by a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server. No tool call reaches the hardware unless it carries an authorization token bound to its exact contents. Tokens are issued in one of two ways: automatically, by running the agent's proposed script in an isolated hardware simulation (dax.sim) and checking every operation against preset per-device bounds, or manually by a human operator for sensitive actions. Within this boundary the agent develops its own experiments, rather than only calling pre-built routines. We deploy the system on a co-trapped $^{40}$Ca$^{+}$/$^{40}$CaOH$^{+}$ crystal, where the agent autonomously builds a full calibration stack and, with targeted operator guidance, closes a cross-instrument magnetic-field-stabilization loop. On a separate, independent $^{171}$Yb$^{+}$ platform, we confirm interface-level portability. We systematically test token-authorization mechanism with adversarial scripts that attempt to bypass it, mapping the precise boundary of its protection and prioritizing where to strengthen it next. Analyzing where the agent still requires human guidance, we find that its limits lie in metacognitive control, namely recognizing when a problem must be re-framed, rather than in domain knowledge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于捕获离子平台的LLM生成原生ARTIQ控制代码的硬件安全门控系统
大语言模型（LLM）代理能够编写并运行实验控制代码，从而实现实验室工作的自主化。然而，这种自主性引发了一个此前未被解决的安全问题：未经检查的代码可能损坏设备，且缺乏正式的、每操作级别的界限，以区分人类授权/监督与代理决策。我们提出了一种控制系统，将LLM代理纳入捕获离子实验的循环中，同时强制执行此类界限。代理通过模型上下文协议（MCP）服务器提供的工具控制现有的量子物理高级实时基础设施（ARTIQ）堆栈。除非工具调用携带与其内容完全匹配的授权令牌，否则不会触及硬件。令牌通过两种方式发放：自动方式，即在隔离的硬件仿真环境（dax.sim）中运行代理提议的脚本，并逐操作检查是否符合预设的每设备界限；或由人类操作员手动发放敏感操作。在此边界内，代理可自主开发实验，而不仅限于调用预构建的程序。我们在共捕获的$^{40}$Ca$^{+}$/$^{40}$CaOH$^{+}$晶体平台上部署该系统，代理自主构建完整校准堆栈，并在目标操作员引导下关闭跨仪器的磁场稳定闭环。在独立的$^{171}$Yb$^{+}$平台上，我们验证了接口层面的可移植性。我们系统性测试了令牌授权机制，使用试图绕过该机制的对抗性脚本，精确绘制其保护边界，并优先确定下一步加强的方向。分析代理仍需人类指导的领域，我们发现其限制在于元认知控制，即识别何时需重新构建问题，而非领域知识。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the safety risks of unmonitored LLM-generated experimental code, this work introduces a hardware-safety-gated system that integrates an LLM agent into a trapped-ion quantum experiment while enforcing strict authorization boundaries. The agent operates via the ARTIQ control stack through a Model Context Protocol server, where every hardware command must carry a token verifying its safety against pre-set device limits—either auto-generated via simulation or manually approved by a human. Experiments on a $^{40}$Ca$^{+}$/$^{40}$CaOH$^{+}$ crystal show the agent autonomously builds calibration routines and collaborates with human guidance to close a magnetic-field stabilization loop; on a separate $^{171}$Yb$^{+}$ platform, the system demonstrates portability. Adversarial testing confirms the token mechanism’s effectiveness and identifies its protective boundaries, while analysis reveals the agent’s limitations lie in metacognitive reasoning—not domain knowledge—when deciding to reframe problems.
为解决LLM生成实验控制代码可能引发的安全风险，该研究提出一种硬件安全门控系统，将LLM代理集成到离子阱实验中并强制执行授权边界。代理通过模型上下文协议服务器控制ARTIQ系统，所有硬件指令必须携带验证其内容与设备预设限制匹配的令牌——令牌由模拟环境自动或人工手动发放。在$^{40}$Ca$^{+}$/$^{40}$CaOH$^{+}$晶体平台上，代理自主构建校准流程，并在人工引导下关闭磁稳定闭环；在独立的$^{171}$Yb$^{+}$平台上验证了系统可移植性。对抗性测试揭示了令牌机制的精确安全边界，分析表明代理的局限在于元认知能力（如问题重构能力），而非领域知识。
MetaboNet-Bench: A Multi-modal Benchmark for Glucose Forecasting in Type 1 Diabetes
Authors: Nathaniel Jeffries, Miriam Wolff, Sam Royston, Elizabeth Healey, Caleb Mayer, David Klonoff, Michael Snyder, Tao Wang
First: 2026-06-17T03:25:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:17:47+00:00
Comments: main content in 10 pages with 5 figures; supplementary section with 11 more pages and 5 more figures. v2: fix figure 4 and 5's misordering
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Glucose forecasting algorithms are an important aspect of glycemic control management in type 1 diabetes. So far, the research community has developed numerous algorithms and models for forecasting. However, it is well-recognized that the lack of standardized model performance evaluation benchmarks makes fair comparison difficult and hinders further innovation, and thus benchmark standardization is in urgent need. Furthermore, many published glucose forecasting algorithms are limited to CGM data alone, ignoring other multimodal signals such as insulin dosing and carbohydrate intake. Here, we introduce MetaboNet-Bench, a benchmark for multimodal glucose forecasting for patients with type 1 diabetes that provides an extensible open-source evaluation framework for comparison of glucose forecasting algorithms that leverage glucose, insulin, and carbohydrate data. We then demonstrate its utility by benchmarking several recently published glucose forecasting models and a custom multimodal time-series model, representing different model architectures. The results show that the benefit of adding data modalities is conditioned on the complexity of the model and that incorporating more clinical metrics helps identify meaningful gaps to fill for future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MetaboNet-Bench：用于1型糖尿病血糖预测的多模态基准
血糖预测算法是1型糖尿病血糖控制管理的重要组成部分。目前，研究社区已开发出多种预测算法和模型，但公认的是，缺乏标准化的模型性能评估基准，导致公平比较困难，阻碍了进一步创新，因此亟需基准标准化。此外，许多已发表的血糖预测算法仅依赖连续血糖监测（CGM）数据，忽略了胰岛素剂量和碳水化合物摄入等其他多模态信号。本文引入MetaboNet-Bench，这是一个面向1型糖尿病患者的多模态血糖预测基准，提供了一个可扩展的开源评估框架，用于比较利用血糖、胰岛素和碳水化合物数据的预测算法。我们通过基准测试多个近期发表的血糖预测模型及一个自定义的多模态时间序列模型（代表不同模型架构）来展示其效用。结果表明，增加数据模态的益处取决于模型的复杂性，且纳入更多临床指标有助于识别未来研究需填补的重要差距。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of standardized benchmarks for glucose forecasting in type 1 diabetes and the limited use of multimodal data in existing models, the authors introduce MetaboNet-Bench, an open-source evaluation framework that integrates glucose, insulin, and carbohydrate intake data. They benchmark several published models and a custom multimodal time-series model to assess performance across different architectures. Results indicate that multimodal data improves forecasting accuracy only when paired with sufficiently complex models, and that incorporating clinical metrics reveals meaningful performance gaps for future research.
鉴于当前缺乏标准化的葡萄糖预测基准和多数模型仅依赖CGM数据而忽略胰岛素剂量与碳水化合物摄入等多模态信号，本文提出MetaboNet-Bench，一个开源的多模态葡萄糖预测评估框架。作者对多个现有模型及一个自定义多模态时序模型进行基准测试，结果表明：多模态数据的增益依赖于模型复杂度，且结合临床指标可揭示未来研究的关键改进空间。
CARVE: Content-Aware Recurrent with Value Efficiency for Chunk-Parallel Linear Attention
Authors: Sayak Dutta
First: 2026-06-25T16:16:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:16:51+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 2 figures, multiple tables. Submitted to arXiv. Primary category: cs.LG; cross-list: cs.CL
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recurrent models must forget in order to remember, yet the state of the art decides what to erase without consulting what is stored -- the gate sees only the arriving token, not the memory it is about to modify. This memory-blind gating is one of three coupled defects in the leading delta-rule architecture (GDN-2): the value-axis erase mask wastes parameters at the scale of the value projection, and -- as we prove -- mathematically prevents the WY-form triangular chunk solver that makes recurrent training competitive with Transformers. We introduce CARVE (Content-Aware Recurrent with Value Efficiency), which resolves all three problems through one principle: erase only on the key axis. This is provably necessary and sufficient for the WY-form solver to remain valid. Within it, CARVE reuses the recurrent output tensor -- already written to GPU memory -- as a free content signal for the erase gate, and replaces the per-value write-gate projection with a single scalar per head. At initialisation CARVE is bit-identical to GDN-2; any quality difference emerges from what the content gate learns. At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B tokens, CARVE achieves WikiText perplexity 15.72 (minus 0.18 vs. GDN-2, a 4.5-sigma effect), leads every recurrent baseline on nine common-sense reasoning benchmarks, and sets state of the art on every RULER retrieval probe -- at 0.4% throughput overhead, 13% lower peak memory, and 19% fewer parameters. Six formal theorems cover memory capacity, Lyapunov stability, gradient flow, expressivity separation, Pareto-optimal chunk size, and hybrid optimality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CARVE：面向块并行线性注意力的语义感知循环模型与值效率优化
循环模型必须遗忘才能记住，但当前最先进的模型在决定删除什么时并不参考已存储的内容——门控机制仅看到即将到达的标记，而无法感知即将修改的记忆。这种记忆盲的门控机制是领先delta规则架构（GDN-2）的三个耦合缺陷之一：值轴擦除掩码在值投影尺度上浪费参数，且——如我们所证明——数学上阻止了使循环训练与Transformer竞争的WY形式三角块求解器。 我们提出CARVE（语义感知循环与值效率），通过一个原则解决所有三个问题：仅在键轴上擦除。这被证明是WY形式求解器保持有效所必需且充分的。在该框架内，CARVE重用已写入GPU内存的循环输出张量作为免费的内容信号供擦除门使用，并将每个值的写入门投影替换为每个头的单个标量。初始化时，CARVE与GDN-2完全位一致；任何性能差异源于内容门所学习的内容。 在1.3B参数、100B标记训练下，CARVE在WikiText上实现困惑度15.72（较GDN-2降低0.18，相当于4.5个标准差），在九个常识推理基准上超越所有循环基线，并在所有RULER检索探针上达到最先进水平——仅带来0.4%吞吐量开销、峰值内存降低13%、参数减少19%。六项形式定理涵盖记忆容量、李雅普诺夫稳定性、梯度流、表达能力分离、帕累托最优块大小及混合最优性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of memory-blind gating in recurrent models like GDN-2, which wastes parameters and blocks competitive training via triangular chunk solvers, CARVE introduces a content-aware erase mechanism that operates only on the key axis, resolving three key architectural flaws. It leverages the recurrent output tensor as a free content signal and replaces per-value write-gates with scalar per-head controls, maintaining bit-identical initialization to GDN-2 while enabling learned content-based erasure. Experimentally, at 1.3B parameters trained on 100B tokens, CARVE achieves 15.72 perplexity on WikiText (a 4.5-sigma improvement over GDN-2), outperforms all recurrent baselines on nine reasoning benchmarks, and sets new state-of-the-art on RULER retrieval probes with 0.4% throughput overhead, 13% lower peak memory, and 19% fewer parameters.
为解决现有循环架构（如GDN-2）中记忆盲擦除机制导致的参数浪费、无法支持高效三角块求解器及内存效率低下等问题，CARVE提出仅在键轴上进行擦除的感知内容机制，从理论上证明该设计是维持高效块求解器的必要且充分条件。其方法复用已写入GPU内存的循环输出张量作为免费内容信号，并将每值写门投影替换为每头标量控制，初始化与GDN-2完全一致，性能差异源于内容门的学习能力。实验表明，CARVE在1.3B参数、100B token训练下，WikiText困惑度达15.72（较GDN-2提升4.5个标准差），在九项常识推理基准上全面领先所有循环基线，并在RULER检索评测中达到最先进水平，同时仅增加0.4%吞吐开销、降低13%峰值内存与19%参数量。
Compositionality and the lexicon in evolutionary semantics
Authors: Fausto Carcassi
First: 2026-06-25T16:16:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:16:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Formal semantics has shown that sentence meanings arise by recursively composing lexical meanings, yet much of the literature on semantic universals models either lexicons with fixed signal structures or holistic composition without interpretable lexical parts. We introduce a framework that integrates this fundamental insight of formal semantics in evolutionary modeling, by allowing lexical meanings and a composition function to co-evolve under pressures for conceptual simplicity and communicative accuracy. We apply this framework to the evolution of quantificational meaning. Analyzing the Pareto frontier, we find that the most well-known semantic universal, conservativity, emerges as an efficient system-wide abstraction. The account is sensitive to syntactic structure and helps reconcile tensions between empirical evidence on quantifier learnability and prior evolutionary models. More broadly, the results demonstrate that the picture of sentential meaning developed in formal semantics can be productively combined with evolutionary modeling. The framework offers a template for studying universals that involve global compression within a grammatical category, semantic specialization of syntactic arguments, and the co-evolution of lexical and compositional meaning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：演化语义学中的组合性与词典
形式语义学表明，句子意义通过递归组合词义产生，但语义普遍性文献大多采用固定信号结构的词典或整体组合而不具可解释词义部分的模型。我们引入一个框架，将形式语义学这一基本洞见整合进演化建模，允许词义与组合函数在概念简洁性和交流准确性压力下共同演化。我们将该框架应用于量化意义的演化研究。通过分析帕累托前沿，我们发现最著名的语义普遍性——保守性——作为系统级高效抽象自然浮现。该模型对句法结构敏感，有助于调和量化词习得的实证证据与先前演化模型之间的张力。更广泛地说，结果表明形式语义学中发展出的句子意义图景可与演化建模有效结合。该框架为研究涉及语法类别内全局压缩、句法论元的语义特化以及词义与组合义共同演化的普遍性提供了模板。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reconcile formal semantics' compositional view of meaning with evolutionary models that often neglect lexical structure, this study introduces a framework where lexical meanings and composition functions co-evolve under pressures for conceptual simplicity and communicative accuracy. Applied to quantificational meaning, the model identifies conservativity as an emergent semantic universal on the Pareto frontier, demonstrating its efficiency as a system-wide abstraction. The framework also accounts for syntactic structure and bridges empirical findings on quantifier learnability with prior evolutionary models, showing that formal semantics and evolutionary modeling can productively interact to explain semantic universals involving global compression and lexical-compositional co-evolution.
为弥合形式语义学中句义组合性观点与演化模型常忽略词项结构之间的鸿沟，本研究提出一个框架，使词义与组合函数在概念简洁性和交际准确性压力下共同演化。应用于量化意义演化时，模型发现保守性——一个广为人知的语义普遍性——在帕累托前沿上作为高效系统性抽象自然浮现。该方法考虑句法结构，弥合了量化词习得实证数据与传统演化模型间的矛盾，表明形式语义学与演化建模可相互促进。
Ask, Don't Judge: Binary Questions for Interpretable LLM Evaluation and Self-Improvement
Authors: Sangwoo Cho, Kushal Chawla, Pengshan Cai, Zefang Liu, Chenyang Zhu, Shi-Xiong Zhang, Sambit Sahu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-25T16:14:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:14:50+00:00
Comments: Acceepted to the Second Workshop on Compositional Learning at ICML 2026, Seoul, South Korea
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating LLM outputs remains a major bottleneck in NLP: human evaluation is expensive and slow, lexical metrics correlate poorly with human judgments on open-ended generation, and holistic LLM judges often produce opaque scores that are hard to debug. We propose BINEVAL, a framework that decomposes evaluation criteria into atomic binary questions and aggregates the resulting verdicts into interpretable, multi-dimensional scores. Given a task prompt, a meta-prompt generates fine-grained evaluation questions, and an LLM answers them independently for each output, yielding transparent question-level feedback together with calibrated overall scores. This decomposition makes evaluation easier to inspect, easier to diagnose, and directly usable for prompt improvement. Across SummEval, Topical-Chat, and QAGS, BINEVAL matches or outperforms strong baselines including UniEval and G-Eval, with especially strong results on factual consistency benchmarks such as QAGS. Beyond competitive correlation with human judgments, BINEVAL better matches human score distributions and avoids the ceiling effects common in prior LLM judges, leading to better discrimination between borderline and clearly flawed outputs. We further show that the same question-level feedback supports iterative prompt optimization, improving evaluator prompts on summarization and generation prompts on IFBench under both self-update and cross-model update settings. Overall, BINEVAL provides a task-agnostic, training-free, and interpretable evaluation framework that combines strong empirical performance with practical diagnostic and optimization value.
中文标题/摘要
标题：提问而非评判：用于可解释LLM评估与自我改进的二元问题
评估LLM输出仍是自然语言处理中的主要瓶颈：人工评估成本高且缓慢，词汇指标与人类对开放生成的判断相关性差，而整体LLM评估者常产生难以调试的模糊评分。我们提出BINEVAL框架，将评估标准分解为原子级二元问题，并将结果裁决聚合为可解释的多维评分。给定任务提示，元提示生成细粒度评估问题，LLM独立回答每个输出，从而提供透明的问题级反馈与校准后的整体评分。这种分解使评估更易检查、更易诊断，且可直接用于提示优化。在SummEval、Topical-Chat和QAGS等多个基准上，BINEVAL匹配或超越UniEval和G-Eval等强基线，尤其在事实一致性基准（如QAGS）上表现突出。除与人类判断高度相关外，BINEVAL更贴近人类评分分布，避免了先前LLM评估者常见的天花板效应，从而更有效区分边缘与明显错误输出。我们进一步表明，相同的问题级反馈支持迭代提示优化，在自更新和跨模型更新设置下，可提升摘要和生成提示在IFBench上的表现。总体而言，BINEVAL提供了一种任务无关、无需训练、可解释的评估框架，兼具强大实证性能与实用诊断优化价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of human evaluation and the opacity of existing LLM-based evaluators, the authors propose BINEVAL, a framework that breaks down evaluation criteria into atomic binary questions answered by an LLM for each output, then aggregates results into interpretable, multi-dimensional scores. This method enables transparent, question-level feedback and calibrated overall scores without requiring training. Experiments across SummEval, Topical-Chat, and QAGS show BINEVAL matches or exceeds strong baselines like UniEval and G-Eval, particularly excelling on factual consistency benchmarks. It also better aligns with human score distributions, avoids ceiling effects, and improves discrimination between borderline and flawed outputs. Furthermore, the same feedback supports prompt optimization, enhancing performance on summarization and generation tasks under both self-update and cross-model update settings.
为解决人工评估成本高、LLM综合评分不透明的问题，作者提出BINEVAL框架，将评价标准分解为原子级二元问题，由LLM对每个输出独立作答，再聚合为可解释的多维评分。该方法通过元提示生成细粒度问题，输出问题级反馈与校准后的总体评分，便于诊断与优化。在SummEval、Topical-Chat和QAGS等任务上，BINEVAL表现优于或持平UniEval和G-Eval等基线，尤其在事实一致性任务上表现突出，评分分布更贴近人类判断且避免天花板效应，同时支持迭代提示优化，在摘要和生成任务中提升提示效果。
Organizing in the Digital Age: Understanding Community, Challenges, and Consequences in Digitally-facilitated Labor Organizing
Authors: Frederick Reiber, Alishah Chator, Dana Calacci, Allison McDonald
First: 2026-06-18T15:36:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:13:17+00:00
Comments: To appear in CSCW 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The contemporary American labor force is highly dispersed, necessitating the use of digital communication tools to bridge spatial and temporal gaps in union organizing. This study provides an in-depth analysis of how workers within various labor unions utilize digital, text-based communication platforms -- including Discord, WhatsApp, and Slack -- for labor organizing. Through 17 qualitative interviews, we examine the challenges and opportunities presented by digital organizing, identifying both technical and social obstacles. Our findings reveal that although digital tools are integral to contemporary labor successes, they also introduce new complexities, such as navigating technical security, managing information overload, and building trust and consensus. Based on these insights, we draw connections to broader understandings of digital organizing and the role of digital tools in unions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：数字时代的组织：理解数字赋能劳动组织中的社群、挑战与后果
当代美国劳动力高度分散，需要借助数字通信工具弥合工会组织中的空间与时间差距。本研究深入分析了不同工会工人如何利用数字文本通信平台（包括Discord、WhatsApp和Slack）进行劳动组织。通过17次定性访谈，我们考察了数字组织带来的挑战与机遇，识别出技术与社会层面的障碍。研究发现，尽管数字工具是当代劳动成功的关键，但也引入了新复杂性，如技术安全、信息过载管理及信任与共识构建。基于这些洞察，我们将其与更广泛的数字组织理解及数字工具在工会中的作用相联系。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the spatial dispersion of the modern American workforce, this study investigates how labor unions leverage digital text-based platforms like Discord, WhatsApp, and Slack to organize workers. Through 17 qualitative interviews, researchers identify both the enabling potential and the emerging challenges of digital organizing, including technical security concerns, information overload, and difficulties in building trust. Findings show that while digital tools are essential for contemporary union success, they also introduce new organizational complexities that require careful management.
本研究探讨了美国分散劳动力如何利用Discord、WhatsApp和Slack等文字平台进行劳工组织，动机在于克服空间与时间上的组织障碍。通过对17位劳工的深度访谈，研究分析了数字组织中的技术安全、信息过载和信任构建等挑战。结果表明，尽管数字工具对现代劳工运动至关重要，但也带来了需要谨慎应对的新复杂性。
SatSplatDiff: Geometry-preserving generative refinement for high-fidelity satellite Gaussian Splatting
Authors: Jiyong Kim, Shuang Song, Ronjgun Qin
First: 2026-06-25T16:12:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:12:36+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 15 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Gaussian Splatting has been recently explored for satellite 3D reconstruction, demonstrating flexibility and efficiency in representing radiometrically diverse satellite scenes. However, the limited top viewpoint of satellite imagery results in insufficient supervision on building facades, leaving surface holes and degraded visual fidelity. Generative refinement, which leverages pretrained generative priors to iteratively refine and update the rendered images used as supervision targets, has recently been investigated to improve the visual fidelity of Gaussian-rendered images. However, since these models refine each view independently, the resulting images can generate hallucinations and break photo-consistency, leading to geometric degradation. To address these limitations, we propose SatSplatDiff, which aims to minimize geometric degradation prevalent in generative refinement. Building on photogrammetric DSM initialization and 2DGS-based shadow casting established in our prior work SatSplat, we first introduce monocular depth supervision and multi-scale geometric refinement to establish a geometrically accurate and well-regularized surface representation. We then apply shadow-guided generative refinement, where geometrically calculated shadow maps guide the Gaussians to maintain consistency with the underlying geometry, improving visual fidelity while reducing geometric degradation. Extensive evaluations on the IARPA2016 and DFC2019 datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, reducing geometric MAE by up to 18% and improving visual fidelity (FID-CLIP) by 28-45% over existing baselines. Our method delivers up to 5x resolution enhancement with minimal hallucination and sensor-consistent appearance, demonstrating seamless cross-tile consistency and strong scalability for large-scale reconstruction. Source code is available at https://github.com/GDAOSU/SatSplatDiff
中文标题/摘要
标题：SatSplatDiff：保持几何一致性的生成式细化方法，用于高保真度卫星高斯点渲染
高斯点渲染近期被用于卫星三维重建，展示了其在表示辐射特性多样的卫星场景时的灵活性与效率。然而，卫星影像的有限俯视视角导致建筑立面监督不足，造成表面孔洞和视觉保真度下降。生成式细化方法通过利用预训练生成先验，迭代优化用作监督目标的渲染图像，以提升高斯渲染图像的视觉保真度。但现有方法因独立优化每个视角，易产生幻觉并破坏照片一致性，导致几何退化。为解决此问题，我们提出SatSplatDiff，旨在最小化生成式细化中普遍存在的几何退化。基于我们先前工作SatSplat中建立的摄影测量DSM初始化与基于2DGS的阴影投射方法，我们首先引入单目深度监督与多尺度几何细化，构建几何精确且正则化的表面表示。随后应用阴影引导生成式细化，通过几何计算的阴影图引导高斯点保持与底层几何的一致性，从而提升视觉保真度并减少几何退化。在IARPA2016和DFC2019数据集上的广泛评估表明，本方法达到当前最优性能，几何MAE降低最高达18%，视觉保真度（FID-CLIP）较现有基线提升28-45%。本方法可实现最高5倍分辨率增强，且幻觉极少、外观传感器一致，展现出无缝跨瓦片一致性与大规模重建的强扩展性。源代码见https://github.com/GDAOSU/SatSplatDiff

Motivated by the geometric degradation and visual fidelity issues in generative refinement of satellite Gaussian Splatting, this work proposes SatSplatDiff to preserve geometry while enhancing image quality. The method combines photogrammetric DSM initialization and 2DGS-based shadow casting from prior work, then introduces monocular depth supervision and multi-scale geometric refinement to establish a regularized surface representation. It further applies shadow-guided generative refinement, using computed shadow maps to constrain Gaussian updates and maintain photo-consistency. Learning from the Self-future: On-policy Self-distillation for dLLMs
Authors: Yifu Luo, Zeyu Chen, Haoyu Wang, Xinhao Hu, Yuxuan Zhang, Zhizhou Sha, Shiwei Liu
First: 2026-06-16T17:24:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:08:43+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has proven effective for post-training large language models (LLMs), yet its application to diffusion LLMs (dLLMs) remains unexplored. Existing OPSD methods are inherently autoregressive-centric. They inject privileged information via left-to-right prefix conditioning with token-level divergence supervision, a design that fundamentally conflicts with the arbitraryorder generation of dLLMs. We introduce d-OPSD, the first OPSD framework tailored for dLLMs. Our approach makes two core contributions. First, we reframe self-teacher construction by using self-generated answers as suffix conditioning, enabling the student model to learn from "self future-experience" rather than privileged prefixes. Second, we shift supervision from token-level to step-level, aligning training with the iterative denoising process of dLLMs. Experiments across four reasoning benchmarks show that d-OPSD consistently outperforms RLVR and SFT baselines with superior sample efficiency, requiring only around 10% of the optimization steps by RLVR and opening a promising pathway for dLLM posttraining. The code is available at https://github.com/xingzhejun/d-OPSD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从自我未来中学习：面向扩散大语言模型的在线自蒸馏
在线自蒸馏（OPSD）已被证明在大语言模型（LLMs）的后训练中有效，但其在扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）中的应用尚未探索。现有OPSD方法本质上以自回归为中心，通过左到右前缀条件和词级差异监督注入特权信息，这与dLLMs的任意顺序生成机制根本冲突。我们提出了d-OPSD，这是首个专为dLLMs设计的OPSD框架。我们的方法有两个核心贡献：首先，通过自生成答案作为后缀条件重构自教师构建，使学生模型从“自我未来经验”中学习而非特权前缀；其次，将监督从词级转移到步级，使训练与dLLMs的迭代去噪过程对齐。在四个推理基准上的实验表明，d-OPSD在样本效率上显著优于RLVR和SFT基线，仅需RLVR约10%的优化步数，为dLLM后训练开辟了有前景的路径。代码见https://github.com/xingzhejun/d-OPSD。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to adapt on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) to diffusion LLMs (dLLMs), which differ fundamentally from autoregressive models, the authors propose d-OPSD, the first OPSD framework designed for dLLMs. The method constructs a self-teacher using suffix conditioning from self-generated outputs, enabling the student to learn from its own future experience rather than privileged prefixes, and shifts supervision from token-level to step-level to align with the denoising process of dLLMs. Experimental results across four reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that d-OPSD outperforms RLVR and SFT baselines with significantly better sample efficiency, achieving comparable or superior performance using only about 10% of RLVR’s optimization steps.
为填补扩散式大语言模型缺乏自监督蒸馏方法的空白，本文提出d-OPSD，这是首个专为dLLMs设计的自蒸馏框架。其核心创新在于以自生成的后缀作为自教师构建方式，替代传统前缀条件，同时将监督信号从词级调整为步级，以匹配扩散模型的去噪过程。在四个推理基准上的实验表明，d-OPSD显著优于RLVR与SFT基线，样本效率更高，仅需RLVR约10%的优化步数即可达到相近性能。
Hierarchical Muon: Tiled Newton-Schulz Updates for Efficient Muon Optimization
Authors: Ziyuan Tang, Tianshi Xu, Yousef Saad, Yuanzhe Xi
First: 2026-06-25T16:05:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:05:55+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 10 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Muon-type optimizers construct update directions for dense neural-network weights by applying a finite Newton-Schulz map to momentum-gradient matrices. For an $H \times W$ matrix, with $r=\min\{H,W\}$ and $s=\max\{H,W\}$, $K$ steps of the full-matrix Newton-Schulz update require $O(r^2 s K)$ work and couple all rows and columns through repeated Gram matrix products. We introduce Hierarchical Muon (HiMuon), a tiled Newton-Schulz scheme for Muon-type optimization. HiMuon partitions each momentum-gradient matrix into $T \times T$ tiles, applies the same finite Newton-Schulz map independently to each tile, and reassembles the results. For finite $T$ below the matrix dimensions, HiMuon defines a local matrix-function map rather than a convergent approximation to the full-matrix update: spectral interactions are preserved within tiles and discarded across tile boundaries. For fixed finite $T$, the leading Newton-Schulz work decreases to $O(H W T K)$, and the computation decomposes into independent small dense matrix operations. This structure enables tile-size-dependent GPU kernels, cross-layer batching, memory-bounded chunking, and runtime tile-size schedules. Experiments on transformer training and controlled matrix-function diagnostics show that HiMuon improves optimizer-step efficiency while keeping training behavior close to full-matrix Muon in the tested regimes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分层穆昂：用于高效穆昂优化的分块牛顿-舒尔兹更新
穆昂型优化器通过将有限的牛顿-舒尔兹映射应用于动量-梯度矩阵，为稠密神经网络权重构建更新方向。对于一个 $H \times W$ 矩阵，设 $r=\min\{H,W\}$，$s=\max\{H,W\}$，完整矩阵的牛顿-舒尔兹更新 $K$ 步需要 $O(r^2 s K)$ 的计算量，并通过重复的格拉姆矩阵乘积耦合所有行和列。我们引入分层穆昂（HiMuon），一种针对穆昂型优化的分块牛顿-舒尔兹方案。HiMuon 将每个动量-梯度矩阵划分为 $T \times T$ 的块，对每个块独立应用相同的有限牛顿-舒尔兹映射，然后重组结果。对于小于矩阵维度的有限 $T$，HiMuon 定义的是局部矩阵函数映射，而非对完整矩阵更新的收敛近似：谱交互在块内保留，块间则被舍弃。对于固定的有限 $T$，主导的牛顿-舒尔兹计算量降低至 $O(H W T K)$，计算分解为独立的小规模稠密矩阵运算。该结构支持块大小相关的 GPU 内核、跨层批处理、内存受限的分块以及运行时块大小调度。在 Transformer 训练和受控矩阵函数诊断实验中，HiMuon 在测试范围内提升了优化器步的效率，同时保持了与完整矩阵穆昂相近的训练行为。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost and memory bottlenecks of full-matrix Newton-Schulz updates in Muon-type optimizers, the paper proposes Hierarchical Muon (HiMuon), a tiled approach that partitions momentum-gradient matrices into smaller T×T blocks, applies the Newton-Schulz map independently to each tile, and reassembles the results. This method reduces the leading computational complexity from O(r²sK) to O(HWT K) for matrices of size H×W, enabling efficient GPU kernels and memory-bounded processing. Experimental results on transformer training and matrix-function diagnostics demonstrate that HiMuon maintains training performance comparable to full-matrix Muon while significantly improving step efficiency through localized computation and runtime tile scheduling.
为解决Muon型优化器中全矩阵Newton-Schulz更新带来的高计算开销与内存瓶颈，本文提出分块式Muon（HiMuon），将动量-梯度矩阵划分为T×T小块，对每块独立应用有限Newton-Schulz映射，再重组结果。该方法将计算复杂度从O(r²sK)降至O(HWT K)，支持GPU内核定制、跨层批处理与内存受限分块，同时允许动态调整分块大小。实验表明，在Transformer训练与矩阵函数诊断中，HiMuon显著提升优化步效率，且训练行为与全矩阵Muon方法在测试条件下高度一致。
Vulnerability of Natural Language Classifiers to Evolutionary Generated Adversarial Text
Authors: Manjinder Singh, Alexander E. I. Brownlee, Mohamed Elawady
First: 2026-06-25T16:05:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:05:38+00:00
Comments: 24 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep learning models have achieved impressive performance across various fields but remain vulnerable to adversarial inputs, particularly in NLP, where such attacks can have significant real-world consequences. Adversarial attacks often involve small, semantically similar token replacements to fool NLP models, and recent methods have become more precise by targeting specific vulnerable words, often by exploiting some level of access to the model's internal structure. This paper proposes GAversary, a hybrid Genetic Algorithm (GA) to generate adversarial attacks on natural language models. The GA is able to treat the target model as a black box, requiring only the logit value output by the model to guide the search. GAversary differs from GAs previously proposed for this problem by using GloVe embeddings to propose word replacements (the mutation operator) to improve the semantic similarity of the adversarial examples. GAversary is applied to several benchmark data sets and well-known target models. GAversary is able to substantially reduce the target model's accuracy on test data compared to the BAE and A2T attacks compared against (in the best case, reducing a 76.8% accuracy to 5.8%, compared to BAE's 27.6%). The trade-off is that GAversary perturbs just under twice as many words as the other two methods, with a slightly lower semantic similarity to the original text and around a 5% increase in run-time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自然语言分类器对进化生成对抗性文本的脆弱性
深度学习模型在多个领域取得了令人瞩目的性能，但在对抗性输入面前仍存在脆弱性，特别是在自然语言处理（NLP）领域，此类攻击可能带来重大现实后果。对抗性攻击通常通过替换语义相近的小量词元来欺骗NLP模型，而近期方法通过针对特定脆弱词并利用对模型内部结构的部分访问权限，提高了攻击精度。本文提出GAversary，一种混合遗传算法（GA），用于生成针对自然语言模型的对抗性攻击。该算法可将目标模型视为黑盒，仅需模型输出的logit值来引导搜索。GAversary与此前针对该问题提出的GA不同，它使用GloVe词嵌入提出词元替换（变异操作），以提升对抗样本的语义相似性。GAversary在多个基准数据集和知名目标模型上进行了应用，相较于BAE和A2T攻击方法，其在测试数据上显著降低了目标模型的准确率（最佳情况下，将76.8%的准确率降至5.8%，而BAE仅为27.6%）。代价是GAversary对文本的扰动词数约为其他两种方法的两倍，语义相似性略低，运行时间增加约5%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of natural language classifiers to adversarial inputs, this paper introduces GAversary, a hybrid Genetic Algorithm designed to generate adversarial text without requiring access to the model’s internal structure, using only logit outputs. The method employs GloVe embeddings to guide mutation operations, ensuring semantically plausible word substitutions while maximizing attack effectiveness. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show GAversary significantly degrades model accuracy—reducing it from 76.8% to 5.8% in the best case—outperforming BAE and A2T attacks, albeit at the cost of perturbing nearly twice as many tokens, slightly lower semantic fidelity, and a 5% longer runtime.
该论文针对自然语言分类器对对抗攻击的脆弱性，提出GAversary——一种黑盒遗传算法，仅需模型输出的logits即可引导搜索。为提升NLP领域对抗样本的精准性，GAversary利用GloVe词向量生成语义相近的词替换，无需模型内部结构。实验表明，GAversary在多个基准数据集上显著优于BAE和A2T方法，将目标模型准确率从76.8%降至5.8%，但代价是扰动词数近翻倍、语义相似度略低，且运行时间增加约5%。
Paved with True Intents: Intent-Aware Training Improves LLM Safety Classification Across Training Regimes
Authors: Jeremias Ferrao, Niclas Müller-Hof, Iustin Sîrbu, Traian Rebedea, Yftah Ziser
First: 2026-06-25T16:03:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:03:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We argue that safety classifiers should model user intent as an explicit signal between the prompt and the final label. To study this, we introduce AIMS, a human-annotated dataset of 1,724 difficult safety prompts, each paired with an intent description and harm label. We use AIMS to evaluate intent-aware training across supervised fine-tuning, preference learning, reasoning distillation, and reinforcement learning. Despite its size, AIMS enables competitive safety classifiers across training regimes: DPO from model-generated intent errors improves over SFT, and intent-conditioned distillation outperforms reasoning-only distillation in most teacher-student pairs. Most notably, directly rewarding intent faithfulness with GRPO yields the strongest average performance across five external safety benchmarks, while our intent-aware models form the inference latency-F1 Pareto frontier. These results show that faithful intent modeling is a compact, high-quality supervision signal for more robust safety classifiers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：以真实意图铺路：意图感知训练提升大语言模型安全分类能力
我们认为，安全分类器应将用户意图建模为提示与最终标签之间的显式信号。为此，我们引入AIMS数据集，该数据集包含1724个困难安全提示，每个提示均配以意图描述和危害标签。我们利用AIMS评估意图感知训练在监督微调、偏好学习、推理蒸馏和强化学习等不同训练范式中的表现。尽管AIMS规模不大，却能在各类训练范式中实现竞争力的安全分类器：基于模型生成意图错误的DPO优于SFT，意图条件蒸馏在多数教师-学生对中优于仅推理蒸馏。最值得注意的是，直接用GRPO奖励意图忠实度，在五个外部安全基准测试中获得最强平均性能，而我们的意图感知模型构成推理延迟- F1帕累托前沿。这些结果表明，忠实意图建模是一种紧凑且高质量的监督信号，可提升安全分类器的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more robust safety classification in large language models, this study proposes intent-aware training, treating user intent as an explicit signal between prompts and safety labels. Using the AIMS dataset—a human-annotated collection of 1,724 challenging safety prompts with intent descriptions and harm labels—the researchers evaluate intent-aware methods across supervised fine-tuning, preference learning, reasoning distillation, and reinforcement learning. Results show that intent-aware approaches consistently outperform baseline methods: DPO using model-generated intent errors improves over SFT, intent-conditioned distillation surpasses reasoning-only distillation in most teacher-student pairs, and GRPO directly rewarding intent faithfulness achieves the highest average performance across five safety benchmarks, while also forming the latency-F1 Pareto frontier.
为提升大语言模型的安全分类能力，本研究提出将用户意图作为提示与安全标签之间的显式信号进行建模。基于人工标注的AIMS数据集（含1724个复杂安全提示及其意图描述与危害标签），作者在监督微调、偏好学习、推理蒸馏和强化学习等多种训练范式中评估了意图感知方法。实验表明，意图感知方法普遍优于基线：使用模型生成意图错误的DPO优于SFT，意图条件蒸馏在多数教师-学生对中超越纯推理蒸馏，而直接通过GRPO奖励意图忠实度获得最高跨五个外部安全基准的平均性能，同时在推理延迟与F1分数的帕累托前沿上表现最优。
Syntactic Belief Update as the Driver of Garden Path Processing Difficulty
Authors: Alan Zhou, Miloš Stanojević, John T. Hale
First: 2026-06-25T16:02:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:02:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Garden path sentences present a processing difficulty for humans -- the sentence prefix leads the listener towards one interpretation, until the listener hears a critical word that shows that the initial interpretation was wrong. Lexical surprisal, a measure that usually predicts sentence processing difficulty quite well, fails to provide good predictions for garden path sentences. We propose an alternative that actively predicts a probability distribution over syntactic trees (its syntactic belief) and updates that distribution after each new word. If a processor is led down a garden path, syntactic beliefs will be wrong and will require a large update at the critical word. The magnitude of the update is measured with a generalized Rényi divergence. Crucially, this metric is dependent on lexical items, but is fully independent of the probability of lexical items. This Syntactic Belief Update provides a better fit to the human reading time data on garden path sentences. This suggests a new research direction examining purely non-lexical alternatives to surprisal for psycholinguistics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：句法信念更新作为花园路径句处理困难的驱动因素
花园路径句对人类处理构成困难——句子前缀引导听者朝向一种解释，直到听者听到一个关键词，表明初始解释错误。词频意外性（lexical surprisal），通常能很好地预测句子处理难度，但在花园路径句中预测效果不佳。 我们提出一种替代方案：主动预测句法树的概率分布（即句法信念），并在每个新词后更新该分布。若处理者被引导进入花园路径，句法信念将错误，并在关键词处需要大幅更新。更新幅度通过广义Rényi散度衡量。关键点在于，该指标依赖于词汇项，但完全独立于词汇项的概率。这种句法信念更新更贴合人类在花园路径句中的阅读时间数据。这提示心理学语言学研究可探索纯非词汇替代词频意外性的新方向。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the failure of lexical surprisal to predict processing difficulty in garden path sentences, where initial syntactic interpretations are overturned by later words. The authors propose a syntactic belief update model that maintains and dynamically revises a probability distribution over syntactic trees after each word, measuring the cost of revision via a generalized Rényi divergence. This divergence, unlike surprisal, depends on lexical items but not their probabilities, making it sensitive to structural reinterpretation. Experimental results show that this model better predicts human reading times in garden path sentences, suggesting that syntactic reanalysis, not lexical surprise, drives processing difficulty.
该研究针对词频惊讶度在解释花园路径句处理难度时的不足，提出语法信念更新模型：在每接收一个词后，动态更新对句法树的概率分布。通过广义Rényi散度衡量信念更新幅度，该指标与人类阅读时间高度相关，优于词频惊讶度。关键在于，该散度依赖词项本身但不依赖其概率，暗示了非词项层面的加工机制。
Graph Neural Networks Applications Across Domains: All Insights You Need
Authors: Abderaouf Bahi
First: 2026-06-25T16:00:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:00:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph neural networks have moved from a niche representation-learning technique to the default model class wherever data carry relational structure. The interesting question is no longer whether message passing helps on a given dataset, but where graph structure earns its computational cost and where it does not. This survey organises the field around a single design space, derives the spectral and spatial formulations from shared first principles, and connects expressive power to the Weisfeiler-Leman hierarchy with explicit statements of what current architectures can and cannot separate. Against that methodological backbone we examine twelve application domains, among them recommendation and social networks, knowledge graphs and language-model integration, drug discovery and molecular property learning, healthcare and neuroscience, computer vision, traffic and urban computing, power and renewable-energy systems, wireless and sixth-generation networks, fraud and cybersecurity, industrial prognostics, materials science, and climate modelling. For each domain we specify the graph-construction choices and their costs, identify which architecture families dominate and why, and separate reported gains from artefacts of weak baselines or favourable splits. A cross-domain comparison exposes recurring patterns: heterophily and scale undercut the same models almost everywhere, temporal graphs remain harder than their static counterparts, and the architectures that top public leaderboards are seldom the ones that reach deployment. We treat over-smoothing, over-squashing, robustness, distribution shift, fairness, and explainability not as a closing checklist but as the constraints that decide adoption.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图神经网络在各领域的应用：您所需的所有见解
图神经网络已从一种小众的表示学习技术演变为在数据具有关系结构的任何领域中默认的模型类别。有趣的问题不再是否消息传递对特定数据集有帮助，而是图结构在何处值得其计算成本，又在何处不值得。本综述围绕单一设计空间组织该领域，从共同的基本原理推导出谱和空间形式，并将表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层次结构明确关联，说明当前架构能和不能区分的内容。在此方法论基础上，我们考察了十二个应用领域，包括推荐系统与社交网络、知识图谱与语言模型集成、药物发现与分子属性学习、医疗与神经科学、计算机视觉、交通与城市计算、电力与可再生能源系统、无线与第六代网络、欺诈与网络安全、工业预测、材料科学和气候建模。对每个领域，我们指定图构建选择及其成本，识别主导架构家族及其原因，并区分报告的收益与弱基线或有利划分造成的伪影。跨领域比较揭示了重复出现的模式：异质性与规模几乎在所有地方削弱同一类模型，时序图比其静态对应物更难处理，而排名领先公共排行榜的架构往往并非实际部署的架构。我们将过平滑、过压缩、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性与可解释性不视为收尾清单，而是决定采用与否的约束条件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing ubiquity of graph neural networks in relational data modeling, this survey investigates where graph structures provide computational value and where they do not, by unifying spectral and spatial formulations under shared theoretical principles and linking expressiveness to the Weisfeiler-Leman hierarchy. It systematically reviews twelve application domains, detailing graph construction trade-offs, dominant architecture families, and distinguishing genuine performance gains from artifacts of weak baselines or data splits. Key findings reveal that heterophily and scale consistently degrade model performance, temporal graphs remain more challenging than static ones, and architectures dominating public benchmarks rarely translate to real-world deployment, while issues like over-smoothing, robustness, and explainability are framed as critical adoption constraints rather than optional features.
鉴于图神经网络在关系数据建模中的广泛应用，本综述旨在探究图结构在哪些场景下具有计算价值、哪些场景下无益，通过统一谱域与空间域方法论并关联表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层次，系统分析十二个应用领域，包括推荐系统、知识图谱、药物发现、医疗健康、计算机视觉等，明确图构建选择的成本、主导架构及其原因，并区分真实性能提升与基线薄弱或数据分割有利带来的假象。主要发现表明，异质性与规模几乎在所有领域都削弱模型表现，时序图比静态图更难处理，而公共排行榜上表现最佳的架构往往难以落地部署，且过平滑、过压缩、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性与可解释性被视作决定实际采纳的关键约束而非可选功能。
Explaining Temporal Graph Neural Networks via Feature-induced Information Flow
Authors: Ping Xiong, Thomas Schnake, Klaus-Robert Müller, Shinichi Nakajima
First: 2026-06-25T16:00:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T16:00:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Event-based Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ETGNNs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of applications, including social network analysis, epidemic tracing, recommender systems, and political event forecasting. However, their increasing complexity poses significant challenges for explainability. Existing explanation methods focus only on a subset of the information flow within ETGNNs, typically tracing contributions from the event-related embeddings to the output. Consequently, they overlook the important pathways through event-induced variables, which mediate interactions between nodes and thereby play a central role in capturing long-range temporal dependencies. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel attribution method that analyzes the \emph{entire} information flow through all event-associated variables. Our method is built upon the recent Normalized Relevance Measure (NRM) framework, which enables explicit quantification of information flow originating from event embeddings as well as information flow passing through event-induced variables. It also ensures comparability of latent variables across layers, and supports higher-order analysis of interactions between events. To handle the architectural complexity of ETGNNs, we extend the NRM framework with a modular decomposition procedure that facilitates the systematic construction of relevance structure for complex neural architectures. We evaluate our approach on two synthetic datasets for epidemic tracing and social dynamics, as well as a real-world dataset of political event networks. Our qualitative and quantitative experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing explanation approaches while producing more human-interpretable explanations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过特征诱导的信息流解释时序图神经网络
基于事件的时序图神经网络（ETGNNs）在社交网络分析、流行病追踪、推荐系统和政治事件预测等广泛应用中表现出色。然而，其日益增长的复杂性对可解释性提出了重大挑战。现有解释方法仅关注ETGNNs信息流的子集，通常仅追踪事件相关嵌入到输出的贡献，因而忽略了通过事件诱导变量的重要路径，这些变量在节点间交互中起中介作用，对捕捉长程时序依赖至关重要。为克服这一局限，我们提出一种新颖的归因方法，分析通过所有事件相关变量的\emph{全部}信息流。该方法基于近期的归一化相关度量（NRM）框架，能够显式量化从事件嵌入出发的信息流以及通过事件诱导变量传递的信息流，同时确保层间潜在变量的可比性，并支持事件间高阶交互分析。为应对ETGNNs的架构复杂性，我们扩展NRM框架，引入模块化分解程序，以系统构建复杂神经架构的相关性结构。我们在两个用于流行病追踪与社交动态的合成数据集，以及一个真实政治事件网络数据集上评估了该方法。定性与定量实验表明，我们的方法在性能上持续优于现有解释方法，同时生成更易被人理解的解释。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to explain complex Event-based Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ETGNNs) that capture long-range temporal dependencies, this work introduces a novel attribution method that traces the full information flow through all event-induced variables, not just direct event embeddings. Building on the Normalized Relevance Measure (NRM) framework, the method decomposes the network’s architecture into modular components to enable layer-wise relevance comparison and higher-order interaction analysis. Evaluated on synthetic epidemic and social dynamics datasets and a real-world political event network, the approach consistently outperforms existing methods in both quantitative metrics and qualitative interpretability, offering clearer insights into how events mediate node interactions over time.
为了解释复杂的时间图神经网络（ETGNN）在捕捉长程时序依赖关系中的机制，该研究提出一种新的归因方法，追踪所有事件诱导变量中的完整信息流，而不仅限于事件嵌入的直接贡献。该方法基于归一化相关度量（NRM）框架，可量化直接与间接信息流，支持跨层变量可比性，并支持高阶交互分析。为应对ETGNN架构复杂性，引入模块化分解过程，系统构建复杂神经网络的归因结构。在合成流行病与社交动态数据集及真实政治事件网络上的实验表明，该方法在定量指标与定性可解释性上均优于现有方法。
Forecasting With LLMs: Improved Generalization Through Feature Steering
Authors: Humzah Merchant, Bradford Levy
First: 2026-06-25T15:59:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T15:59:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Successful forecasting involves identifying patterns between historical and future states of the world which generalize to future observations. We apply LLMs to a variety of forecasting tasks and inspect their internal states using sparse autoencoders to understand whether they appear to rely on time-specific pieces of knowledge versus generalizable patterns. Our analyses identify features associated with both time-aware reasoning and look-ahead-biased reasoning. We then apply the LLMs to an entirely different domain and intervene on these features. We find that amplifying time-awareness features substantially reduces look-ahead bias on forecasting prompts while preserving general reasoning performance. In contrast, steering the candidate look-ahead-bias features does not produce an effect. These results suggest that interpretable temporal features can be used to causally shift LLMs toward more historically grounded reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用大语言模型进行预测：通过特征引导提升泛化能力
成功的预测需要识别历史与未来世界状态之间的模式，并使其泛化到未来的观测。我们对多种预测任务应用大语言模型，并使用稀疏自动编码器检查其内部状态，以判断其是否依赖于时间特定的知识或可泛化的模式。分析发现与时间感知推理和前瞻偏倚推理相关的特征。随后，我们将模型应用于完全不同的领域，并干预这些特征。结果表明，增强时间感知特征可显著减少预测提示中的前瞻偏倚，同时保持一般推理性能；而干预候选前瞻偏倚特征则无明显效果。这些结果表明，可解释的时间特征可用于因果性地引导大语言模型向更基于历史的推理方向转变。
