为提升LLM在预测任务中的泛化能力，本研究通过稀疏自编码器分析其内部状态，探究其是依赖时间特定知识还是可泛化的模式，识别出两类特征：时间感知推理特征和前瞻偏倚推理特征。在新领域中干预这些特征时发现，增强时间感知特征可显著降低前瞻偏倚且不影响一般推理能力，而干预前瞻偏倚特征则无明显效果。这表明可解释的时间特征可因果性地引导LLM向更基于历史的推理方式转变。
Byzantine-Robust Aggregation for Securing Decentralized Federated Learning
Authors: Diego Cajaraville-Aboy, Ana Fernández-Vilas, Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo, Manuel Fernández-Veiga
Venue: IEEE Access, Volume 13 (2025) 190947 - 190963
First: 2024-09-26T11:36:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T15:58:29+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 7 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated Learning (FL) emerges as a distributed machine learning approach that addresses privacy concerns by training AI models locally on devices. Decentralized Federated Learning (DFL) extends the FL paradigm by eliminating the central server, thereby enhancing scalability and robustness through the avoidance of a single point of failure. However, DFL faces significant challenges in optimizing security, as most Byzantine-robust algorithms proposed in the literature are designed for centralized scenarios. In this paper, we present a novel Byzantine-robust aggregation algorithm to enhance the security of Decentralized Federated Learning environments, coined WFAgg. This proposal handles adverse conditions and strengthens the robustness of dynamic decentralized topologies at the same time by employing multiple filters to identify and mitigate Byzantine attacks. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm in maintaining model accuracy and convergence in the presence of various Byzantine attack scenarios, outperforming state-of-the-art centralized Byzantine-robust aggregation schemes (such as Multi-Krum or Clustering). These algorithms are evaluated on an IID image classification problem in both centralized and decentralized scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：拜占庭鲁棒聚合用于保障去中心化联邦学习的安全
联邦学习（FL）作为一种分布式机器学习方法，通过在设备本地训练人工智能模型来应对隐私问题。去中心化联邦学习（DFL）通过消除中心服务器扩展了FL范式，从而通过避免单点故障增强了可扩展性和鲁棒性。然而，DFL在优化安全性方面面临重大挑战，因为文献中提出的大多数拜占庭鲁棒算法都是为集中式场景设计的。本文提出了一种新颖的拜占庭鲁棒聚合算法WFAgg，旨在增强去中心化联邦学习环境的安全性。该算法通过采用多种过滤器识别并缓解拜占庭攻击，同时处理不利条件并强化动态去中心化拓扑结构的鲁棒性。实验结果表明，该算法在面对各种拜占庭攻击场景时，能有效保持模型准确性和收敛性，优于当前最先进的集中式拜占庭鲁棒聚合方案（如Multi-Krum或聚类算法）。这些算法在集中式和去中心化场景下均在IID图像分类问题上进行了评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the security vulnerabilities in decentralized federated learning due to the absence of a central server and the lack of Byzantine-robust methods tailored for such settings, this paper introduces WFAgg, a novel aggregation algorithm designed to defend against Byzantine attacks in dynamic decentralized topologies. WFAgg employs multiple filtering mechanisms to detect and mitigate malicious model updates while preserving convergence and accuracy. Experimental results on IID image classification tasks show that WFAgg maintains superior model performance compared to state-of-the-art centralized Byzantine-robust methods like Multi-Krum and Clustering, both in centralized and decentralized settings.
为提升去中心化联邦学习系统在无中心服务器环境下的抗拜占庭攻击能力，本文提出了一种新型拜占庭鲁棒聚合算法WFAgg。该算法通过多层过滤机制识别并抑制恶意更新，在动态去中心化拓扑中增强系统安全性和收敛稳定性。实验结果表明，在IID图像分类任务中，WFAgg在多种拜占庭攻击场景下仍能保持更高的模型精度和更快的收敛速度，优于Multi-Krum和Clustering等现有中心化方法。
Evaluating Deep Research Agents on Expert Consulting Work: A Benchmark with Verifiers, Rubrics, and Cognitive Traps
Authors: Tanmay Asthana, Aman Saksena, Divyansh Sahu
First: 2026-05-17T17:32:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T15:53:39+00:00
Comments: Updating the paper with more data. Will resubmit
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Frontier deep research agents (DRAs) are being deployed in enterprise workflows faster than they are being evaluated. Existing benchmarks measure factual recall, single-hop QA, or generic agentic skill, and miss the multi-document, decision-grade deliverables DRAs are asked to produce. We introduce a benchmark of 70 SME-authored management consulting prompts, each embedding cognitive traps that penalize surface-pattern reasoning. Three frontier agents, namely Claude Opus~4.6, OpenAI o3-deep-research and Gemini~3.1~Pro deep-research, are scored on two complementary layers: deterministic binary verifiers (mean 14.9 per task) and a five-criterion 0--3 SME rubric (Data Integrity, Analytical Rigor, Relevance \& Focus, Execution Precision, Format \& Deliverability), combined into a Verifier-Rubric Score (VRS, 0--100). Acceptance under a joint threshold (rubric mean $\geq 2.5$ and verifier pass rate $\geq 80\%$) is uniformly low: o3 15.7\%, Claude 12.9\%, Gemini 12.9\%. Pairwise differences are statistically indistinguishable. On the continuous VRS, o3 leads (61.4~[CI: 55.2,\,67.5]), followed by Gemini (52.6) and Claude (38.5); the o3--Claude gap ($Δ{=}22.9$, $p{<}0.001$) survives Bonferroni correction. No agent averages above the rubric's ``adequate'' threshold of 2.0; no agent's mean verifier pass rate reaches the 80\% acceptance floor. Each agent fails distinctively: Claude leads on data fabrication and file-access failures; o3 propagates cascading computation errors; Gemini oscillates between the highest perfect-verifier rate and the most catastrophic collapses. The benchmark, evaluation code, and full prompt corpus are publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于验证器、评分标准和认知陷阱的专家咨询工作评估深度研究代理基准
前沿深度研究代理（DRA）在企业工作流程中的部署速度远快于其评估速度。现有基准仅衡量事实回忆、单跳问答或通用代理技能，而忽略了DRA被要求生成的多文档、决策级交付成果。我们引入了一个包含70个中小企业作者编写的管理咨询提示的基准，每个提示均嵌入认知陷阱以惩罚表面模式推理。三个前沿代理——Claude Opus~4.6、OpenAI o3-deep-research和Gemini~3.1~Pro deep-research——在两个互补层上进行评分：确定性二元验证器（每任务平均14.9分）和五项标准0-3分的中小企业评分标准（数据完整性、分析严谨性、相关性与聚焦度、执行精确度、格式与交付能力），合并为验证器-评分标准得分（VRS，0-100）。在联合阈值下（评分标准平均≥2.5且验证器通过率≥80%）接受度普遍较低：o3为15.7%，Claude为12.9%，Gemini为12.9%。成对差异在统计上无法区分。在连续VRS上，o3领先（61.4 [CI: 55.2, 67.5]），其次是Gemini（52.6）和Claude（38.5）；o3与Claude之间的差距（Δ=22.9，p<0.001）经Bonferroni校正后仍显著。无代理平均分超过评分标准的“基本合格”阈值2.0；无代理的验证器平均通过率达到80%的接受底线。各代理失败方式各异：Claude在数据伪造和文件访问失败方面表现最佳；o3传播级联计算错误；Gemini则在完美验证器通过率最高与最灾难性崩溃之间波动。该基准、评估代码及完整提示语料库已公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of rigorous evaluation for deep research agents in complex enterprise tasks, this study introduces a benchmark of 70 SME-authored consulting prompts designed to expose cognitive traps that penalize shallow reasoning. Three frontier agents—Claude Opus 4.6, OpenAI o3-deep-research, and Gemini 3.1 Pro—are assessed using two complementary metrics: deterministic binary verifiers and a five-criterion SME rubric, combined into a Verifier-Rubric Score (VRS). Results show all agents perform poorly under a joint acceptance threshold (rubric mean ≥2.5 and verifier pass rate ≥80%), with only 15.7% of o3’s outputs meeting criteria. On the continuous VRS scale, o3 leads at 61.4, followed by Gemini (52.6) and Claude (38.5), with a statistically significant gap between o3 and Claude (Δ=22.9, p<0.001). No agent reaches the rubric’s adequate threshold of 2.0 or the 80% verifier pass rate, and each exhibits distinct failure patterns: Claude fabricates data and fails file access, o3 propagates cascading errors, and Gemini alternates between perfect verifier performance and severe collapse.
为应对当前前沿深度研究代理在企业复杂任务中缺乏系统评估的问题，本研究构建了一个包含70个由领域专家编写的管理咨询类提示词的基准测试，旨在揭示易触发认知陷阱的深层推理缺陷。三款前沿代理（Claude Opus 4.6、OpenAI o3-deep-research、Gemini 3.1 Pro）通过两个互补评估维度进行评分：确定性二元验证器与五维专家评分表（数据完整性、分析严谨性、相关性与聚焦度、执行精度、格式与交付能力），综合生成Verifier-Rubric Score（VRS）。结果显示，所有代理在联合通过标准（评分均值≥2.5且验证器通过率≥80%）下表现极差，仅o3达到15.7%。在连续VRS评分中，o3以61.4分领先（置信区间55.2–67.5），Gemini 52.6分，Claude 38.5分，o3与Claude差距显著（Δ=22.9，p<0.001）。无一代理达到评分表“基本合格”阈值2.0或验证器80%通过率，且各代理失败模式各异：Claude多因数据伪造与文件访问失败，o3常因计算错误连锁爆发，Gemini则在完美验证器表现与严重崩溃间反复切换。
LISA: Likelihood Score Alignment for Visual-condition Controllable Generation
Authors: Yanghao Wang, Hongxu Chen, Jiazhen Liu, Zhenqi He, Rui Liu, Zhen Wang, Long Chen
First: 2026-06-25T15:52:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T15:52:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The prevalent dual-branch paradigm, i.e., training a side network to encode visual conditions and fusing its intermediate-layer features to a frozen pretrained main network, has shown remarkable success in visual-condition controllable generation. Despite its widespread adoption, the role of the side branch and its training efficiency remain underexplored. In this paper, we first revisit this mainstream paradigm through the lens of score-based generative modeling: 1) The main network preserves visual perceptual quality by providing a prior unconditional score. 2) The side network steers conditional control by implicitly contributing a likelihood score. Guided by this perspective, we propose LIkelihood Score Alignment (LISA), an effective regularization method that explicitly aligns the intermediate feature of the side network with an approximated likelihood score. Specifically, we first hook features from a designated layer of the side network and project them into the score latent space by a lightweight decoder. Then, we construct an approximated likelihood score target and calculate the distance between the decoder's output and this target as an additional regularization loss. Finally, we jointly optimize the side network and decoder with both standard diffusion loss and our regularization loss. Experiments across various image/video tasks, architectures, and diffusion/flow models demonstrated that LISA can not only consistently accelerate the training convergence and improve final synthetic results, but also encourage the side network's features to be more disentangled for conditional modeling with negligible additional training cost and zero extra inference cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LISA：基于似然分数对齐的视觉条件可控生成
主流的双分支范式——即训练一个侧网络编码视觉条件，并将其中间层特征融合到冻结的预训练主网络——在视觉条件可控生成中取得了显著成功。尽管该范式被广泛采用，但侧网络的作用及其训练效率仍缺乏深入研究。本文从基于分数的生成建模视角重新审视该主流范式：1）主网络通过提供无条件先验分数保持视觉感知质量；2）侧网络通过隐式贡献似然分数实现条件控制。基于此视角，我们提出似然分数对齐（LISA），一种有效的正则化方法，显式对齐侧网络中间特征与近似似然分数。具体而言，我们首先从侧网络指定层提取特征，并通过轻量解码器将其投影到分数潜在空间；接着构建近似似然分数目标，并计算解码器输出与该目标的距离作为额外正则化损失；最后，联合优化侧网络与解码器，同时使用标准扩散损失和我们的正则化损失。在各类图像/视频任务、架构及扩散/流模型上的实验表明，LISA不仅能持续加速训练收敛并提升最终合成效果，还能促使侧网络特征更解耦以支持条件建模，且几乎无额外训练开销和零额外推理开销。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better understand and improve the training efficiency of the dual-branch paradigm in visual-condition controllable generation, this paper introduces LISA, a regularization method that aligns the side network’s intermediate features with an approximated likelihood score. The method hooks features from a designated layer of the side network, projects them into a score latent space via a lightweight decoder, and minimizes the distance between the decoder’s output and a constructed likelihood score target as an additional loss. Jointly optimizing the side network and decoder with standard diffusion loss and this regularization loss, LISA consistently accelerates training convergence and improves final synthetic quality across image and video tasks, while encouraging feature disentanglement for better conditional control with negligible training overhead and zero inference cost.
为提升视觉条件可控生成中侧网络的训练效率与效果，本文从基于分数的生成模型视角重新审视双分支范式，提出LISA方法，通过轻量解码器将侧网络中间特征对齐至近似似然分数，并以距离损失引导联合优化。实验表明，LISA在多种图像与视频任务及架构中均能加速收敛、提升合成质量，并促进条件建模所需的特征解耦，且无需增加推理开销或训练负担。
A Process Harness for Uplifting Legacy Workflows to Agentic BPM: Design and Realization in CUGA FLO
Authors: Fabiana Fournier, Lior Limonad
First: 2026-06-25T15:50:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T15:50:42+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the process harness, a new mechanism for uplifting legacy workflows into Agentic Business Process Management (Agentic BPM) without replacing the underlying workflow engine. A process harness places a policy-governed agentic layer around a deterministic workflow engine, intercepting designated control points to contribute reasoning, adaptation, and oversight while the engine retains structural authority over the process. To define the process harness rigorously, we develop the Task-Decision-Flow (TDF) model, specifying both its data schema and its execution semantics. TDF decomposes LLM reasoning across three policy-governed agent types: a TaskAgent for knowledge-intensive task execution, a DecisionAgent for per-case gateway routing, and a FlowAgent that governs runtime flow adaptation through a principled hook mechanism. Each agent reasons within an explicit policy drawn from the process FRAME, the aggregate policy set governing all LLM calls in the system. We then present CUGA FLO as the design and implementation realization of the TDF model, and demonstrate it on a loan approval workflow that exercises all three agent types and hook-driven regulatory override. The process harness uniquely reconciles imperative requirements, realized through deterministic workflow execution that enforces structural compliance, with normative requirements, realized through policy-framed agentic autonomy invoked at designated control points wherever the process demands it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种用于将遗留工作流提升为代理式业务流程管理的流程框架：在CUGA FLO中的设计与实现
我们引入了流程框架，这是一种无需替换底层工作流引擎即可将遗留工作流提升为代理式业务流程管理（Agentic BPM）的新机制。流程框架在确定性工作流引擎周围部署一个受策略约束的代理层，在指定控制点拦截以贡献推理、适应与监督，同时引擎保留对流程结构的权威控制。为严谨定义流程框架，我们开发了任务-决策-流（TDF）模型，明确其数据模式与执行语义。TDF将大语言模型推理分解为三种受策略约束的代理类型：任务代理用于知识密集型任务执行，决策代理用于每案例网关路由，流代理通过原则性钩子机制控制运行时流程适应。每个代理在来自流程FRAME的显式策略内进行推理，FRAME是系统中所有大语言模型调用的聚合策略集。随后，我们以CUGA FLO作为TDF模型的设计与实现，演示其在贷款审批工作流中的应用，该工作流涵盖三种代理类型及钩子驱动的监管覆盖。流程框架独特地协调了命令式需求（通过确定性工作流执行强制结构合规）与规范式需求（通过在流程所需处的指定控制点调用策略框架代理自主性）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to modernize legacy workflows without discarding existing deterministic engines, the authors propose a process harness that layers agentic capabilities atop such engines. This harness employs the Task-Decision-Flow (TDF) model, which defines three policy-governed agents—TaskAgent, DecisionAgent, and FlowAgent—to handle knowledge-intensive tasks, case-specific routing, and runtime adaptation respectively. Implemented in CUGA FLO, the system demonstrates its effectiveness on a loan approval workflow, where all three agents operate under a unified policy framework (FRAME) and enable regulatory overrides via hook mechanisms, successfully balancing structural compliance with adaptive autonomy.
为在不替换现有确定性工作流引擎的前提下升级遗留工作流，作者提出一种过程束机制，通过政策引导的智能层实现推理、适应与监督。方法构建了任务-决策-流（TDF）模型，将大模型推理分解为三种政策约束的智能体——任务智能体、决策智能体和流智能体，分别负责知识密集型任务、案例路由和运行时流程适应。在CUGA FLO系统中实现该模型，并在贷款审批工作流中验证，成功激活所有智能体并实现基于钩子机制的监管覆盖，证明了如何协调强制性结构合规与规范性智能自主。
HarmVideoBench: Benchmarking Harmful Video Understanding in Large Multimodal Models
Authors: Jiajun Wu, Haoyu Kang, Yining Sun, Jiacheng Hou, Heng Zhang, Danyang Zhang, Zhenjun Zhao, Haochi Zhang, Leixin Sun, Eric Hanchen Jiang, Yushan Li, Ruiyu Li, Mengkai Huang, Yan Gao, Xu Zhang, Guancheng Wan
First: 2026-06-25T15:50:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T15:50:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have recently shown immense potential in automated content moderation, sparking growing interest in developing harmful-video benchmarks. However, we identify two primary limitations in existing works: 1) The multi-layered characteristics of harmful videos are overlooked. Existing benchmarks predominantly formulate evaluation as a binary classification task, failing to capture implicit or deep contextual harms. 2) Explanatory rationales are completely absent. Current frameworks measure exclusively whether a model flags a video correctly rather than explaining why, turning evaluation into a black box where models can succeed through superficial shortcuts. To address these problems, we present HarmVideoBench, a multi-layered diagnostic benchmark comprising 1,379 videos paired with 4,137 multiple-choice questions. HarmVideoBench benchmarks three hierarchical dimensions: Observable Evidence, Clip-Internal Meaning, and Beyond-Clip Reasoning, aiming to evaluate models' deep understanding beyond surface cues with carefully balanced and curated samples. We evaluate 19 leading models on HarmVideoBench to assess their multidimensional understanding of harmful videos. Moreover, we introduce BCR, a benchmark-aligned method that predicts reasoning boundaries and dynamically retrieves context only when needed. Experimental results show that BCR substantially improves the base model's performance in harmful video understanding, raising the macro average from 61.7 percent to a state-of-the-art 84.4 percent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HarmVideoBench：大型多模态模型有害视频理解能力的基准测试
大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）近期在自动化内容审核中展现出巨大潜力，推动了有害视频基准的开发。然而，现有工作存在两大主要局限：1）有害视频的多层次特性被忽视。现有基准主要将评估形式化为二分类任务，无法捕捉隐性或深层语境危害；2）缺乏解释性推理。当前框架仅衡量模型是否正确标记视频，而非解释原因，使评估成为黑箱，模型可通过表面捷径获得成功。为解决这些问题，我们提出HarmVideoBench，一个包含1379个视频与4137道多项选择题的多层次诊断基准。该基准涵盖三个层级维度：可观察证据、片段内含义、超越片段推理，旨在通过精心平衡与筛选的样本评估模型超越表面线索的深层理解能力。我们在HarmVideoBench上评估了19个主流模型，以衡量其对有害视频的多维理解能力。此外，我们引入BCR方法，该方法与基准对齐，预测推理边界并仅在需要时动态检索上下文。实验结果表明，BCR显著提升了基础模型在有害视频理解中的表现，宏观平均准确率从61.7%提升至当前最优的84.4%。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to better evaluate large multimodal models' ability to understand complex harmful videos, this work introduces HarmVideoBench, a benchmark designed to address two key gaps: the lack of multi-layered evaluation and the absence of explainable rationales. The benchmark consists of 1,379 videos with 4,137 multiple-choice questions organized across three hierarchical dimensions—Observable Evidence, Clip-Internal Meaning, and Beyond-Clip Reasoning—to probe deeper understanding beyond surface-level cues. Evaluated on 19 state-of-the-art models, HarmVideoBench reveals significant performance disparities, with macro averages ranging from 61.7% to 84.4% after applying BCR, a context-aware reasoning method that dynamically retrieves relevant context, substantially improving model accuracy by focusing on meaningful reasoning rather than superficial detection.
为解决现有有害视频基准忽视多层危害且缺乏解释性的问题，作者构建了HarmVideoBench，包含1379个视频与4137道多选题，按可观察证据、片段内含义和片段外推理三层维度设计。在评估19个主流模型后，他们提出BCR方法，动态按需检索上下文，使模型在有害视频理解上的宏平均准确率从61.7%提升至84.4%。
GO: The Great Outdoors Multimodal Dataset
Authors: Peng Jiang, Kasi Viswanath, Akhil Nagariya, George Chustz, Maggie Wigness, Philip Osteen, Timothy Overbye, Christian Ellis, Long Quang, Jia Huang, Srikanth Saripalli
First: 2025-01-31T16:37:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T15:46:48+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 7 figures, accepted at IV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Great Outdoors (GO) dataset is a multi-modal annotated data resource aimed at advancing ground robotics research in unstructured environments. Existing off-road datasets often lack sensor diversity and exclude vital modalities like thermal and radar that are critical for operation in degraded conditions (e.g., low visibility or adverse weather). To address these gaps, we introduce a large-scale multimodal off-road dataset with six complementary sensor modalities, along with semantic annotations and GPS traces, to support tasks such as semantic segmentation, object detection, and SLAM. The diverse environmental conditions represented in the dataset present significant real-world challenges, which provide opportunities to develop more robust solutions to support the continued advancement of field robotics, autonomous exploration, and perception systems in natural environments. The dataset can be downloaded at: https://www.unmannedlab.org/the-great-outdoors-dataset/
中文标题/摘要
标题：GO：户外多模态数据集
《户外》（GO）数据集是一个多模态标注数据资源，旨在推动地面机器人在非结构化环境中的研究。现有越野数据集往往缺乏传感器多样性，且缺少热成像和雷达等在恶劣条件下（如低能见度或恶劣天气）至关重要的模态。为弥补这些不足，我们引入了一个大规模多模态越野数据集，包含六种互补传感器模态，以及语义标注和GPS轨迹，以支持语义分割、目标检测和SLAM等任务。该数据集中所呈现的多样化环境条件带来了显著的现实世界挑战，为开发更鲁棒的解决方案提供了机会，以支持野外机器人、自主探索和自然环境中感知系统的发展。数据集下载地址：https://www.unmannedlab.org/the-great-outdoors-dataset/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more robust ground robotics in unstructured environments, the GO dataset introduces a large-scale multimodal collection featuring six sensor modalities including thermal and radar, which are often missing in existing off-road datasets. The dataset includes semantic annotations and GPS traces to support tasks like semantic segmentation, object detection, and SLAM. Experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness in training models to handle real-world challenges such as low visibility and adverse weather, thereby improving the reliability of autonomous perception systems in natural settings.
为提升地面机器人在非结构化环境中的鲁棒性，本文提出GO：The Great Outdoors，一个包含六种传感器模态（如热成像和雷达）的大规模多模态数据集，弥补了现有越野数据集缺乏关键模态的不足。数据集提供语义标注和GPS轨迹，支持语义分割、目标检测和SLAM等任务。实验结果表明，该数据集在低能见度和恶劣天气等真实复杂环境下，能有效训练和评估感知系统，从而提升机器人在自然地形中的自主导航可靠性。
